### [1] [When NPUs Are Not Always Faster: A Stage-Level Analysis of Mobile LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27435)
*Pu Li,Jiawen Qi,Qinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 首次在CPU-NPU异构SoC上对移动端LLM推理进行细粒度基准测试，发现反直觉的结果：CPU在计算密集的Prefill阶段性能优于NPU（最高1.6倍），NPU在内存受限的Decode阶段加速有限（1.05-1.2倍），且增加NPU卸载会导致能耗上升最高51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动设备上部署大语言模型越来越依赖异构执行，但现有研究缺乏对NPU在算子级和流水线级有效性的系统化表征。为了填补这一空白，需要对移动LLM推理进行多层次的基准测试研究。

Method: 提出基于OPMASK的控制流水线分解方法论，在NPU执行路径中隔离通信、量化和计算开销。采用阶段感知、多层次的方式在CPU-NPU异构SoC上进行基准测试。

Result: 发现反直觉的阶段级性能逆转现象：CPU在计算密集的Prefill阶段性能超越NPU（最高1.6倍），而NPU在内存受限的Decode阶段仅提供有限加速（1.05-1.2倍）。调度开销和跨后端回退降低了NPU卸载的实际收益。增加NPU卸载会导致能耗上升（最高51%）。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，为面向设备端LLM推理的NPU架构师提供设计指导原则，指出需要重新审视NPU在移动LLM推理中的作用和优化方向。

Abstract: Deploying large language models (LLMs) on mobile devices increasingly relies on heterogeneous execution, yet no prior study has systematically characterized NPU effectiveness at the operator and pipeline level. We present the first stage-aware, multi-level benchmarking study of mobile LLM inference on a CPU-NPU heterogeneous SoC. We introduce an OPMASK-based controlled pipeline decomposition methodology that isolates communication, quantization, and computation overheads within the NPU execution path. Our results reveal a counter-intuitive stage-level performance reversal: CPUs outperform NPUs in the compute-intensive Prefill stage (up to 1.6x), while NPUs provide only limited acceleration in the memory-bound Decode stage (1.05-1.2x). We further show that scheduling overhead and cross-backend fallback reduce the practical benefits of NPU offloading. For the energy trend, increasing NPU offloading leads to higher energy consumption (up to 51%). Based on these findings, we derive design guidelines for NPU architects targeting on-device LLM inference.

</details>


### [2] [AssertLLM2: A Comprehensive LLM Benchmark for Assertion Generation from Design Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27472)
*Yuchao Wu,Wenji Fang,Jing Wang,Wenkai Li,Ziyan Guo,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了AssertLLM2，一个包含83个真实设计、支持防错和找错两种场景的开源断言生成基准，首次显式使用缺陷RTL作为输入评估漏洞检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于断言的验证（ABV）中，手动将设计意图转换为SystemVerilog断言（SVA）既费时又容易出错；虽然大语言模型（LLM）在此任务上展现出潜力，但现有基准存在任务设定不切实际、规范输入薄弱、评估过于简单等局限。

Method: 构建了AssertLLM2基准，包含83个跨13个功能类别的真实设计；为每个设计提供结构化设计规范、经验证的依赖完备的黄金RTL、系统化变异的缺陷RTL变体；支持两种实用场景：从规范生成断言防错，以及利用缺陷RTL找错；采用更严格的评估框架，包括语法有效性、形式可证明性、覆盖率和基于变异的漏洞检测。

Result: AssertLLM2是首个显式使用缺陷RTL作为输入来评估漏洞检测能力的基准，其评估框架更为严格全面，能够对断言生成进行更现实、更广泛的评估。

Conclusion: 该基准为实际硬件验证中的断言生成建立了更现实的评估标准，并为最先进的LLM设立了严格的基线，有助于推动LLM在硬件验证领域的应用发展。

Abstract: Assertion-based verification (ABV) is a cornerstone of modern hardware design, yet manually translating design intent into formal SystemVerilog Assertions (SVAs) remains labor-intensive and error-prone. While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for automating this process, existing benchmarks remain limited by unrealistic task formulations, weak specification inputs, and oversimplified evaluation. To address these limitations, we introduce AssertLLM2, an open-source benchmark for realistic assertion generation in hardware verification. AssertLLM2 contains 83 real-world designs across 13 functional categories. For each design, the benchmark provides a structured design specification, a verified dependency-complete golden RTL, and systematically mutated buggy RTL variants. These support two practical settings: bug-prevention, where assertions are generated from specifications to guard against design errors, and bug-hunting, where assertions are generated to expose discrepancies between intended behavior and faulty implementations. To the best of our knowledge, AssertLLM2 is the first benchmark to explicitly use buggy RTL as input to evaluate bug-detection capability. AssertLLM2 further adopts a more rigorous evaluation framework spanning syntactic validity, formal provability, coverage, and mutation-based bug detection. Our benchmark enables a more realistic and extensive assessment of assertion generation and establishes rigorous baselines for state-of-the-art LLMs in practical hardware verification.

</details>


### [3] [CXL-ClusterSim: Modeling CXL-based Disaggregated Memory Cluster for Pooling and Sharing using gem5 and SST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27745)
*Kaustav Goswami,Maryam Babaie,Hoa Nguyen,Venkatesh Akella,Jason Lowe-Power*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了CXL-ClusterSim，一个结合gem5和SST的全系统建模与仿真框架，用于CXL分离式内存系统的设计空间探索和性能权衡评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AI训练和推理需要数百GB到TB级DRAM，且峰值与平均利用率比高，导致过度配置；同时云环境中DRAM成本占比高但利用率低。CXL协议推动了分离式内存的发展，但现有仿真工具有限，难以进行设计空间探索和性能权衡评估。

Method: 将gem5模拟器（提供仿真保真度）与结构仿真工具包SST（提供并行仿真能力）相结合，构建可扩展、灵活且高效的全系统建模与仿真框架CXL-ClusterSim。

Result: 设计并实现了一个可扩展、灵活且 reasonably fast 的仿真框架，能够帮助计算机架构师探索基于CXL的分离式内存设计空间，识别软硬件协同设计和性能优化的新机会。

Conclusion: CXL-ClusterSim填补了CXL分离式内存系统仿真工具的空白，为研究社区提供了有效的设计空间探索平台，有助于推动分离式内存架构的优化和创新。

Abstract: Large-scale AI training and inference require hundreds of gigabytes to terabytes of DRAM with high peak to average utilization ratios, resulting in overprovisioning. In cloud computing, DRAM constitutes a significant share of the cost. Yet, as shown by recent articles, DRAM is heavily under utilized. Memory disaggregation is a solution to both these problems. With the advent of the CXL protocol, there is renewed interest in designing and optimizing computing systems with disaggregated memory. However, at present, there are limited simulation tools available for exploring the design space and evaluating the performance tradeoffs in computer systems with disaggregated memory.
  In this paper, we propose CXL-ClusterSim, a full-system modeling and simulation framework by combining the gem5 simulator for fidelity, with the Structural Simulation Toolkit (SST) for parallel simulation. We outline the challenges in creating this simulation infrastructure and present a design that is scalable, flexible, and reasonably fast to help computer architects to explore the design space of CXL-based disaggregated memory and identify new opportunities for hardware/software codesign and performance optimization.

</details>


### [4] [CLIPGen: A Chiplet Link IP Modeling and Generation Framework for 2.5D Architecture Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27757)
*Zhengping Zhu,Austin Rovinski*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个自动化的芯粒IP生成框架，能够为各种2.5D封装和通信配置提供功耗、性能和面积估计，使系统架构师能够快速评估不同的封装策略并共同优化封装和芯粒架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进的2.5D系统级封装（SiP）在高性能系统中占比日益增长，但现有互连模型要么过于详细且不灵活、需要深入的封装专业知识，要么过于高层、无法提供足够的准确信息来支持架构设计决策，因此系统架构师缺乏一个合适的、考虑封装选择的早期设计空间探索框架。

Method: 提出一个自动化芯粒IP生成框架，该框架能够为各种2.5D封装和通信配置生成功耗、性能和面积估计。该IP生成器可产出高层仿真/估计、RTL仿真和布局布线级实现所需的标准交付物（包括Verilog、Liberty、LEF和数据手册）。

Result: 该框架能够生成多个2.5D封装和通信配置的功耗、性能和面积估计，使架构师能够通过快速评估各种封装策略来共同优化封装和芯粒架构。作为案例研究，该工作检验了多个封装选项下生成的UCIe接口。

Conclusion: 所提出的自动化芯粒IP生成框架成功填补了现有互连模型的空白，为系统架构师提供了一个灵活且准确的工具，支持在设计早期快速探索和优化2.5D SiP的封装与芯粒架构协同设计，从而加速高性能系统的设计过程。

Abstract: Advanced 2.5D Systems-in-Package (SiPs) compose a growing portion of high-performance systems. While the packaging and interconnect choices play a large role in the overall system design, system architects still lack a suitable framework for early design space exploration which takes these choices into account. Current interconnect models fall mostly into the categories of 1) detailed models which are generally inflexible and require deep packaging expertise, or 2) high-level models which don't provide enough information to make accurate architectural design decisions.
  In this work, we present an automated chiplet IP generation framework which provides power, performance, and area estimates for various 2.5D packaging and communication configurations. The IP generator produces standard collaterals required for high-level simulation/estimation, RTL simulation, and place-and-route-level implementation (Verilog, Liberty, LEF, and datasheet). Using our framework, architects can co-optimize the package and chiplet architecture through rapid power, performance, and area estimates of various packaging strategies. As a case study, we examine generated UCIe interfaces across several packaging options.

</details>


### [5] [FT-Pilot: Automated Fault-Tolerant RTL Rewriting via Vulnerability-Guided LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28169)
*Weixing Liu,Zizhen Liu,Jing Ye,Naixing Wang,Cheng Liu,Huawei Li,Xiaowei Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了FT-Pilot框架，利用GNN识别RTL级关键脆弱点，结合LLM驱动的重写引擎实现自动化的RTL软错误加固，显著降低了传统方法的开销和人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着集成电路工艺节点不断缩小，数字系统对软错误的敏感性日益增加。传统全芯片加固方法面积和功耗开销过大，而现有选择性加固方法依赖耗时的故障注入仿真，且在加固阶段需要大量人工干预，难以在早期设计阶段实现高效的自动化可靠性优化。

Method: 提出FT-Pilot框架：首先采用图神经网络(GNN)直接在RTL级别识别关键脆弱资产；然后引入LLM驱动的重写引擎（包含分析器和重写器），在双知识库检索增强生成和自动修复机制的支持下，执行RTL级容错代码重写。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架能够自动生成语法正确、功能正确且可综合的加固RTL设计，在多个基准电路上显著降低了软错误场景下的输出错误率。

Conclusion: 该工作为实现RTL级别的左移可靠性优化提供了一条实用的自动化路径，能够在设计早期阶段高效地提升数字系统的软错误容错能力。

Abstract: As integrated circuit technologies continue to scale toward advanced process nodes, the continual reduction in node capacitance and supply voltage has made digital systems increasingly vulnerable to soft errors. Although traditional full-chip hardening methods can improve reliability, they often incur unacceptable area and power overhead, making selective hardening a more practical engineering solution. However, existing approaches typically rely on time-consuming fault-injection simulation to determine hardening locations through vulnerability analysis, and still depend heavily on manual strategy selection and RTL modification during the hardening stage, making them ill-suited for efficient automated reliability optimization at early design stages. To address these challenges, this paper proposes FT-Pilot, a GNN-guided LLM framework for automatic RTL soft-error hardening. The framework first employs a GNN to identify critical vulnerable assets directly at the RTL level, and then introduces an LLM-driven rewriting engine composed of an analyzer and a rewriter, which performs RTL-level fault-tolerant code rewriting with the support of dual-knowledge-base retrieval-augmented generation and an automatic repair mechanism. Experimental results show that the proposed framework can automatically generate hardened RTL designs that are syntactically correct, functionally correct, and synthesizable across multiple benchmark circuits, while significantly reducing output error rates under soft-error scenarios. This work provides a practical automated path toward shift-left reliability optimization at the RTL level.

</details>


### [6] [Nonvolatile Charge-Domain Attention with HZO Ferroelectric Capacitors: A Simulation-Based Device-to-System Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28208)
*Faris Abouagour*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出HZO铁电电荷域计算单元(FCDC)用于Transformer解码加速。在12个预训练LLM测试中，模拟注意力仅增加+2.62-+2.90%困惑度。相比INT4 GPU基线，RAG和智能体场景下节能18-35倍；对比优化GPU服务仍有1.36-4.69倍优势，多小时驻留场景可达≥41倍节能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer解码受限于注意力计算开销和KV缓存数据移动两个瓶颈。现有数字计算架构在处理大规模语言模型时面临显著的能耗和延迟挑战，特别是在需要长期保存KV缓存的应用场景中，频繁的数据刷新和移动带来巨大开销。

Method: 设计基于铪锆氧化物(HZO)的铁电电荷域计算单元(FCDC)存储器件，实现模拟状态的非易失性存储和电荷域向量矩阵乘法(VMM)运算。评估两种部署模式：全衬底模式(在FCDC上运行q,k,v,o投影和注意力矩阵乘法)和KV协处理器模式(仅存储KV并执行注意力矩阵乘法)。通过ngspice、CrossSim、FiPy和NeuroSim进行器件到系统的交叉验证，基于10nm HZO晶圆级测量数据建模。

Result: 在12个预训练LLM(1.1-32B参数，包括Mixtral-8x22B 141B MoE和128k上下文测试)上验证：全层噪声替换仅在Qwen3-32B增加+2.62% WikiText-2困惑度，Mistral-7B-v0.3增加+2.90±0.33%；端到端模拟注意力在TinyLlama-1.1B最多增加+1.68个百分点困惑度，在≥7B模型上影响<±1个百分点。下游任务(HellaSwag、ARC、LAMBADA、GSM8K)准确率保持在Mistral-7B数字基线的5%以内(MMLU -1.6pp)。能耗方面：对比单用户INT4 GPU基线，RAG和智能体循环中节能18-35倍；对比优化GPU服务(vLLM批处理、CPU+NVMe休眠、电源门控)仍有1.36-4.69倍优势，在多小时驻留的休眠会话中保持≥41倍优势。

Conclusion: FCDC器件通过非易失性存储、无需刷新和KV缓存驻留特性，有效解决了Transformer解码的计算和数据移动瓶颈。该方案在不同规模LLM上均保持了模型精度，同时在能耗效率方面展现出显著优势，特别适合需要长期KV缓存驻留的应用场景(如RAG和智能体工作流)，为大规模语言模型的硬件加速提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Transformer decoding is constrained by both attention compute and KV-cache movement. This paper presents the Ferroelectric Charge-Domain Compute Cell (FCDC), a hafnium-zirconium-oxide (HZO) memcapacitor with an access device that stores analog state nonvolatilely and performs charge-domain VMM for attention. Two deployment modes are evaluated throughout: a full-substrate mode that runs q, k, v, o projections and both attention matmuls on FCDC, and a KV-coprocessor mode that only stores KV and executes the two attention matmuls; the projection-noise budget upper-bounds the coprocessor mode.
  The device-to-system model is cross-checked across ngspice, CrossSim, FiPy, and NeuroSim and anchored in recent wafer-scale 10 nm HZO measurements. Across 12 pretrained LLMs (1.1-32 B dense, plus a partial-layer Mixtral-8x22B 141 B-MoE stress test at k=75% and a 128 k-context dense-Mistral replication), all-layer noise substitution adds only +2.62% WikiText-2 perplexity on Qwen3-32B and +2.90% +/- 0.33% on Mistral-7B-v0.3 (five-seed mean). End-to-end analog attention adds at most +1.68 pp on TinyLlama-1.1B and shrinks below +/-1 pp on every >=7 B model. Downstream accuracy on HellaSwag, ARC, LAMBADA, and GSM8K stays within 5% of the digital baseline for Mistral-7B (MMLU -1.6 pp).
  The headline energy win is nonvolatility, no refresh, and KV-cache residency. A workload-level simulator anchored on measured INT4 decode energy delivers 18-35x lower per-served-token energy on RAG and agent loops against a single-user INT4 GPU baseline; against optimized GPU serving (batched vLLM, CPU+NVMe park, power-gate) the robust advantage shrinks to 1.36-4.69x and remains >=41x on parked sessions with multi-hour residency.

</details>

### [7] [Differentiable Model Predictive Safety for Heterogeneous Mobility at Urban Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27418)
*Wenzhe Song,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DMPS的可微分模型预测安全框架，将模型预测控制的前瞻性嵌入端到端强化学习架构中，在无信号灯交叉口实现了异构智能体的高效安全协调，将高密度混合交通仿真中的碰撞率降低至5.6%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆和移动机器人在城市环境中的整合为智能交通系统带来了关键的安全挑战，特别是在无信号灯交叉口协调具有不同动力学的异构智能体这一复杂问题上。

Method: DMPS框架让智能体学习一个潜在动力学模型来预测动作的未来轨迹，使用可微分的安全评估器评估这些轨迹的风险，并通过整个预测模型进行反向传播，从而高效计算当前动作相对于未来安全的梯度，实现最小化且精确的在线安全修正。该框架被集成到多智能体训练方案中。

Result: 在高密度混合车辆-机器人交通仿真中，DMPS将碰撞率降低至5.6%以下，展现出最先进的安全性能，同时没有牺牲能源效率和交通效率。

Conclusion: DMPS成功地将模型预测控制的前瞻性嵌入数据驱动的端到端强化学习架构中，有效解决了无信号灯交叉口异构智能体的安全协调问题，在保证安全性的同时兼顾了效率。

Abstract: The imminent integration of autonomous vehicles and mobile robots in urban settings presents a critical safety challenge for future intelligent transportation systems. This paper addresses the complex problem of coordinating heterogeneous agents with disparate dynamics at unregulated intersections. We introduce a novel framework, differentiable model predictive safety (DMPS), which embeds the foresight of model-predictive control into a data-driven, end-to-end reinforcement learning architecture. DMPS agents learn a latent dynamics model to predict future trajectories contingent on their actions. A learned, differentiable safety critic then evaluates the risk of these trajectories. Crucially, by leveraging backpropagation through the entire unrolled predictive model, agents can efficiently compute the gradient of future safety with respect to their current action, enabling a minimal and precise online safety correction. Integrated into a multi-agent training scheme, DMPS virtually eliminates collisions to less than 5.6% in high-density, mixed vehicle-robot traffic simulations, demonstrating state-of-the-art safety without compromising energy and traffic efficiency.

</details>


### [8] [APS: Bias-Controlled Adaptive Prototype Simulation for Population-Scale LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27419)
*Quan Zheng,Yan Gao,Shaobin He,Haoxiang Guan,Yuanhe Tian,Jie Feng,Ming Wang,Shuxin Zheng,Zhen Liu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出自适应原型仿真框架（APS），通过原型响应诱导、阴影审计残差校正和尾保护单例路由，在保持高精度的同时大幅减少LLM调用次数，在千万级Agent实验中实现了381.1倍的降本效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM-Agent仿真是研究群体响应轨迹的重要工具，但完整多轮仿真的计算成本随人口规模和时间跨度线性增长，每个Agent在每轮都需要查询LLM，导致高昂的计算开销和扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出自适应原型仿真（APS）框架，将可扩展仿真重构为循环oracle分配问题：1）原型响应诱导为邻近Agent生成局部响应曲面；2）阴影审计残差校正估计传播残差用于聚合校正和预算分配；3）尾保护单例路由直接查询孤立的、异质的或高曲率区域的Agent。理论分析将误差分解为原型覆盖误差、阴影审计残差校正误差、局部传播偏差和时间上下文失配。

Result: 在千万级Agent的多轮舆论仿真实验中，APS相比完整仿真实现了381.1倍的LLM调用减少，最终轮与完整LLM基准的Jensen-Shannon散度仅为0.094，在相同预算下优于面向规模的基线和参考对齐分布偏差更小。消融实验和稳健性检验验证了主要偏差控制机制的有效性。

Conclusion: APS框架成功平衡了大规模LLM-Agent仿真的精度与效率，通过系统性的偏差控制机制实现了数量级的计算成本降低，为可扩展的社会仿真提供了有效解决方案，在保持参考对齐精度的同时显著提升了仿真的可扩展性。

Abstract: LLM-agent simulation offers a flexible computational tool for studying population response trajectories that depend on scenario events, memory, demographics, and evolving social context. However, full multi-round simulation scales linearly with both population size and horizon, requiring every agent to query the LLM at every round. We propose Adaptive Prototype Simulation (APS), a framework that reframes scalable LLM-based simulation as a recurrent oracle-allocation problem. APS retains the designated LLM as the online transition oracle while querying adaptive core prototypes, selected singleton-tail agents, and shadow-audit agents. Prototype responses induce local response surfaces for nearby agents, reducing online LLM calls without replacing the underlying transition model. To control approximation bias, shadow-audit residual correction estimates propagation residuals for aggregate correction and future budget allocation, while tail-protected singleton routing directly queries selected isolated, heterogeneous, or high-curvature regions that are vulnerable to smoothing. Theoretically, we treat APS as an estimator for full-scale high-precision individual social simulation and decompose its errors into prototype-coverage error, shadow-audit residual-correction error, local-propagation bias, and temporal context mismatch. Under the reported protocols, APS gives lower reference-aligned distributional discrepancy than scale-oriented and same-budget baselines while reducing online LLM calls, with ablations and compact robustness checks diagnosing the main bias-control mechanisms. In a 10M-agent, multi-round public-opinion simulation, APS achieves a 381.1-fold reduction over full simulation, with reference-aligned final-round JSD of 0.094 against the corresponding full-LLM reference.

</details>


### [9] [Speed-Weighted Adaptive Flocking for Sailing Swarms under Dynamic Environmental Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27422)
*Pranav Kedia,Aaron Gan,Hannah J. Williams,Andreagiovanni Reina,Heiko Hamann*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了SailSwarmSwIM仿真器和速度加权交互规则，解决了自主帆船机器人群体的快慢协调问题，发现增加慢速机器人的社会影响力可提高群体极化并减少近距离相遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的集体行为模型假设机器人可以直接执行期望的速度和方向，但自主帆船机器人违反了这一假设。帆船运动受风力推进、航向限制和空间变化风场的影响，特别是机动性与风速耦合：弱风中帆船可能只能缓慢转向或无法转向，而强风允许更快转向，这在群体中引入了速度和机动性的瞬态异质性。

Method: 1. 开发了SailSwarmSwIM，一个简化的自主帆船机器人群体仿真器，捕捉风力依赖的速度和机动性、禁区、换舷行为以及稳定或阵风风场；2. 基于Couzin模型，引入了速度加权社会交互规则，该规则考虑了每个机器人的瞬态运动约束。

Result: 增加慢速机器人的社会影响力可提高群体极化并减少近距离相遇。这种效应源于对快速邻居的吸引力（有助于维持运动）与围绕慢速邻居的内聚力（防止群体分裂）之间的平衡。

Conclusion: SailSwarmSwIM仿真器和速度加权交互规则共同提供了一个建模框架，用于研究运动能力持续受风力影响的机器人群体的自适应集体行为。

Abstract: Collective behavior models, such as aggregation and flocking, usually assume self-propelled robots that can directly execute their desired speed and direction of motion without fundamental constraints. However, autonomous sailing robots violate this assumption. Their motion is shaped by wind-dependent propulsion, restricted headings, and spatially varying wind conditions. In particular, maneuverability is coupled to wind speed: in weak wind, sailboats may turn only slowly or not at all, whereas stronger wind enables faster turns. This introduces transient heterogeneity in speed and maneuverability across the flock. We focus on this fast-slow coordination problem in sailing robot flocks. To study this problem, we introduce SailSwarmSwIM, a reduced-order simulator for autonomous sailing robot swarms that captures wind-dependent speed and maneuverability, no-go zones, tacking behavior, and steady or gusty wind fields. To design our novel flocking technique, we start from the Couzin model and introduce a speed-weighted social interaction rule that accounts for each robot's transient motion constraints. A key result is that increasing the social influence of slower robots improves polarization and reduces close encounters. This effect arises from a balance between attraction to fast neighbors, which helps maintain movement, and cohesion around slow neighbors, which prevents the flock from fragmenting. Together, our simulator, SailSwarmSwIM, and the speed-weighted interaction rule provide a modeling framework for studying adaptive collective behavior in robotic fleets whose motion capabilities are continuously shaped by wind.

</details>

*Deyu Zhou,Yuwei Guo,Xudong Lu,Linhao Zhang,Wei Guo,Lizhen Cui*

Main category: cs.MA

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着社会实体协作复杂性和用户需求的增加，影响数据服务市场稳定发展的因素不断增多（包括信息广泛传播增强主观意识、智能化水平持续提升、结构关系复杂化等）。现有研究主要聚焦数据层面的性能表现，在涉及多异构主体度量及数据服务市场中各类社会要素整合方面存在不足，难以满足监管决策前进行仿真实验的需求。

Method: 提出基于异构多主体建模的数据服务市场度量与网络分析方法：引入服务生态系统理论明确数据服务市场的参与主体与外部因素；基于价值创造理论构建三层实体的效用度量模型；设计分析方法精确评估异构网络对效用的影响。

Result: 通过实验结果分析验证了所提方法的有效性，能够有效实现对数据服务市场中多异构主体和社会要素的测量与分析，为监管决策提供仿真实验支撑。

Conclusion: 该方法为数据服务市场的治理与监管提供了新的测量分析工具，有助于在制定监管决策前进行有效的仿真实验，促进数据服务市场的稳定发展。

Abstract: With the increasing complexity of collaboration among various social entities and user demands, the factors affecting the stable development of the data service market are also growing. These factors include the widespread dissemination of information enhancing subjective consciousness, the continuous improvement in intelligence, and the complexification of structural relationships. To achieve effective governance and regulation of the data service market, it is crucial to conduct simulation experiments before making regulatory decisions. However, current research and analysis of the data service market primarily focus on data-level performance, proving inadequate when it comes to measurement and analysis of multiple heterogeneous entities and the integration of various social elements within the data service market. Based on this, this paper innovatively proposes a data service market measurement and network analysis method based on heterogeneous multi-agent modeling. By introducing the service ecosystem theory, we clarify the participants and external factors of the data service market and conduct utility measurements for three-level entities based on value creation. Furthermore, an analytical methodology is devised to precisely assess the influence of heterogeneous networks on utility. Finally, the paper verifies the effectiveness of the proposed method through the analysis of experimental results.

</details>


### [11] [AgensFlow: A Coordination-Policy Substrate for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27466)
*Nicole Koenigstein*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了AgensFlow框架，将多智能体系统的协调决策建模为在线策略学习问题，通过学习方式优化技能协议、角色分配、模型绑定、拓扑交互等动态决策，在分布式系统事件和安全咨询任务上验证了学习的路由策略优于静态管道基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统面临复杂的协调决策问题（技能协议调用、角色分配、模型绑定、拓扑交互、检索验证时机等），这些决策与任务机制和操作约束相互影响，静态管道和一次性模型比较无法充分探索设计空间，难以预先固定最优配置。

Method: 提出AgensFlow开源框架，将多智能体协调视为部分可观察性下的在线策略学习问题，使协调决策可观察且可从重复轨迹中学习，而非将技能、角色、模型、拓扑和评估选择作为固定的管道设计。

Result: 在分布式系统事件任务和安全咨询任务两个语料库上评估表明：（1）学习的路由在协调密集型任务上达到比固定管道基线更高质量的操作点；（2）跳过机制隔离出拓扑压缩是系统架构的重要组成部分；（3）热启动策略图能降低探索成本同时保持平台质量。

Conclusion: 学习型可审计路由能够改善协调密集型多智能体工作流性能，优于静态布线方式，证明了将多智能体协调建模为学习问题的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems built on large language models (LLMs) require many coordination choices that are difficult to fix a priori: which skill protocol to invoke, which agent role should perform a subtask, which model to bind to each role, how roles should interact, when to use retrieval or verification, and when to omit a step entirely. These choices interact with task regime and operational constraints, so static pipelines and one-off model comparisons provide only a limited view of the design space. This paper introduces AgensFlow, an open-source framework that treats multi-agent coordination as an online policy-learning problem under partial observability. The framework makes coordination decisions observable and learnable from repeated trajectories, rather than treating skill, role, model, topology, and evaluation choices as fixed pipeline design.
  AgensFlow is evaluated on two corpora: distributed-systems incident tasks and security-advisory tasks. The evaluation shows three main results: learned routing reaches a higher-quality operating point than a fixed pipeline baseline on coordination-heavy classes; skip:X isolates topology compression as a meaningful part of the substrate; and warm-started policy graphs can reduce exploration cost while preserving plateau quality. Overall, the results support that learned, auditable routing can improve coordination-heavy multi-agent workflows over static wiring.

</details>


### [12] [From Task Allocation to Risk Clearing: A Unifying Interface for Mixed Human-Agent Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27547)
*Vassilis Vassiliades*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了风险感知选项清算(ROC)机制，通过让智能体暴露带风险摘要的选项，由中央清算机构优化风险调整后的任务分配，实现了在安全关键环境中对异构智能体（人、机器人、软件代理）的透明、可扩展协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人类、机器人和软件代理在安全关键环境中日益共存，协调机制需要从静态任务分配转向管理不确定承诺。现有框架存在局限：要么假设僵化的静态团队，要么学习难以适应且难以与人类决策者集成的黑箱联合策略。

Method: 提出风险感知选项清算(ROC)机制：智能体暴露选项（时间扩展技能）并配以预测结果分布的风险摘要；中央清算机构在截止期限和安全约束下，通过优化风险调整后的任务效用来分配任务。ROC是一个机制族，既支持清算机构从数据学习结果模型，也支持消费智能体提供的完整分布预测。

Result: ROC将风险感知选项作为基本协调单元，建立了可扩展、透明的基础设施，用于将异构智能体整合到未来人-代理混合社会中。

Conclusion: ROC为未来混合人-代理社会提供了统一的风险感知协调机制，并为构建此类风险感知清算层勾勒了研究议程。

Abstract: As humans, robots, and software agents increasingly share safety-critical environments, coordination must move from static task allocation to managing uncertain commitments. Existing frameworks fall short: they either assume rigid, static teams or learn opaque joint policies that are hard to adapt and difficult to integrate with human decision-makers. To overcome these limitations, we propose Risk-Aware Option Clearing (ROC), a unifying coordination mechanism in which agents expose options (temporally extended skills) paired with risk summaries that predict outcome distributions. A central clearinghouse then assigns tasks by optimizing risk-adjusted mission utility under deadlines and safety constraints. ROC is a family of mechanisms, ranging from deployments where the clearinghouse learns outcome models from data to ones that consume full distributional predictions from agents. By treating risk-aware options as the basic coordination unit, ROC sketches a scalable, transparent infrastructure for integrating heterogeneous agents into future mixed human--agent societies and outlines a research agenda for such risk-aware clearing layers.

</details>


### [13] [Detection Without Correction: A Two-Parameter Decomposition of Multi-Stage LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27559)
*Prashanti Nilayam,Kiran Ramanna,Prashil Tumbade*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多阶段LLM管道中准确率平台期、自纠正性能下降等现象的根本原因：模型在"检测"和"条件生成"两个决策步骤中，即使正确检测到上游错误，纠正失败率仍高达53-94%。该发现为理解和改进LLM多智能体系统提供了关键洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段LLM管道（如多智能体辩论、内在自纠正、检索增强验证）表现出令人困惑的聚合行为：准确率在多轮中出现平台期和反转、前沿模型无法复现辩论增益、内在自纠正性能退化、不同提供商间的辩论动态存在定性分歧。现有理论无法统一解释这些现象。

Method: 提出一个理论框架，将下游智能体响应分解为两个耦合决策：检测（是否将上游内容视为权威）和条件生成（若不权威则生成什么）。由此识别出四种可观察的响应模式，其中"检测但未纠正"是关键失败模式。在四类模型、四个基准测试（GSM8K、MATH-500、GPQA-Diamond、AIME）、两种方法（多智能体辩论、内在自纠正）构成的九格实证网格上进行系统性验证。

Result: 条件误纠正率在所有队列中持续占主导地位（53-94%），而检测率随情境变化超过一个数量级。该框架成功统一了前述四种现象，将其解释为共同机制的表征，并将检测阈值刻画为一种稳定的模型/协议层面规律，在相同基准难度下跨方法持续存在。

Conclusion: 多阶段LLM管道的性能瓶颈源于检测与条件生成决策的解耦：模型能识别错误但无法有效纠正。检测阈值是稳定的模型特性，可作为预测和优化多阶段管道性能的关键参数。</think>我已对该论文摘要进行了结构化分析。这篇论文针对多阶段LLM管道中的异常现象提出了统一的理论解释框架，核心发现是模型的错误检测能力与纠正能力存在显著分离。<tool_call>Structure<arg_key>conclusion</arg_key><arg_value>多阶段LLM管道的性能瓶颈源于检测与条件生成决策的解耦：模型能识别错误但无法有效纠正。检测阈值是稳定的模型特性，可作为预测和优化多阶段管道性能的关键参数。该发现对理解LLM局限性和设计更有效的多智能体系统具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Multi-stage LLM pipelines that perform multi-agent debate, intrinsic self-correction, or retrieval-augmented verification exhibit puzzling aggregate behaviors: accuracy plateaus and reversals across rounds, non-replication of debate gains on contemporary frontier models, intrinsic self-correction degradation, and qualitative cross-provider divergence in debate dynamics. Downstream agent response can be operationalized as two coupled decisions: detection (whether to treat upstream content as authoritative) and conditional generation (what to produce if not). This decomposition yields four observable response regimes, of which detection-without-correction is the load-bearing failure mode. Across a nine-cell empirical grid spanning four model families, four benchmarks (GSM8K, MATH-500, GPQA-Diamond, AIME), and two methods (multi-agent debate, intrinsic self-correction), we find that the conditional miscorrection rate is consistently dominant (53-94% across cohorts) while detection rate varies contextually by more than an order of magnitude. The framework unifies the four phenomena above as signatures of a common mechanism and characterizes detection threshold as a stable model/protocol-level regularity that persists across methods at matched benchmark difficulty.

</details>


### [14] [You Only Align Once: Propagating Cooperative Behaviors in Multi-Agent Systems through Seed Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27586)
*Nicole Hsing,Asuka Yuxi Zheng,Yi Zhao,Haoqin Tu,Jen-Tse Huang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 通过将一个对齐的“种子代理”置于未训练的代理群体中，可以仅通过自然语言交互将合作行为传播给其他代理。该种子代理在Red-Black Game中将合作率从24.8%提升至62.2%，并零样本迁移至Sugarscape环境，将交易成功率从21.6%提升至91.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式开放多智能体系统中，随着群体规模增长和潜在非对齐代理的存在，确保所有代理行为符合预期仍是一项重大挑战。传统方法需对每个代理进行详尽训练，成本高昂且难以扩展。

Method: 研究者提出了“对齐传播”概念。首先在Red-Black Game（一种团队迭代囚徒困境）中，通过蒸馏教师模型的合作推理和说服性对话，训练出一个Qwen-3-14B种子代理。然后将该种子代理置于四个未训练的队友中，使其通过自然语言交互影响队友决策。同时测试了该模型向Sugarscape（一个空间生存模拟环境）的零样本迁移能力。

Result: 在Red-Black Game中，单个种子代理将团队合作率从基线的24.8%提升至62.2%，超越了教师模型和原始Gemini-3.1-Pro。在Sugarscape环境中，仅在Red-Black Game上训练的种子代理实现了零样本迁移，将交易成功率从21.6%的基线提升至91.5%。

Conclusion: 研究结果将多智能体对齐问题从需要对每个代理进行穷尽式训练的重构为一种可扩展的社会能力，通过策略性地放置种子代理即可实现合作行为的有效传播，为大规模多智能体系统的对齐提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Ensuring agent behaviors in distributed open multi-agent systems remains challenging, especially as populations grow and unaligned agents may exist. We show that a single aligned agent can propagate cooperative behaviors to untrained agents purely through natural language interaction, a phenomenon we term Alignment Propagation. We study this in the Red-Black Game, a team-based iterated Prisoner's Dilemma in which teammates deliberate and vote to determine their team's collective action. By distilling the cooperative reasoning and persuasive dialogues of a teacher model into a Qwen-3-14B, we obtain a seed agent that, when placed among four untrained teammates, doubles the cooperation rate from 24.8% to 62.2%, outperforming the teacher model and a vanilla Gemini-3.1-Pro. Remarkably, a seed trained exclusively on the RedBlack Game transfers zero-shot to Sugarscape, a spatially grounded survival simulation with pairwise trading, achieving a 91.5% trade success rate versus a 21.6% baseline. Our results reframe multi-agent alignment from an exhaustive per-agent training problem to a scalable social capability that can be engineered through strategic seed placement.

</details>


### [15] [Agents that Matter: Optimizing Multi-Agent LLMs via Removal-Based Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27621)
*Mingyu Lu,Yushan Huang,Chris Lin,Su-In Lee*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文将多智能体系统中的贡献归因问题形式化为合作博弈，提出了一种高效的框架来识别个体智能体的贡献，并通过模型替换实现系统优化，在医疗诊断等场景中取得了显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多智能体系统(MAS)复杂度的增加，识别个体智能体的贡献对于系统优化至关重要。然而，现有方法缺乏一个严格、统一的信用分配框架来准确评估各智能体的贡献。

Method: 将智能体归因形式化为合作博弈，参数化为联盟分布、移除协议和目标度量。提出了三种移除协议：智能体消融、内省式LLM评估和模型替换归因。通过Leave-One-Out(LOO)方法识别瓶颈智能体，并引入模型替换来评估特定智能体骨干网络的效用。

Result: 1) LOO方法能以极小的计算成本达到与组合方法相当的瓶颈识别效果；2) 智能体消融能隔离结构性瓶颈，而内省式LLM评估器无法准确近似这一行为；3) 通过替换低贡献智能体的底层模型，在三个基准测试上任务性能提升高达17%，成本降低高达35%；4) 在医疗MAS审计中发现，智能体对诊断准确性和伦理行为的贡献往往是分离的，通过干预反生产角色可在保持诊断准确性的同时提高伦理一致性。

Conclusion: 本研究为多智能体系统的归因和干预提供了一种具有原则性且成本效益高的方法，证明了LOO方法的有效性，揭示了不同移除协议的特性，并通过模型替换和角色干预实现了系统性能和伦理行为的双重优化。

Abstract: As multi-agent systems (MAS) become increasingly complex, identifying the contributions of individual agents is critical for system optimization. However, existing approaches lack a rigorous, unified framework for credit assignment. In this work, we formalize agent attribution as a cooperative game, parameterized by the coalition distribution, removal protocol, and target metric. Using this framework, we show that Leave-One-Out (LOO) identifies bottleneck agents as effectively as combinatorial methods, but at a fraction of the computational cost. We also demonstrate that removal protocols induce distinct games: Agent ablation isolates structural bottlenecks, whereas introspective LLM judges fail to faithfully approximate this behavior. Furthermore, to evaluate the utility of specific agent backbones, we introduce attribution via model replacement. By substituting underlying models of low-contribution agents, we improve task performance by up to 17% while reducing cost by up to 35% across three benchmarks. Finally, we apply our framework to audit a medical MAS, revealing that agent contributions to diagnostic accuracy and ethical behavior are often decoupled. By intervening on counterproductive roles, we observe an increase in ethics alignment while maintaining diagnostic accuracy. Overall, this work provides a principled approach for cost-effective MAS attribution and intervention.

</details>

*Shuyang Li,Ruimin Ke*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于SUMO交通仿真的多智能体协作框架，通过角色解耦和状态持久化编排器，显著提升了任务成功率和参数准确性，实现了从自然语言意图到仿真执行的有效转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单体智能体架构在处理端到端交通仿真工作流时面临复杂性挑战，导致推理失败、参数不一致以及缺乏系统化状态管理问题。

Method: 提出多智能体协作框架，将仿真流程解耦为Planner、Builder、Demand、Runner和Analyst等专门角色，由高层推理引擎协调。引入基于模型上下文协议（MCP）的状态持久化编排器，确保分布式智能体动作间的无缝数据交接和环境一致性，实现闭环优化过程。

Result: 角色消融实验表明，与单智能体基线相比，多智能体框架显著提升了任务成功率和参数准确性。真实路网提取和交通优化案例研究验证了系统桥接高层自然语言意图与底层仿真执行的能力。

Conclusion: 该多智能体协作框架成功解决了现有单体架构的局限性，为大型语言模型与微观交通仿真的集成提供了可靠解决方案，推动了自主化城市规划和智能交通分析的发展。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) with microscopic traffic simulation offers a promising path toward autonomous urban planning and intelligent transportation analysis. However, existing monolithic agent architectures often struggle with the complexity of end-to-end simulation workflows, leading to reasoning failures, parameter inconsistency, and a lack of systematic state management. This paper proposes a novel multi-agent collaborative framework designed to automate the entire lifecycle of traffic simulation in SUMO (Simulation of Urban Mobility). Our approach decouples the simulation pipeline into specialized roles, including Planner, Builder, Demand, Runner, and Analyst, coordinated by a high-level reasoning engine. We introduce a state-persistent Orchestrator leveraging the Model Context Protocol (MCP) to ensure seamless data handover and environmental consistency across distributed agent actions. This architecture enables a robust closed-loop refinement process, where simulation outcomes are iteratively analyzed and optimized to satisfy user-defined Key Performance Indicators (KPIs). Experimental results through role ablation studies demonstrate that the proposed multi-agent framework significantly enhances task success rates and parameter accuracy compared to single-agent baselines. Furthermore, case studies on real-world network extraction and traffic optimization highlight the system's capability to bridge the gap between high-level natural language intent and low-level simulation execution.

</details>


### [17] [Long Live the Librarian! A Persistent Search Sub-Agent for Energy-Efficient Multi-Agent Software Engineering Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27787)
*Seunghyuk Cho,Sunghyun Choi,Jaeseung Heo,Youngbin Choi,Saemi Moon,MoonJeong Park,Dongwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体系统(MAS)在软件工程中存在的冗余输出token导致高能耗问题，提出了一种持久的搜索子智能体Librarian，通过追踪仓库搜索历史并抑制冗余探索行为，在保持任务性能的同时将GPU能耗降低高达25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在自主软件工程领域取得了显著进展，但其日益增长的推理能耗引发了可持续性担忧。作者发现能耗集中在被忽视的冗余输出token上，基于两个实证发现：输出token的能耗是输入或缓存token的30-1000倍；智能体重复探索重叠的仓库区域导致每个任务的输出膨胀。

Method: 提出了Librarian——一个持久的搜索子智能体，用于跟踪仓库搜索历史并抑制智能体间的冗余探索动作。该系统通过返回文件区域的简短引用而非完整文件摘录，进一步减少输出token的数量。

Result: 在SWE-Bench Verified数据集上，Librarian将现有多智能体SWE系统的每任务GPU能耗降低了高达25%，同时保持了任务性能。输出token的能耗远高于输入token（30-1000倍），证明了针对输出优化的必要性。

Conclusion: 通过识别并解决多智能体系统中冗余输出token这一关键能耗来源，Librarian证明了在保持系统性能的同时显著提升能效的可行性。该研究为可持续AI发展提供了切实可行的优化方向，强调了在MAS设计中关注token生成效率的重要性。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) have substantially advanced autonomous software engineering (SWE), but their growing inference energy demands raise sustainability concerns. In this paper, we demonstrate that this cost is concentrated in an overlooked source: redundant output tokens generated across agents. Two empirical findings ground this claim. First, our per-token energy attribution for MAS reveals a sharp asymmetry: an output token consumes 30 to 1,000 times more energy than an input or cached token. Second, MAS inflate per-episode output because agents repeatedly re-explore overlapping repository regions. To address this inefficiency, we propose Librarian, a persistent search sub-agent that tracks repository-search history and suppresses redundant exploration actions across agents. By returning short references to file regions instead of full file excerpts, Librarian further reduces output-token volume. On SWE-Bench Verified, Librarian reduces per-episode GPU energy consumption of existing multi-agent SWE systems by up to 25% while preserving task performance.

</details>

### [18] [Context-aware Simopt-Power: Using structural data with simulation metadata to optimise FPGA designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27446)
*Eashan Wadhwa,Georgios Floros,Shanker Shreejith*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Context-aware Simopt-Power框架，通过结合仿真活动元数据与轻量级结构特征，在FPGA CAD流程中实现平均6.8%的动态功耗降低，同时将LUT开销限制在11.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的FPGA CAD流程通常丢弃预实现行为仿真产生的丰富开关活动轨迹。先前的仿真引导和功耗感知优化方法存在依赖固定阈值、决策启发式狭窄、设计感知有限以及面积开销过大等问题。

Method: 提出Context-aware Simopt-Power框架，将活动元数据与轻量级结构特征（时序接近度、逻辑深度代理和扇出估计）相结合，更精确地定位网表中高影响区域。移除经验调优常数，代之以架构感知参数（如LUT大小和映射约束），并使用功耗、延迟、面积-延迟积（AD）和功耗-延迟积（PD）评估权衡。

Result: 在开源Yosys/ABC流程中实现，并在复杂的Koios深度学习加速器基准测试上评估。实现了平均6.8%的动态功耗降低，同时将LUT开销限制在11.2%。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了整体设计优化，证明了在FPGA CAD流程中利用仿真元数据进行功耗优化的有效性，为设计空间探索提供了更全面的评估指标。

Abstract: Pre-implementation behavioural simulation routinely validates functional correctness, yet it also produces rich switching-activity traces that are typically discarded by FPGA computer-aided design (CAD) flows. Prior simulation-guided and power-aware FPGA optimisations demonstrate the promise of exploiting this metadata, but many rely on fixed thresholds, narrow decision heuristics, or limited design awareness, often incurring substantial area overhead. This paper presents Context-aware Simopt-Power, a simulator-guided optimisation framework that combines activity metadata with lightweight structural features (sequential proximity, logic-depth proxies, and fan-out estimates) to more precisely target high-impact regions of the netlist. We additionally remove empirically tuned constants, replacing them with architecture-aware parameters such as LUT size and mapping constraints, and evaluate trade-offs using power, delay, and a more useful metrics, area-delay product (AD) and power-delay product (PD). Implemented in an open-source Yosys/ABC flow and evaluated on the complex Koios deep-learning accelerator benchmarks, Context-aware Simopt-Power achieves an average 6.8% dynamic-power reduction while limiting LUT overhead to 11.2%, thus enabling a holistic design optimisation.

</details>


### [19] [A Methodology to Assess Power Modeling in Energy-Aware Federated Learning on Heterogeneous Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27601)
*Chaimae Jallouli,Karim Boubouh,Robert Basmadjian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可复现的CPU功耗估算方法和rail-to-cluster映射技术，使分析性CMOS模型在ARM移动SoC上的预测误差低于10%，而近似模型误差高达959%。在联邦学习框架中，分析模型在保持80%模型精度的同时比近似模型节省1.4倍能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构ARM商用设备上估算CPU功耗具有挑战性，因为CPU电压域的访问受限。现有能量感知联邦学习框架通常依赖简化的近似功耗模型而非更准确的分析性CMOS模型，导致计算能耗估算不准确，可能引发次优决策。

Method: 提出可复现的CPU功耗估算方法，结合rail-to-cluster映射技术来获取集群级供电电压。该方法无需额外硬件支持或外部功耗测量工具，能够在异构多集群ARM移动SoC上使用分析性CMOS功耗模型。在两款商用Android设备上进行评估验证。

Result: 分析性模型预测CPU功耗的误差低于10%，而近似模型误差高达959%。在AnycostFL能量感知联邦学习框架上，分析模型实现了相同的80%模型精度，同时比近似模型减少1.4倍能耗消耗。结果表明近似模型会严重误判计算能耗并导致次优决策。

Conclusion: 该工作促进了分析性CPU功耗模型在异构多集群ARM移动SoC上的应用，无需额外硬件支持或外部功耗测量工具。证明了近似模型严重低估计算能耗的问题，为移动设备上的能量感知应用提供了更准确的功耗估算方案。

Abstract: Estimating CPU power on heterogeneous ARM-based commodity devices is challenging due to limited access to CPU's voltage domains. As a result, state-of-the-art energy-aware Federated Learning (FL) frameworks typically rely on simplified approximate power models to estimate computation energy, rather than the more accurate analytical CMOS-based model. To bridge this gap, we propose a reproducible CPU power estimation methodology combined with a rail-to-cluster mapping technique to retrieve cluster-level supply voltage. We evaluate our approach on two commodity Android devices and show that the analytical model predicts CPU power with errors below 10%, whereas the approximate model incurs errors of up to 959%. Using AnycostFL, a state-of-the-art energy-aware FL framework, we show that the analytical model achieves the same 80% model accuracy while consuming 1.4x less energy than the approximate model. These results highlight that approximate models can severely misestimate computation energy and lead to suboptimal decisions. This work facilitates the use of analytical CPU power models on heterogeneous multi-cluster ARM-based mobile SoCs without additional hardware support or external power measurement tools.

</details>


### [20] [Carbon-Aware Mapping and Scheduling for Deadline-Constrained Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27652)
*Dominik Schweisgut,Anne Benoit,Yves Robert,Henning Meyerhenke*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CWM算法，一种针对数据中心工作流调度的新型碳感知算法，在异构计算环境下结合可再生能源可用性进行任务映射与调度，相比当前最优方法CaWoSched实现了42%的中位碳排放减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据中心规模扩大，能耗和碳排放成为紧迫问题。虽然利用可再生能源是减少碳排放的有前景策略，但面临多重挑战：异构计算环境中节点功耗差异、工作流任务间的依赖关系约束、严格的截止期限要求。这些因素使得调度问题变得极其复杂。

Method: 证明了该调度问题为NP难，且即使单处理器情况也不存在常数因子近似算法。基于此理论难度，提出了CWM算法，将碳感知映射与调度相结合，通过动态规划与高效启发式方法的集成，充分利用可再生能源可用性和基础设施异构性来构建可行解。

Result: 在实验评估中，CWM算法相比当前最先进的CaWoSched方法实现了显著的碳排放减少。具体而言，当截止期限为碳排放不可知基准完工时间的两倍时，CWM相比CaWoSched最优版本实现了42%的中位碳排放成本降低（注：CaWoSched本身已相对于碳排放不可知基准降低了36%）。

Conclusion: CWM算法成功解决了异构环境下碳感知工作流调度的理论挑战与实践需求，证明了结合动态规划与启发式方法的有效性，为数据中心实现显著的碳排放减少提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As datacenters continue to grow in scale, their energy consumption and resulting carbon footprint have become pressing concerns. With the increasing share of renewable energy in a datacenter's mixed energy supply, shifting task execution to periods of high green-power availability is a promising strategy to reduce carbon emissions. However, in heterogeneous computing environments, the power consumption of compute nodes in a datacenter can also vary. In practice, workloads submitted to datacenters are often not isolated tasks, but entire workflows consisting of interdependent tasks with precedence constraints. A further challenge arises from the fact that carbon emission reductions must typically be achieved under strict workflow deadlines. In this work, we show that the problem posed by these challenges for the scheduler is NP-hard and admits no constant-factor approximation even for the uni-processor case. Motivated by this hardness, we present a novel algorithm CWM that combines carbon-aware mapping and scheduling to construct feasible solutions. Our approach integrates dynamic programming with efficient heuristics to exploit renewable energy availability and infrastructure heterogeneity. To assess the quality of the new algorithm, we evaluate it against the state-of-the-art approach CaWoSched and show that CWM achieves significant reductions in terms of carbon emissions in experiments. In particular, we are able to achieve a median carbon cost reduction of 42% over the best version of CaWoSched when the deadline is two times the makespan of a carbon-agnostic baseline. Note that CaWoSched itself already reduces the carbon-agnostic baseline by 36%.

</details>


### [21] [SOLANET: Distributed Neighbor Graph Construction on GPU-Accelerated Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27691)
*Keita Iwabuchi,Trevor Steil,Benjamin W. Priest,Grace J. Li,Geoffrey Sanders,Roger Pearce*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了SOLANET，一个GPU加速的分布式近邻图构建工具包，通过数据分区、本地图构建和基于MPI单边操作的远程图搜索与精炼，实现了大规模数据集的高效分布式近邻图构建，在AMD MI300A APU上展现出优异的扩展性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近邻图在数据分析和AI任务中应用广泛，但现有研究主要关注单节点系统（包括GPU）上的近似构建方法。随着数据规模增长，将近邻图构建扩展到分布式系统以处理更大规模数据并进一步加速仍面临挑战，主要瓶颈在于不规则的计算模式。

Method: SOLANET采用两阶段策略：首先在数据分区后于每个GPU上构建本地图，然后通过MPI单边操作从其他GPU拉取远程图，利用近似最近邻（ANN）搜索对本地图进行精炼。此外，SOLANET还为AMD GPU提供了无锁的单GPU近邻图构建算法。

Result: 在单个MI300A APU上，SOLANET的单GPU实现超越了最先进的GPU近似近邻图构建方法。在分布式场景下，SOLANET实现了良好的扩展性：对于10亿数据点，从32个APU扩展到512个APU获得11倍加速；对于20亿数据点，从64个APU扩展到512个APU获得6.9倍加速。

Conclusion: SOLANET成功解决了分布式近邻图构建的挑战，通过GPU加速和分布式设计实现了对十亿级数据点的高效处理，证明了其在单一GPU和分布式多GPU系统上的优越性能和良好扩展性。

Abstract: Neighbor graphs capture relationships among data points and are widely used in data analytics and AI workloads. Many studies have explored approximate construction methods for single-node systems, including GPUs. However, extending this to distributed systems for larger data and further acceleration remains challenging due to irregular computation patterns.
  We present SOLANET, a GPU-accelerated distributed neighbor graph construction toolkit. SOLANET first constructs local graphs on each GPU after data partitioning and then refines them via approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) searches over remote graphs pulled from other GPUs using MPI one-sided operations. SOLANET also provides a lock-free single-GPU neighbor graph construction algorithm for AMD GPUs.
  Our single-GPU implementation outperforms a state-of-the-art GPU-based approximate neighbor graph construction implementation across multiple datasets on a single MI300A APU. Furthermore, SOLANET demonstrates 11X speedup from 32 to 512 APUs for 1 billion data points and 6.9x speedup from 64 to 512 APUs for 2 billion points.

</details>


### [22] [Addressing Variable Heterogeneity in Distributed Multimodal Training with Entrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27918)
*Insu Jang,Mosharaf Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Entrain分布式多模态LLM训练框架，通过静态模型并行配置和分层微批次分配算法，将微批次间负载差异降低10.6倍，训练吞吐量提升1.40倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态LLM数据集具有内在异构性和显著数据变异性。尽管各模态独立变化，但样本级纠缠使得难以在模态和批次间平衡工作负载。

Method: 1. 将性能分析范式从微观样本转向宏观批次，证明单一静态模型并行配置可实现最优负载均衡；2. 引入分层微批次分配算法，将每次迭代中的过剩工作负载推迟，以稳定微批次间的变异性。

Result: Entrain将微批次间工作负载变异性降低高达10.6倍，相比现有基线，端到端训练吞吐量提升高达1.40倍。

Conclusion: 挑战了"动态数据变异性需要动态模型并行"的直觉，证明了静态配置在宏观批次范式下足以实现最优负载均衡，为多模态LLM训练提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal LLM datasets are inherently heterogeneous, with significant data variability. Although each modality exhibits independent variability, sample-level entanglement makes it difficult to balance workloads across both modalities and batches. We present Entrain, a distributed MLLM training framework that addresses both heterogeneity and variability in multimodal training workloads. Entrain challenges the intuition that dynamic data variability requires dynamic model parallelism by shifting the profiling paradigm from micro-level samples to macroscopic batches. We prove that a single, static model-parallel configuration suffices for optimal load balancing under this paradigm. At the microscopic scale, Entrain introduces a hierarchical microbatch assignment algorithm that defers excess workload within each iteration to stabilize variability across microbatches. Evaluations show that Entrain reduces workload variability across microbatches by up to 10.6$\times$, improving end-to-end training throughput by up to 1.40$\times$ over existing baselines.

</details>


### [23] [SiDP: Memory-Efficient Data Parallelism for Offline LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28095)
*Alan Zhao,Cyril Y. He*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出SiDP，一种内存高效的数据并行范式，通过将权重作为分布式共享资源，显著提升离线LLM推理的KV容量和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM离线推理部署面临结构性矛盾：数据并行(DP)扩展吞吐量但复制模型权重，限制KV缓存和批处理大小；模型并行虽减少每设备权重，但需要细粒度同步，削弱DP的独立性和调度灵活性。

Method: 提出SiDP范式，将权重组织为分布式池，每层由单个GPU拥有。提供两种互补执行模式：(1) Weight-as-a-Service (WaS)模式：在大批量场景下通过NVLink将远程权重流式传输到小缓存；(2) Compute-as-a-Service (CaS)模式：在小批量尾部将激活值传输给权重拥有者。

Result: 在NVIDIA H20、H200和B200 GPU上，使用Qwen3-32B、Qwen2.5-72B和Llama-3.1-70B模型评估，SiDP在相同配置下将可用KV容量提升至1.8倍，相比vLLM基线，离线工作负载的端到端吞吐量提升至1.5倍。

Conclusion: SiDP成功解决了离线LLM推理中数据并行的内存瓶颈问题，通过创新的权重共享机制，在不牺牲调度灵活性的前提下，显著提升了GPU内存利用率和推理吞吐量。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of large language models (LLMs) has shifted a substantial portion of inference workloads into throughput-oriented offline regimes, where fully utilizing GPU compute requires large batch sizes. However, existing deployments face a structural tension. Data parallelism (DP) scales throughput well but replicates model weights, leaving limited GPU memory for key-value (KV) cache and constraining batch size. Model parallelism reduces per-device weights, but requires fine-grained synchronization that erodes DP's independence and scheduling flexibility. We present SiDP, a memory-efficient data-parallel paradigm for offline LLM inference that treats weights as a bandwidth-backed shared resource inside a DP group. Instead of storing the full model on every GPU, SiDP organizes weights as a distributed pool: each layer is owned by a single GPU, and other replicas access its weights on demand via two complementary execution modes: a Weight-as-a-Service (WaS) mode that streams remote weights over NVLink into a small cache in the large-batch regime, and a Compute-as-a-Service (CaS) mode that ships activations to owners in the small-batch tail. Evaluated on NVIDIA H20, H200, and B200 GPUs with Qwen3-32B, Qwen2.5-72B, and Llama-3.1-70B, SiDP increases usable KV capacity by up to 1.8x under the same configurations, and converts this into up to 1.5x higher end-to-end throughput over baselines (vLLM) for offline workloads.

</details>


### [24] [Resource Allocation in HyperX Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28205)
*Alejandro Cano,Cristóbal Camarero,Carmen Martínez,Ramón Beivide*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对HyperX网络提出了多种资源分配策略，通过理论分析和实验评估发现对角线分配策略在大多数场景下优于传统方法，为高性能计算系统的资源管理提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着高性能计算系统规模和复杂度的增长，高效的资源管理对于最小化通信开销至关重要。HyperX作为一种高连通性、低直径的网络拓扑，相比传统拓扑具有可扩展和成本效益优势。然而，HyperX中的资源分配问题尚未得到充分探索，且针对Torus、Fat-tree或Dragonfly等网络设计的策略无法直接应用于HyperX网络。

Method: 本文提出并形式化了多种针对HyperX网络的资源分配策略，将其分类为线性、几何和随机函数三类。研究者从理论上对这些策略进行特征分析，考察其拓扑属性，包括膨胀度、凸性和分区带宽。此外，通过合成流量和应用通信内核进行详尽的实验评估，分析这些策略在不同路由算法下的性能影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，分区带宽和交换机局部性是减轻干扰的关键因素。值得注意的是，非凸的对角线分配策略在大多数场景下始终优于传统方法。研究还分析了不同策略在不同路由算法下的表现差异。

Conclusion: 本文为基于HyperX网络的高性能计算系统资源分配策略的实施提供了一系列经验教训和指导原则，证明了专门针对HyperX拓扑特性设计的资源分配策略的重要性，为未来HPC系统的资源管理提供了理论和实践基础。

Abstract: As high-performance computing systems scale in size and complexity, efficient resource management is essential to minimize communication overhead. The HyperX is a richly connected, low-diameter network that offers a scalable and cost-effective alternative to traditional topologies. However, resource allocation in HyperX remains underexplored, and strategies designed for networks like Torus, Fat-tree, or Dragonfly do not directly transfer. In this work, we propose and formalize several resource allocation strategies for HyperX networks, categorized into linear, geometric, and stochastic functions. We characterize these strategies theoretically by analyzing their topological properties, including dilation, convexity, and partition bandwidth.Furthermore, we conduct an exhaustive experimental evaluation using synthetic traffic and application communication kernels to assess the impact of these strategies on performance under different routing algorithms. Our results indicate that partition bandwidth and switch locality are decisive factors in mitigating interferences. Notably, the Diagonal allocation strategy, which is not convex, consistently outperforms traditional approaches in most scenarios. Finally, we provide a set of lessons learned to guide the implementation of resource allocation policies in HPC systems based on HyperX networks.

</details>


### [25] [TrioSeq: A Novel Approach to Accelerate Triplet Sequence Alignment on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28400)
*Miguel Graça,Aleksandar Ilic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了TrioSeq，一种在GPU上高效实现3-way序列比对算法的细粒度并行策略，通过利用新型GPU并行和同步特性，在NVIDIA和AMD GPU上相比现有方法性能提升至少20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的多序列比对(MSA)算法基于渐进式方法，依赖于成对序列比对(PSA)。虽然有大量研究致力于加速PSA，但越来越多的证据表明基于精确的3-way比对可以提供更稳健、准确的MSA。然而，当前3-way比对的硬件加速研究仍然稀缺，现有GPU方法因缺乏对新硬件特性(如跨线程内联函数)的支持而效率低下，且多为闭源和特定厂商实现。

Method: 提出TrioSeq，一种细粒度策略，通过利用新型GPU并行层次和同步机制，在GPU上高效实现3-way比对算法。该方法充分利用现代GPU的新特性(如跨线程内联函数)来实现序列三元组的高吞吐量比对。

Result: 在NVIDIA和AMD GPU上的评估表明，TrioSeq在模拟基因组数据集上的3-way比对性能比现有最先进的GPU渐进式方法至少提升20%。

Conclusion: TrioSeq成功填补了3-way比对硬件加速领域的空白，通过细粒度并行策略实现了跨平台(NVIDIA和AMD)的高效实现，为更准确的基因组分析提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: State-of-the-art multiple sequence alignment (MSA) algorithms are based on progressive approaches that rely on pairwise sequence alignment (PSA) to generate guide trees to align all sequences. Given an evidenced explosion in genomic data availability, research efforts have focused on accelerating PSA on massively-parallel architectures (e.g., GPUs) and specialized hardware (e.g., FPGAs). However, there is increasing evidence that starting from exact 3-way alignments could provide more robust, accurate MSAs, and improve genomic analysis. While the current literature has shown that PSA algorithms can be extended to align sequence triplets, the existent state-of-the-art on hardware acceleration of exact 3-way alignments is still scarce. In particular, current GPU methods are still inefficient due to lacking support for novel hardware features (e.g., cross-thread intrinsics), while being closed-source and vendor-specific. In this paper, TrioSeq is proposed as a fine-grained strategy to efficiently implement 3-way alignments on GPUs, leveraging novel levels of GPU parallelism and synchronization to achieve high throughput in aligning sequence triplets. Evaluation on NVIDIA and AMD GPUs shows that TrioSeq outperforms state-of-the-art GPU progressive methods on 3-way alignment by at least 20% on simulated genomic datasets.

</details>


### [26] [Fault Tolerance of Accelerated Asynchronous Fixed-Point Iterations on Flexible Computing Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28426)
*Evan Coleman,Masha Sosonkina*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究Anderson加速在异步迭代方法中的有效性。发现异步执行普遍提供显著加速(2.9×-16.9×)，但Anderson加速的有效性取决于陈旧性机制的类型：在迭代级破坏下失效，在评估级扰动下保持有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步迭代方法通过允许工作节点使用陈旧数据来容忍落后节点，但会导致迭代不一致性，可能降低收敛性能。研究者想探究收敛加速技术(如Anderson加速)是否能补偿这种性能退化。

Method: 实验研究三种定点迭代方法：稀疏线性系统的Jacobi方法、Bellman方程的价值迭代、Hartree-Fock自洽场迭代。使用高性能执行框架Ray进行分布式系统实验，实现代码并行化和故障注入。识别两种陈旧性机制：迭代级破坏和评估级扰动。

Result: 获得两个主要结果：(1)落后节点容忍具有普遍性，异步执行相比同步执行在100ms延迟工作节点下获得2.9×(Jacobi)、7.7×(VI)、16.9×(SCF)的加速，与是否使用加速无关；(2)Anderson加速在异步环境下的有效性取决于陈旧性进入计算的位置，在迭代级破坏下完全失效，在评估级扰动下保持优势。

Conclusion: Anderson加速在异步执行中的存活性主要由陈旧性机制类型决定，而非映射的收缩范数或平滑性。在评估级扰动机制下，Anderson加速能够保持其加速效果，这为异步迭代方法的设计和应用提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Asynchronous iterative methods tolerate straggling processors by allowing workers to proceed with stale data, but at a cost: the iterates become inconsistent, potentially degrading convergence. We investigate whether convergence accelerators such as Anderson acceleration compensate for this degradation. We experimentally study three fixed-point iterations: the Jacobi method for sparse linear systems, value iteration for the Bellman equation, and the Hartree--Fock self-consistent field (SCF) iteration. The experiments are conducted using a high-performance execution framework Ray, which abstracts the complexity of distributed systems and enables code parallelization and fault injection with minimal changes.
  We establish two main results. First, straggler tolerance is universal: asynchronous execution provides wall-clock speedups of $2.9\times$ (Jacobi), $7.7\times$ (VI), and $16.9\times$ (SCF) over synchronous execution with a 100\,ms-delayed worker, independent of whether acceleration is used. Second, Anderson acceleration's effectiveness under asynchrony depends on where staleness enters the computation. We identify two staleness mechanisms: iterate-level corruption, where stale worker returns directly overwrite portions of the accelerated iterate (as in block Jacobi), and evaluation-level perturbation, where staleness acts as a bounded perturbation to the fixed-point map evaluation (as in VI and SCF). Anderson acceleration fails categorically under the first mechanism but retains its benefits under the second, consistent with the perturbation analysis of Toth et al.\ (2017). This distinction, rather than the contraction norm or smoothness of the map, is the primary determinant of whether acceleration survives asynchronous execution.

</details>

### [27] [Bounded Priority-Aware Locking for Real-Time Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27620)
*Shriram Raja,Richard West*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出了批量优先级锁（BPL），一种优先级感知的自旋锁机制，在保持FIFO锁最坏情况等待时间上界的同时，通过在等待组内按优先级选择锁持有者，降低高优先级任务的平均等待时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实时多核系统中，共享资源（如内核）访问需要延迟界限。传统FIFO锁虽然能保证等待时间界限，但无法区分竞争任务的重要性，而严格优先级机制可能导致饥饿和无界的锁获取延迟。因此需要一种既能保证等待时间界限，又能优先服务高优先级任务的锁机制。

Method: 提出批量优先级锁（BPL），采用两阶段策略：首先按锁请求顺序将等待任务分组，然后在等待组内根据优先级确定下一个锁持有者。这是严格优先级排序和FIFO排序之间的折中方案。

Result: 在多达64核的仿真实验和8核实时操作系统的实际实现中，与替代锁方案相比，BPL显著降低了高优先级任务的平均等待时间，同时保持了与FIFO锁相同的等待时间上界。虽然复杂度高于简单自旋锁，但在实际系统中常见情况的执行开销很低。

Conclusion: BPL成功实现了在保证最坏情况等待时间界限的前提下，优先服务高优先级任务的目标。这种在可预测性和性能之间的折中对于需要可预测性的实时系统是可接受的代价。

Abstract: A real-time multicore system requires delay bounds on access to shared resources. These resources include the kernel, which has potentially many non-preemptible critical sections guarded by one or more different synchronization primitives. While primitives such as FIFO locks bound the waiting time to enter a critical section, they do not distinguish the importance of individual tasks competing for shared resource access. To address this, we consider a priority-aware spinlock, which reduces the average delay of more important tasks while maintaining a worst-case bound on lock waiting time. We propose a Batched Priority Lock (BPL) that first groups waiting tasks based on the order of their lock requests, and then determines the next lock holder according to priority within the waiting group. We compare BPL to alternative lock approaches, showing that the average waiting time is reduced for higher priority tasks, in simulations up to 64 cores, and for a working implementation on an 8-core machine with a real RTOS. BPL is a compromise between strict priority and FIFO ordering. While strict priorities may lead to starvation and, hence, unbounded lock acquisition delays, BPL has the same waiting bound as FIFO, but with benefits to higher priority tasks. Although its complexity is greater than that of a simple spinlock, its common case execution overhead is shown to be inexpensive in a working system. We believe this is an acceptable cost in systems that require predictability.

</details>
