Abstract: Large language models increasingly rely on either reinforcement learning or multi-agent prompting to improve reasoning, yet these two paradigms remain difficult to combine. Directly applying single-agent reinforcement learning to multi-turn multi-agent systems faces following dilemmas: i) Sparse rewards, role-level free-riding and excessive training overhead. ii) Agents only imitate to collaborate. iii) Fixed collaboration protocol falls into oscillating local optimum. We introduce TRACER, a turn-level reinforcement framework for cooperative multi-LLM reasoning. TRACER separates collaborative decision making into a controller-regret layer, where controllers learn whether the agents should speak or skip the current round through regret matching, and a generation-credit layer, which optimizes proposer and reviewer utterances with role-specific GSPO rewards. This design i) assigns credit at the level of both action modes and generated utterances, thus avoiding free-riding and sparse rewards. We only expand the choices made by the controllers, thus greatly reducing computational cost of training. Moreover, ii) agents acquire collaborative capability as they learn when to utter and what to speak. Finally, iii) by designing binary actions ingeniously, we extend classical game theory established for finite action spaces to deep learning, thus achieving mathematically rigorous convergence. We train all local RL-style methods on the GSM8K training split and evaluate on held-out GSM8K, MATH500, and GPQA-Diamond to measure in-domain accuracy, cross-benchmark generalization, inference cost, and correction-preservation behavior. The resulting framework provides a compact and reproducible testbed for studying learned collaboration policies beyond fixed debate, voting, or aggregation protocols. Code is available at https://github.com/Shark-Forest/TRACER.

</details>


### [176] [The Importance of Being Statistically Earnest: A Critical Re-evaluation of GSM-Symbolic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28700)
*Dominika Agnieszka Długosz,Arlindo Oliveira,Natalia Díaz Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过严格的统计方法重新评估了GSM-Symbolic基准，发现原研究的结论缺乏统计支持：仅半数模型表现显著下降，且数据集存在未被承认的数值分布偏移。研究揭示了大语言模型失败具有模型特异性，驳斥了对LLM推理能力的一刀切论断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GSM-Symbolic基准声称25个大语言模型在变体问题上性能下降，断言模型缺乏真正的推理能力。作者认为该结论统计基础不牢固，需要进行严格的统计重评估，并探索潜在的影响因素。

Method: 使用广义线性混合模型（包含问题层面的随机效应）重新评估20个开源权重模型；通过Kolmogorov-Smirnov检验检测数据集分布偏移；控制大数效应进行统计分析；识别模型特异性的失败模式。

Result: 在原始提示格式下，仅半数模型表现出统计显著的性能变化；GSM-Symbolic数据集相比GSM-Base存在系统性数值分布偏移（K-S统计量=0.12，p<0.001）；控制大数效应可解释剩余显著案例的一半；不同模型呈现独特的失败特征：变量绑定脆弱性、算术局限性、双任务干扰等。

Conclusion: 对大语言模型推理能力的一刀切论断在统计上为时过早，在机制上具有误导性。模型表现下降源于多种模型特异性因素，而非统一的"缺乏推理能力"，需要更细致的分析来理解LLM的真实能力边界。

Abstract: The GSM-Symbolic benchmark (Mirzadeh et al., 2025) reported consistent performance drops across 25 Large Language Models (LLMs) when tested on template-generated variants of GSM8K problems, concluding that the models lack genuine reasoning capabilities. We argue that this conclusion rests on shaky statistical ground. Re-evaluating 20 open-weight models using Generalised Linear Mixed Models with per-question random effects, we find that only half exhibit statistically significant performance changes under the original prompt format. Moreover, we identify a previously unacknowledged factor: the main GSM-Symbolic dataset contains a systematically shifted distribution of larger integers in problem texts relative to GSM-Base (K-S statistic = 0.12, p < 0.001), contradicting the original authors' claims. Controlling for this large number effect accounts for significance in roughly half the remaining cases. Among models with statistically significant performance deltas, we identify distinct, model-specific failure profiles - including fragility of variable binding, arithmetic limitations, and dual-task interference - underscoring that blanket claims about LLM reasoning are both statistically premature and mechanistically misleading.

</details>


### [177] [Beyond Binary Moral Judgment: Modeling Ethical Pluralism in AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28707)
*Aisha Aijaz,Rahul Goel,Arnav Batra,Raghava Mutharaju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个将道德推理建模为规范伦理学理论分布（伦理多元主义）的框架，通过规范伦理学单纯形和双流规范-语义架构，在450个伦理困境案例上实现了88.89%的分类准确率，为AI系统的道德决策提供了更具解释性和问责性的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主道德决策方法多采用标量或二元判断，缺乏充分的解释性，无法提供支持问责制所需的情境和理论信息，不足以进行可接受的道德推理。

Method: 提出规范伦理学单纯形框架整合多种伦理理论；构建包含15个细粒度子理论的450个伦理困境案例基准；采用双流规范-语义架构实现单纯形，融合规范信息与语义嵌入；使用顺序堆叠集成学习方法对后果主义、德性伦理学和义务论三大理论及其15个子类别进行分类。

Result: 实验表明，整合情境和规范先验知识与语义嵌入显著提升了分类性能，准确率达到88.89%；消融研究显示结构化伦理表征超越了类比推理，堆叠架构因粒度的渐进学习而表现最佳；通过熵、置信度和可视化分析了伦理多元主义。

Conclusion: 将伦理多元主义建模为概率性规范分布，能够支持类人道德推理、伦理分歧分析以及未来AI系统的对齐工作，为自主系统的道德决策提供了更完善的解决方案。

Abstract: Critical decision-making in socially consequential spaces is increasingly involving AI systems at varying capacities. Yet, despite the ubiquity of autonomous systems, most approaches to handling autonomous moral decision-making resort to scalar or binary judgments. These methods are insufficient for acceptable moral reasoning, as they provide little explanation, leaving out imperative contextual and theoretical information that must be included to support accountability. For this, we propose a framework to model moral reasoning as a distribution over normative ethical theories or ethical pluralism. We introduce a normative ethics simplex that integrates these theories. A benchmark of 450 cases across 15 fine-grained subtheories was also prepared for the purposes of stacked ensemble learning. These cases describe ethical dilemmas in natural language and have associated extracted contextual features. The implementation of the simplex was achieved via a two-stream normative-semantic architecture. This is followed by the fusion of normative information and a sequential, stacking ensemble to learn the best fit of the three broad theories: consequentialism, virtue ethics, and deontology, and the 15 subcategories. Our experiments demonstrate that the integration of contextual and normative priors with the semantic embeddings significantly improves the performance of the classification, displaying an accuracy of 88.89%. We conducted ablation studies to show that structured ethical representations contribute beyond analogical reasoning, and the chosen stacking architecture gives the best results due to the gradual learning of granularity. Ethical pluralism is also analyzed through entropy, confidence, and visualization. Thus, modeling ethical pluralism as a probabilistic normative distribution supports human-like moral reasoning, ethical disagreement analysis, and future alignment in AI systems.

</details>


### [178] [Thinking as Compression: Your Reasoning Model is Secretly a Context Compressor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28713)
*Guoxin Ma,Yibing Liu,Chengzhengxu Li,Yu Liang,Yan Wang,Yueyang Zhang,Kecheng Chen,Zhaohan Zhang,Zhiyuan Sun,Daiting Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Thinking as Compression (TaC)和TaC-C方法，利用思考模型自身的能力进行上下文压缩，无需专门压缩模块，在4倍和8倍压缩率下平均F1分别超越最强基线17.4%和23.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文压缩方法通常依赖复杂的压缩模块或特定训练，而未充分探索LLM的内在能力。本文旨在挖掘思考模型本身自然压缩长上下文的潜力。

Method: 提出Thinking as Compression (TaC)范式，将思考过程直接作为压缩上下文；进一步提出TaC-C，通过奖励驱动的优化框架引导模型生成紧凑可控的压缩上下文。

Result: 在四个长上下文QA基准测试中，TaC-C持续超越现有基线。在4倍和8倍压缩率下，平均F1分别比最强竞争者高出17.4%和23.4%，平均精确匹配分数(EM)分别高出15.7%和21.7%。

Conclusion: 思考模型本身具备自然压缩长上下文的能力，通过简单有效的奖励驱动优化可以充分激发这一内在能力，实现更优的压缩性能。

Abstract: Context compression aims to shorten long context inputs with minimal information loss for LLM inference acceleration. While existing methods have shown promise, they typically rely on complex compression modules or compression-specific training, leaving the intrinsic capabilities of LLMs underexplored. In contrast, this work reveals that a thinking model itself can naturally compress long contexts by organizing task-relevant information. We thus derive Thinking as Compression (TaC), a new compression paradigm that treats thinking itself as compressed context. Without relying on specific dedicated compressor, TaC directly prompts the thinking model to generate thinking traces as the shortened context, already outperforming most representative compression methods. Further, given that raw thinking output may struggle with budget control and shortcut behaviors, we introduce Thinking as Compression Constrained (TaC-C), leveraging a simple reward-driven optimization framework to elicit intrinsic thinking as compact and controllable compressed context. Experiments across four long-context QA benchmarks demonstrate that TaC-C consistently outperforms existing baselines. At 4x and 8x compression ratios, it surpasses the strongest competitor by 17.4% and 23.4% in average F1, and by 15.7% and 21.7% in average Exact Match Score (EM), respectively.

</details>


### [179] [LiveBrowseComp: Are Search Agents Searching, or Just Verifying What They Already Know?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28721)
*HuiMing Fan,Xiao Wang,Zheng Chu,Qianyu Wang,Zhuoyao Wang,Ming Liu,Bing Qin,XingYu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM搜索代理存在内在知识依赖(IKD)问题，常依赖模型内编码知识而非外部证据。作者提出LiveBrowseComp基准测试，使用近期发布的事实构建问题，有效区分了真实搜索能力与记忆验证能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索基准测试可能混淆了代理已知信息与真实搜索发现能力，奖励基于记忆的验证而非证据驱动的发现，无法真实评估LLM搜索代理的实际检索能力。

Method: 1) 在BrowseComp上设计三种诊断方法分析内在知识依赖现象；2) 构建LiveBrowseComp基准，包含335个人工编写的问题，答案依赖于基准构建前90天内发布的事实，来源覆盖六个更新源并过滤掉全球显著事件。

Result: BrowseComp上代理无需工具可回答44.5%问题，超半数搜索查询源于内部假设；LiveBrowseComp上所有评估代理闭书准确率低于2%，搜索增强分数相比BrowseComp下降25-40分，原有模型排名不再可靠预测性能。

Conclusion: 静态搜索基准测试会奖励记忆支持的验证而非证据驱动的发现。LiveBrowseComp通过使用近期事实有效规避了内在知识覆盖问题，能够更准确评估代理的真实搜索能力。

Abstract: Are LLM-based search agents genuinely searching, or using the web to verify what they already know? We study this question on BrowseComp with three diagnostics. Our analysis reveals Intrinsic Knowledge Dependence (IKD): even with tool access, agents often rely on intrinsic knowledge -- information encoded in the model before retrieval -- rather than on external evidence. Agents answer up to 44.5% of BrowseComp questions without tools, generate more than half of their search queries from internally produced hypotheses rather than retrieved leads, and perform worse than closed-book baselines when answer-supporting evidence is removed. These results suggest that static search benchmarks can reward memory-backed verification rather than evidence-driven discovery, conflating what agents already know with what they can find. We then introduce LiveBrowseComp, a deep-search benchmark designed to evaluate agents beyond intrinsic coverage. It contains 335 human-authored questions whose answers depend on facts published within the 90 days preceding benchmark construction, drawn from six updated sources and filtered to exclude globally salient events. On LiveBrowseComp, all evaluated agents fall below 2% closed-book accuracy, search-augmented scores drop by 25-40 points relative to BrowseComp, and prior model rankings no longer reliably predict performance. LiveBrowseComp is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Forival/LiveBrowseComp.

</details>


### [180] [Multi-Adapter Representation Interventions via Energy Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28722)
*Manjiang Yu,Hongji Li,Junwei Chen,Xue Li,Priyanka Singh,Yang Cao,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MARI方法，通过竞争性多适配器机制和基于能量的门控模块，实现自适应的表示干预，在大语言模型对齐任务上达到SOTA性能，同时保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示干预方法对所有输入采用统一的固定干预，但研究发现合适的干预方向和强度在不同样本间差异显著，这种不加区分的干预会导致良性输入上的通用能力退化。

Method: 提出MARI（Multi-Adapter Representation Interventions via Energy Calibration）：1）引入竞争性多适配器机制，由专门化专家捕获非线性校正模式，自适应确定不同样本的干预方向和强度；2）设计基于能量的门控模块，利用内部传播动力学区分适用于干预的输入。

Result: 在多个模型家族和参数规模上的广泛实验表明，MARI实现了最先进的对齐性能。在TruthfulQA、BBQ和安全基准测试上显著提升性能，同时在MMLU和ARC等任务上保持甚至提升通用能力。

Conclusion: MARI成功解决了现有表示干预方法中统一干预导致的通用能力退化问题，通过自适应干预实现了对齐性能和通用能力的平衡，为大语言模型对齐提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Representation intervention has emerged as a promising paradigm for aligning large language models toward desired behaviors without modifying model weights. Existing methods typically apply a fixed intervention uniformly across all inputs. However, we find that the appropriate intervention direction and strength vary substantially across samples, and such indiscriminate intervention leads to degradation of general capabilities on benign inputs. To address these challenges, we propose Multi-Adapter Representation Interventions via Energy Calibration (MARI). Specifically, we introduce a competitive multi-adapter mechanism in which specialized experts capture non-linear correction patterns and adaptively determine the appropriate intervention direction and strength for different samples. Furthermore, we design an energy-based gating module that leverages internal propagation dynamics to distinguish inputs that are applicable for intervention. Extensive experiments across diverse model families and parameter scales demonstrate that MARI achieves state-of-the-art alignment performance. Our method significantly improves performance on TruthfulQA, BBQ, and safety benchmarks, while maintaining and even improving general capabilities on tasks such as MMLU and ARC. Our code is available at https://github.com/V1centNevwake/MARI.

</details>


### [181] [AlphaTransit: Learning to Design City-scale Transit Routes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28730)
*Bibek Poudel,Sai Swaminathan,Weizi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AlphaTransit框架，将蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)与神经策略-价值网络结合，解决公交网络设计中的延迟反馈问题，在Bloomington基准测试上相比纯强化学习和纯MCTS分别提升9.9%-11.4%和2.5%-11.2%的服务率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公交路线网络设计问题(TRNDP)面临延迟反馈挑战：路线扩展决策的质量只有在完整网络组装后才能显现，局部看似有用的扩展可能造成换乘瓶颈、冗余重叠或降低整体吞吐量，需要在不运行模拟器的情况下进行决策时前瞻。

Method: 提出AlphaTransit框架，耦合蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)与神经策略-价值网络：策略网络提出路线扩展建议，价值网络估计下游设计质量，搜索过程利用这些预测来优化每个决策，在路线构建过程中提供决策时前瞻，避免在搜索树内运行模拟器推演。

Result: 在包含真实路网拓扑和人口普查衍生需求的新Bloomington TRNDP基准上进行评估，AlphaTransit在混合和全公交需求设置下均达到最高服务率，分别为54.6%和82.1%；相比无搜索的强化学习提升9.9%和11.4%；相比无学习引导的MCTS提升2.5%和11.2%。

Conclusion: 将学习引导与MCTS结合比单独使用任一方法在公交网络设计中更有效，证明了耦合方法在解决延迟反馈问题上的优势。

Abstract: Designing a transit network requires many sequential route extension decisions, but their quality is often visible only after the full network is assembled. This delayed-feedback challenge lies at the heart of the Transit Route Network Design Problem (TRNDP), where route interactions can be deceptive: an extension that appears useful locally can create transfer bottlenecks, produce redundant overlap, or reduce overall throughput. To guide route construction under delayed simulator feedback, we introduce AlphaTransit, a search-based planning framework for cityscale bus network design. AlphaTransit couples Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) with a neural policy-value network: the policy proposes route extensions, the value estimates downstream design quality, and search uses these predictions to refine each decision. This provides decision-time lookahead during route construction without running simulator rollouts inside the search tree. We evaluate AlphaTransit on a new Bloomington TRNDP benchmark with realistic road topology and censusderived demand, under mixed and full transit demand settings. In the Bloomington network, AlphaTransit attains the highest service rate in both demand settings, reaching 54.6% and 82.1%, respectively. Relative to reinforcement learning without search, these correspond to 9.9% and 11.4% service rate gains; relative to MCTS without learned guidance, they correspond to 2.5% and 11.2% gains. These results suggest that coupling learned guidance with MCTS is more effective than using either approach alone for transit network design. Our code and data are publicly available in https://github.com/poudel-bibek/AlphaTransit.

</details>


### [182] [Utility-Aware Multimodal Contrastive Learning for Product Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28733)
*Xiaohang Feng,Yiling Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了效用感知的多模态对比学习框架，通过将消费者需求融入新的效用感知InfoNCE损失函数，引导生成式AI生成既能保持语义一致性又能提升市场需求的产品图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI模型仅关注文本-图像的语义对齐，但语义对齐并不能保证图像能够有效促进销售。在线市场中，产品图像对消费者决策有重大影响，因此需要直接优化市场表现而不仅仅是语义一致性，这是一个关键的研究空白。

Method: 提出效用感知多模态对比学习框架，设计新颖的效用感知InfoNCE损失函数，将消费者需求纳入优化目标。该方法使学习到的图像-文本表示空间向需求驱动的视觉线索转移，并通过理论边界验证了目标函数的有效性。

Result: 在Amazon和Airbnb的下游应用中，该方法生成和编辑的产品图像在提升需求和保持保真度方面均优于最先进模型，同时保持文本-图像一致性。方法能够保持美学和独特性等属性的反U型需求模式，人类受试者实验进一步验证了其商业有效性。

Conclusion: 效用感知框架成功地将市场需求直接纳入生成式AI的优化目标，随着生成式AI技术的不断发展，该效用感知组件可以灵活嵌入新兴生成模型中以改善直接商业应用，为电子商务领域的产品图像生成提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Product images strongly influence consumer decision-making in online marketplaces. Empowered by multimodal contrastive learning, generative AI can output images that closely align with text prompts. Yet existing generative AI models do not directly optimize marketplace performance. This is a critical gap, since semantic alignment alone does not guarantee that an image will sell. To address this limitation, we propose a \textit{utility-aware multimodal contrastive learning} framework that incorporates consumer demand into a novel Utility-Aware InfoNCE loss. Optimizing this utility-aware objective guides generation toward images that are both semantically coherent and demand-enhancing. This effect arises directly from a shift in the learned image-text representation space toward demand-driven visual cues, which we also validate through the theoretical bound of the proposed objective. In downstream applications on Amazon and Airbnb, product images generated and edited by our method outperform state-of-the-art models in increasing demand and preserving fidelity, while maintaining text-image consistency. Notably, our utility-aware framework preserves inverse U-shaped demand patterns for attributes such as aesthetics and uniqueness, improving demand-based performance while preserving fidelity and semantic consistency. Human-subject experiments further validate its commercial effectiveness. As generative AI technology continues to evolve, our utility-aware component can be flexibly embedded into emerging generative models to improve direct commercial use.

</details>


### [183] [CORE: Contrastive Reflection Enables Rapid Improvements in Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28742)
*Linas Nasvytis,Simon Jerome Han,Ben Prystawski,Satchel Grant,Noah D. Goodman,Judith E. Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CORE（对比反思）算法，通过对比成功与失败的推理轨迹生成自然语言洞察，在极少样本和推演预算下实现高效的语言模型自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有利用可验证奖励改进语言模型推理能力的方法（包括参数化的RLVR和非参数化的提示优化）通常需要数百个训练样本和数千次模型推演，成本高昂甚至难以实现。

Method: 引入对比反思（CORE）非参数学习算法，通过对比历史推理轨迹生成"洞察"——简短的自然语言描述，捕捉成功与失败问题尝试之间的推理策略和约束差异。

Result: 在四个推理任务上，CORE在更少推演次数下比参数化方法（GRPO）和非参数化基线方法（GEPA、情景RAG、MemRL）实现更快改进；在仅有5个训练样本的固定推演预算下，CORE达到相当或更大的性能提升；CORE比非参数化基线更具上下文效率，需要更少的提示token。

Conclusion: 将成功与失败推理轨迹之间的对比提炼为抽象且有用的洞察，可以提供比权重更新、提示优化或直接重用存储推理轨迹更高效、更具可解释性的模型自我改进路径。

Abstract: Language models can use verifiable rewards to improve at a wide variety of reasoning tasks. However, both parametric (e.g. RLVR) and non-parametric (e.g. prompt optimization) approaches to doing so typically require hundreds of training samples and thousands of model rollouts, making them expensive in the best case and intractable in the worst. To address this challenge, we introduce Contrastive Reflection (CORE), a non-parametric learning algorithm that compares past reasoning traces to generate insights: short natural-language descriptions of reasoning strategies and constraints that capture differences between successful and unsuccessful problem attempts. Across four reasoning tasks, we demonstrate that CORE enables more rapid improvement than both parametric (GRPO) and non-parametric (GEPA, episodic RAG, and MemRL) methods, while using fewer rollouts. Under fixed rollout budgets with as few as five training samples, we then show that CORE also achieves comparable or greater performance gains than each baseline. Finally, we highlight how CORE is also substantially more context-efficient than non-parametric baselines, requiring fewer prompt tokens while storing learned knowledge as compact, interpretable natural-language insights. Our results therefore suggest that distilling contrasts between successful and unsuccessful reasoning traces into abstract and useful insights can provide a more efficient and interpretable route to model self-improvement than weight updates, prompt optimization, or direct reuse of stored reasoning traces.

</details>


### [184] [CubePart: An Open-Vocabulary Part-Controllable 3D Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28763)
*Yiheng Zhu,Kangle Deng,Jean-Philippe Fauconnier,Inaki Navarro,Daiqing Li,Ava Pun,Yinan Zhang,Peiye Zhuang,Xiaoxia Sun,Maneesh Agrawala,Kiran Bhat,Tinghui Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CubePart是一个支持开放词汇、部件可控的3D网格生成框架，可根据用户定义的部件模式生成具有明确语义结构的可装配3D资产，无需手动后处理即可直接用于游戏引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏和仿真中使用的交互式3D资产通常需要分解为特定语义部件以支持动画、物理和脚本行为，但现有生成式3D模型大多生成整体网格或任意部件分解，无法满足应用特定的部件结构需求。

Method: 1. 构建大规模开放词汇、部件标注的3D数据集的可扩展数据流水线；2. 两阶段生成架构：将全局形状合成与部件级解码分离，以全局文本提示和用户定义的部件模式列表作为输入，为每个部件生成独立网格。

Result: 生成的3D资产部件能够组装成连贯物体，严格遵守指定的语义结构，可直接集成到游戏引擎中并由动画和行为脚本驱动，无需人工后处理。

Conclusion: CubePart成功实现了部件结构的显式推理时控制，为游戏和仿真应用提供了即用型、语义可控的3D资产生成解决方案，填补了生成模型与实际应用需求之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Interactive 3D assets used in games and simulation are typically decomposed into specific semantic parts to support animation, physics, and scripted behaviors, yet most generative 3D models produce either monolithic meshes or arbitrary part decompositions that cannot be aligned with application-specific requirements. We present CubePart, a generative framework for open-vocabulary, part-controllable 3D mesh generation that exposes part structure as an explicit inference-time control signal. Given a global text prompt and a user-defined parts schema expressed as an open-ended list of part names, our method generates a set of meshes - one per schema element - that assemble into a coherent object while respecting the specified semantic structure. To enable this capability, we introduce a scalable data pipeline to construct a large open-vocabulary, part-labeled 3D dataset, along with a two-stage generative architecture that separates global shape synthesis from part-level decoding. We demonstrate that the resulting assets can be directly integrated into game engines and driven by animation and behavior scripts without manual post-processing. Project Page: https://cubepart.github.io/

</details>


### [185] [CaMBRAIN: Real-time, Continuous EEG Inference with Causal State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28792)
*Abhilash Durgam,Nyle Siddiqui,Jeffrey A. Chan-Santiago,Qiushi Fu,Elakkat D. Gireesh,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了CaMBRAIN，首个基于Mamba的因果状态空间模型，能够实时处理变长EEG信号，在三个数据集上达到SOTA性能，吞吐量提升10倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG深度学习模型面临两大挑战：(1) 基于注意力机制的模型随序列长度呈二次方扩展；(2) 固定长度输入要求导致必须使用滑动窗口处理原始EEG信号，无法理解全局信号。此外，关键EEG事件可能极短暂但间隔很长，现有自监督目标优化信号重建，不适合流式SSM，无法显式训练隐藏状态保留远程上下文。

Method: 提出CaMBRAIN——首个因果、基于Mamba的状态空间模型，利用EEG的单向因果特性避免双向方法的不必要开销。设计了多阶段自监督训练流水线，专门鼓励长期记忆保留，同时保持状态空间模型的线性时间复杂度，使模型能够在流式推理中保留显著的远程上下文信息。

Result: 在3个不同EEG数据集上达到SOTA性能，吞吐量比现有模型提高10倍以上，实现了首个能够进行远程、连续推理变长EEG信号的模型。

Conclusion: CaMBRAIN成功解决了EEG信号处理中的序列长度和全局理解问题，为实时、长程EEG信号分析提供了高效解决方案，证明了因果状态空间模型在时间序列医学信号处理中的有效性。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a critical, non-invasive method to monitor electrical brain activity. EEGs can span anywhere from a couple seconds to multiple hours, posing a major hurdle for existing deep learning methods due to two major factors: (1) existing EEG models are predominantly built upon the attention mechanism, incurring quadratic scaling as the sequence length increases, and (2) raw EEG signals must be processed in a sliding-window fashion due to fixed-length input requirements, preventing global understanding of the entire signal. To this extent, we propose CaMBRAIN - the first Causal, Mamba-based state space model (SSM) capable of real-time inference of EEG signals, arguing that bidirectional approaches are needlessly expensive given the causal, unidirectional nature of EEG. However, training such a model is non-trivial, as crucial EEG events can be extremely brief - within fractions of a second - yet separated by long intervals spanning minutes. Current EEG methods use self-supervised objectives that optimize for signal reconstruction, but these are not well suited for streaming SSMs; they fail to explicitly train the hidden state to retain the salient long-range context needed for streaming inference. We therefore introduce a multi-stage self-supervised training pipeline specifically tailored to encourage long-range memory retention and strong performance on EEG signals, while preserving the linear-time complexity of state space models. CaMBRAIN achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) results across 3 different EEG datasets with >10x higher throughput than existing models, enabling the first model capable of long-range, continuous inference of variable-length EEG signals.

</details>


### [186] [Calibrating Conservatism for Scalable Oversight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28807)
*William Overman,Mohsen Bayati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了校准集体监督（CCO）方法，通过聚合辅助评分函数形成惩罚项，结合共形决策理论在线校准，实现对自主AI系统的可扩展监督，在保证统计界限的前提下有效约束强代理的不当行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有自主规划能力的代理AI系统可能超越人类能力，如何对其保持有效监督是核心控制难题。现有可扩展监督方法依赖复杂假设、缺乏统计保证或在序贯场景中不实用。

Method: 提出校准集体监督（CCO）：（1）受可达到效用保持启发，聚合多样化辅助评分函数为惩罚项，度量偏离保守基线的程度；（2）高效用行为仅在监督者认为无异议时被选择，担忧累积时才被否决；（3）利用共形决策理论在线校准保守性，在无分布假设下确保不良结果低于用户指定阈值。

Result: 在改进版SWE-bench上，弱监督者成功约束对抗性错位的强代理；在MACHIAVELLI上，CCO在保留奖励的同时大幅减少伦理违规。两种场景下，实证违规率与理论预测的目标阈值紧密匹配。

Conclusion: CCO为代理AI系统的可扩展监督提供了具有有限时间界限和无分布假设统计保证的实用方法，在理论与实验间达成一致，为高能力AI系统的安全控制开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems capable of autonomous planning and extended environmental interaction pose a fundamental control problem: how can humans maintain meaningful oversight of systems that may exceed their own capabilities? Existing approaches to scalable oversight rely on complex assumptions, remain largely heuristic, or lack practical methods for sequential settings with statistical guarantees. We introduce Calibrated Collective Oversight (CCO), which aggregates diverse auxiliary scoring functions into a penalty measuring deviation from a conservative baseline. Inspired by Attainable Utility Preservation, CCO enables collective conservatism: actions face a penalty proportional to overseer concern, so high-utility actions are still selected when overseers find them unobjectionable and overridden only when concern accumulates. CCO calibrates this conservatism online using Conformal Decision Theory, ensuring that undesirable outcomes remain below a user-specified target threshold with finite-time bounds and no distributional assumptions. On a modified version of SWE-bench, weaker overseers successfully constrain an adversarially misaligned stronger agent; on MACHIAVELLI, CCO substantially reduces ethical violations while preserving reward. In both settings, empirical violation rates closely match the specified targets, as predicted by the theory.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Personalized Observation Normalization for Federated Reinforcement Learning in Simulation Environments with Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27385)
*Yiran Pang,Zhen Ni,Xiangnan Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了个性化观测归一化(PON)方法，通过让每个智能体使用本地运行的均值和方差来归一化状态输入，解决了联邦强化学习在异构环境中的输入分布不一致和参数更新不平衡问题，在异构MuJoCo任务上实现了加速训练和优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦强化学习在异构环境中面临严峻挑战：不同智能体的状态转移动力学差异导致输入分布不一致，在参数聚合时出现不平衡更新。现有方法在处理这种分布异构性时效果有限，需要一种能够保持各智能体本地特征一致缩放且不相互掩盖的解决方案。

Method: 开发个性化观测归一化(PON)方法，允许每个智能体使用持续更新的运行均值和方差在本地归一化原始状态输入。该设计确保本地特征的一致缩放，避免在聚合过程中智能体间的特征相互掩盖。关键创新在于证明了跨智能体共享归一化参数是无效的，确立了个性化统计量的必要性。

Result: 在异构MuJoCo任务上的实验表明，PON方法相比基准方法具有更快的训练速度和更优的性能表现。验证了个性化统计量在处理异构输入分布方面的有效性，以及共享归一化参数在异构环境中的局限性。

Conclusion: 个性化观测归一化是解决联邦强化学习异构性问题的有效途径。在异构环境中，各智能体需要维护独立的归一化统计量而非共享参数，这一设计原则对于构建鲁棒的联邦强化学习系统具有重要意义。

Abstract: Federated reinforcement learning (FedRL) enables multiple agents to collaboratively train a global policy without sharing raw data, making it ideal for privacy-sensitive applications. However, FedRL faces challenges in heterogeneous environments where differing state-transition dynamics lead to non-identical input distributions and imbalanced parameter updates during aggregation. Therefore, this paper develops a personalized observation normalization (PON) method, allowing each agent to locally normalize raw state inputs using a continuously updated running mean and variance. This design ensures consistent scaling of local feature without overshadowing across agents during aggregation. Furthermore, we demonstrate that sharing normalization parameters across agents is ineffective due to the diverse local input distributions, which highlights the necessity of personalized statistics. Experiments on heterogeneous MuJoCo tasks show that our developed PON accelerates training and achieves superior performance compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [188] [IGADA-IoT: IoT Sensor Energy Optimization in Wireless Sensor Networks Driven by Automatic Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27397)
*Mingchun Sun,Rongqiang Zhao,Muhammad Abdul Munnaf,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IGADA-IoT框架，通过分层多生成器协作调度与信息缺口-模型性能联合评估闭环方法，解决IoT传感器数据增强中的信息缺口映射和样本异构性问题，平均准确率提升7.27%-8.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WSNs数据增强方法依赖单一生成器和经验性数量设定，未能建立动态信息缺口与多生成器间的映射关系，忽视了生成样本的异构性，且缺乏联合考虑信息缺口和模型性能的评估与闭环方法，容易导致欠增强或过增强风险。

Method: 提出IGADA-IoT框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) HMGCS策略——分层多生成器协作与调度策略，联合利用不同生成器能力减少信息缺口，增强生成样本分配的针对性与合理性；(2) IGMP-EC方法——信息缺口-模型性能联合评估与闭环方法，提升增强决策准确性，缓解欠增强与过增强风险。

Result: 在UCR Archive公开数据集和真实部署的IoT传感器数据上验证：IGADA-IoT使多个下游模型平均准确率提升7.27%；相比先进数据增强方法平均准确率提升8.67%；相比单个生成器平均准确率提升7.24%。

Conclusion: IGADA-IoT框架有效解决了IoT传感器数据增强中的关键问题，通过多生成器协作与闭环评估机制显著提升了采样频率决策性能和模型准确率，具有良好的准确性和泛化能力。

Abstract: In wireless sensor networks (WSNs), data augmentation is a novel method to improve sampling-frequency decision performance, thereby enabling energy optimization for IoT (Internet of Things) sensors. However, existing methods rely on a single generator and empirically determined quantities, failing to establish a mapping between dynamic information gaps and multiple generators, and overlooking the heterogeneity of generated samples. Moreover, an evaluation and a closed-loop method that jointly considers the information gap and the model performance are lacking. To address these issues, we propose an information gap-guided IoT sensor automatic data augmentation framework (IGADA-IoT) with hierarchical multi-generator collaboration and scheduling over multiple rounds. Capabilities of different generators are jointly utilized to reduce the information gaps. In the IGADA-IoT, a hierarchical multi-generator collaboration and scheduling strategy (HMGCS) is proposed to enhance the targetedness and rationality of generated sample allocation. An information gap-model performance joint evaluation and closed-loop method (IGMP-EC) is proposed to enhance the accuracy of augmentation decisions, and to mitigate the risks of under-augmentation and over-augmentation. Experimental results show that the IGADA-IoT improves the average accuracy of multiple downstream models by 7.27%. Compared with advanced data augmentation methods, the average accuracy is improved by 8.67%. Compared with the individual generators, the average accuracy is improved by 7.24%. Furthermore, public IoT sensor datasets from the UCR Archive and real-world deployments demonstrate the accuracy and generalizability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [189] [A Simple State Space Model Excels at Multivariate Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27406)
*Hassan Saadatmand,Geoffrey I. Webb,Hamid Rezatofighi,Mahsa Salehi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了结构化状态空间模型（SSM）在时间序列分类（TSC）中的应用，发现简单的对角SSM（S4D）在准确率和效率上均优于复杂的Mamba架构；提出的轻量化改进版本MS4和MS4N进一步提升了性能，证明了轻量级结构化SSM是TSC任务的优选方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SSM在序列建模中表现优异，但其在时间序列分类任务中的应用主要局限于复杂的Mamba架构，而对更广泛的SSM设计空间（如对角SSM）缺乏探索。作者旨在探讨是否真的需要Mamba的复杂性才能在TSC任务上获得最优性能。

Method: 1. 首次在大规模TSC基准上系统对比对角SSM（S4D）与输入依赖型SSM（Mamba系列）的性能；
2. 基于S4D提出轻量化改进模型MS4：引入线性输入投影和通道混合机制；
3. 提出MS4N：MS4的归一化变体，以稳定状态动态。

Result: 1. S4D在准确率和效率上均持续优于Mamba系列模型，挑战了"复杂度越高性能越好"的假设；
2. MS4和MS4N在59个数据集（包括大规模MONSTER和UEA基准）上表现优异，准确率超越Mamba模型且更高效；
3. MS4N与参数量约2倍和10倍的竞争深度学习模型性能相当或更优。

Conclusion: 轻量级结构化SSM（如S4D及其改进版MS4/MS4N）是时间序列分类任务的强大替代方案，无需依赖高复杂度的Mamba架构即可实现更优的准确率与效率平衡。这为TSC任务提供了新的模型选择思路，强调了简单有效设计的重要性。

Abstract: Structured state space models (SSMs) have recently emerged as a promising foundation for sequence modeling, with Mamba-based architectures demonstrating strong performance through input-dependent state transitions, albeit at considerable complexity. However, their application to time-series classification (TSC) has been largely limited to Mamba-style architectures, leaving the broader SSM design space underexplored. We present the first systematic study spanning diagonal SSMs (S4D) and input-dependent SSMs (Mamba family) on large-scale TSC benchmarks, asking whether such complexity is necessary for top performance. Our results reveal a surprising finding: S4D consistently outperforms Mamba-based variants in both accuracy and efficiency, challenging the assumption that increased complexity translates to meaningful gains in TSC. Building on this, we introduce MS4, lightweight modifications to S4D via a linear input projection and channel-mixing mechanism, and MS4N, a normalized variant that stabilizes state dynamics with negligible overhead. Evaluated on 59 datasets across MONSTER (up to 60 million samples, 50K timesteps, 82 classes) and the UEA benchmark, against 15 baselines, MS4 and MS4N consistently outperform Mamba-based models while remaining more efficient, and MS4N matches or surpasses competing deep learning models that are roughly 2x and 10x larger in parameters. These results position lightweight structured SSMs as a compelling alternative to scaling complexity for TSC.

</details>


### [190] [$E^3$-Agent: An Executable and Evolving Agent for Resource Management of Edge Generative Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27428)
*Rui Bao,Yaping Sun,Zhiyong Chen,Feng Yang,Meixia Tao,Nan Li,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了$E^3$-Agent，一个用于边缘AI生成内容(AIGC)资源管理的可执行且持续演进的智能体。通过快速路径路由器和慢速路径LLM元控制器的分离架构，在线学习并适应未知且时变的服务时间映射，在动态场景下相比最优静态基线降低65%-73%的平均延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘生成式推理部署面临两个现实挑战：部署时设备-模型性能通常是未知的，且由于用户驱动的语义事件、后台负载和设备流失等因素导致性能非平稳。离线下调优的资源管理器在固定机制下变得脆弱且维护成本高昂。

Method: $E^3$-Agent采用双路径架构：(1)快速路径路由器负责毫秒级调度决策；(2)慢速路径事件驱动的LLM元控制器通过工具接口暴露的小型显式控制面（包括风险门控、路由器配置和快速性能校准）来缓解机制变化。智能体从执行反馈中在线学习，持续适应未知和时变的服务时间映射。

Result: 在基于MLPerf设备-模型测量先验驱动的离散事件模拟器中评估，覆盖冷启动预热和三种动态机制（语义动态、设备流失、隐藏漂移）。在动态场景下，$E^3$-Agent相比最优静态基线降低65%-73%的平均延迟，与在线全信息Oracle的差距控制在7%-10%以内，并在语义降级下有效抑制卡顿率。

Conclusion: $E^3$-Agent通过分离快速调度与慢速适应机制，成功实现了边缘AIGC资源管理在动态环境下的鲁棒性和高效性，证明了事件驱动的LLM元控制器与在线学习相结合的方法能够有效应对边缘部署中的非平稳性和不确定性挑战。

Abstract: Edge deployments of generative inference increasingly face two practical realities: per-device per-model performance is often unknown at deployment time, and it is non-stationary due to user-driven semantic events, background load, and device churn. Consequently, a resource manager that is tuned offline under a fixed regime can become brittle and expensive to maintain. This paper presents $E^3$-Agent, an executable and evolving agent for edge artificial intelligence generated content (AIGC) resource management. $E^3$-Agent separates a fast-path router that makes millisecond-level dispatch decisions from a slow-path, event-driven large language model (LLM) meta-controller that mitigates regime shifts through a small, explicit control surface exposed via a tool interface, including risk gating, router configuration, and rapid performance calibration. The agent learns online from execution feedback and continuously adapts to unknown and time-varying service-time mappings. We evaluate $E^3$-Agent in a discrete-event simulator driven by MLPerf-derived device-model measurement priors, covering cold-start warmup and three dynamic regimes: semantic dynamics, device churn, and hidden drift. Across the dynamic scenarios, $E^3$-Agent reduces average latency by 65%-73% compared to the best static baseline, stays within 7%-10% of an online full-information Oracle used for evaluation, and effectively suppresses stutter rate under semantic degradation.

</details>


### [191] [The Energy Blind Spot: NVIDIA's Flagship Edge AI Hardware Cannot Support Process-Level Energy Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27599)
*Deepak Panigrahy,Aakash Tyagi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对ASUS Ascent GX10（GB10 SoC）的能量观测性审计显示，该平台缺乏关键的能耗监测接口，无法实现进程级能耗归因，阻碍了边缘AI系统的能效优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI工作负载（多步骤编排、工具调用、重试和故障恢复）正部署于边缘设备，但其能耗结构复杂，CPU端处理占总延迟的90.6%和总动态能耗的44%。然而，当前边缘AI平台缺乏能量观测能力，无法进行进程级能耗归因，影响低碳计算的发展。

Method: 对ASUS Ascent GX10（GB10 SoC）平台进行系统能量观测性审计，检查可用的软硬件能耗监测接口，包括CPU能耗计数器、INA功率轨监测器、IPMI/BMC和SCMI powercap协议等，并分析MediaTek固件中未公开的ACPI接口（SPBM）能力。

Result: 该平台未暴露CPU能耗计数器、无INA功率轨监测、无IPMI/BMC支持、无SCMI powercap协议，仅通过NVML提供瞬时GPU功耗。MediaTek固件内部通过未公开的ACPI接口（SPBM）计算各轨能耗，但NVIDIA表示"无计划暴露CPU轨信息"。因此，x86平台通过RAPL实现的进程级能耗归因无法在该平台上通过支持接口复现。

Conclusion: 提出了能量归因AI的硬件需求规范，建议使用外部直流计量结合GPU能耗扣除的校准桥接方案作为临时解决方案，并通过SCMI powercap协议实现标准化路径。呼吁低碳计算社区将能量观测性作为硬件的一级需求。

Abstract: Agentic AI workloads - where a single user goal triggers multi-step orchestration, tool calls, retries, and failure recovery - are being targeted for edge deployment, with NVIDIA, Dell, HP, ASUS, MSI, Acer, and Gigabyte all shipping GB10-based desktop AI systems in 2026. We recently demonstrated that orchestration structure dominates agentic energy cost, with workflows consuming 4.33x more energy per successful goal than linear baselines and OOI reaching 7.63x for multi-step reasoning tasks. Separately, Rajat et al. show that CPU-side processing accounts for up to 90.6% of total latency and 44% of total dynamic energy in agentic workloads. We report a systematic energy-observability audit of the ASUS Ascent GX10 (GB10 SoC) and find that the platform exposes no CPU energy counter, no INA power-rail monitor, no IPMI/BMC, and no SCMI powercap protocol through any supported software interface. The only on-device energy telemetry is instantaneous GPU power via NVML. We further discover that the MediaTek firmware already computes per-rail energy internally via an undocumented ACPI interface (SPBM), but NVIDIA states there are "no plans to expose CPU rail information." On-device per-process energy attribution - as performed on x86 via RAPL - is therefore not reproducible on this platform through supported interfaces. We formalize a hardware requirements specification for energy-attributed AI, propose an interim calibration bridge using external DC metering combined with GPU subtraction, and identify a standards-track path via SCMI powercap. Our findings motivate the low-carbon computing community to demand energy observability as a first-class hardware requirement.

</details>


### [192] [Tackling Multimodal Learning Challenges with Mixture-of-Expert: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27431)
*Liangwei Nathan Zheng,Wei Emma Zhang,Olaf Maennel,Lin Yue,Weitong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本综述系统回顾了混合专家模型（MoE）在多模态学习中的应用，从高效引擎、表示学习器和适配器三个视角阐述了MoE如何解决多模态挑战，并识别了可解释路由、专家通信等关键研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MoE在多模态学习中取得了显著成功，但现有综述往往独立评估多模态学习或MoE，忽视了二者之间的独特交互作用，缺乏对MoE方法如何系统解决多模态挑战的全面综述。本文旨在填补这一空白，回答"MoE如何有效解决多模态挑战"这一核心问题。

Method: 从三个关键视角展开分析：（1）MoE作为高效多模态引擎：通过将计算成本与参数增长解耦以及选择性专家激活来缓解模态冗余；（2）MoE作为多模态表示学习器：整合互补的多意见专家知识以丰富对齐和交互表示；（3）MoE作为多模态适配器：为模态不平衡和缺失模态等不完美数据场景提供模块化、灵活的建模机制。

Result: 通过广泛的文献综述，识别了关键的研究空白，包括可解释路由、专家通信、模态整合和终身多模态学习等方面的问题，为未来研究奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 本综述定位为未来研究的基础，推动向可解释和可持续的多模态混合专家系统发展，为该领域提供了系统性的理论框架和研究方向。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) presents a naturally compatible and scalable framework for multimodal learning, demonstrating strong adaptability across diverse modalities and tasks. Despite its growing success, a comprehensive and systematic review on the MoE metho addressing multimodal challenges remains lacking. Existing surveys tend to evaluate either multimodal learning or MoE independently from method taxonomy, overlooking the unique interplay between them. This survey fills that gap by answering a central question: \textit{How does MoE effectively resolve multimodal challenges?} We approach this from three key perspectives: (1) \textbf{MoE as an Efficient Multimodal Engine:} enabling scalable multimodal modeling by decoupling computational cost from parameter growth and mitigating modality redundancy through selective expert activation; (2) \textbf{MoE as a Multimodal Representation Learner:} integrating complementary multi-opinion expert knowledge to enrich alignment and interaction representations; and (3) \textbf{MoE as a Multimodal Adapter:} providing a modular and flexible mechanism to model imperfect data scenarios such as modality imbalance and missing modality. Through our extensive literature review, we identify critical research gaps, including interpretable routing, expert communication, modality integration, and lifelong multimodal learning. We position this survey as a foundation for future research toward interpretable and sustainable multimodal Mixture-of-Experts system.

</details>


### [193] [Metric-Aware PCA as a Linear Instance of Geometric Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27456)
*Michael Leznik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将度量感知主成分分析（MAPCA）定位为几何深度学习的一个实例，建立了两者间的精确对应关系，并证明了不变主成分分析（IPCA）作为具有特定对称性的唯一线性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何深度学习围绕数据域的对称性组织神经网络架构，对称群的选择作为几何先验决定可学习的表示。本文旨在将MAPCA置于几何深度学习框架中，阐明其几何先验、对称性和等变性，从而建立两者间的理论联系。

Method: 将MAPCA的正定度量矩阵解读为几何先验，其保持的正交群作为诱导的对称群；证明MAPCA解在该群下等变且所得谱不变；建立MAPCA定义约束与等变网络中Schur型权重约束的线性类比；提出唯一性定理，刻画IPCA为MAPCA族中唯一在任意对角重标度下等变且投影到作用不动点集的线性数据导出度量。

Result: 在六个轴（域、对称群、等变性、不变性、架构基元、几何先验）上构建了MAPCA与几何深度学习间的精确字典；证明了IPCA在规范化下等价于精确形式的方差最大化准则；揭示了MAPCA的约束是等变网络Schur型约束的线性对应。

Conclusion: MAPCA可被完整地理解为几何深度学习的一个线性实例，其几何先验通过度量诱导对称性和等变性。通过核PCA（非线性扩展）、图谱方法（图上MAPCA）和深度MAPCA构造三座桥梁，将这一定位拓展至非线性、图和深度等变网络领域。

Abstract: Geometric deep learning organises neural architectures around the symmetries of their data domain, with the choice of symmetry group serving as a geometric prior that determines what representations can be learned. Metric-Aware Principal Component Analysis (MAPCA) parameterises principal component analysis by a positive-definite metric matrix, with a canonical subfamily interpolating between standard PCA and output whitening and a diagonal-metric point recovering Invariant PCA (IPCA). This paper positions MAPCA within the geometric deep learning framework. The metric is read as the geometric prior; the orthogonal group preserving it is the symmetry group it induces; MAPCA solutions are equivariant under this group with the resulting spectrum invariant; and MAPCA's defining constraint is the linear analogue of the Schur-type weight constraints used in equivariant networks. Across six axes - domain, symmetry group, equivariance, invariance, architectural primitive, and geometric prior - we construct a precise dictionary between MAPCA and geometric deep learning. The technical anchor is a uniqueness theorem characterising IPCA as the unique linear data-derived metric in the MAPCA family that is equivariant under arbitrary diagonal rescaling and projects onto the fixed-point set of the action, equivalent under normalisation to the variance-maximisation criterion in its precise form. The paper closes with three bridges: kernel PCA as the nonlinear extension, spectral graph methods as MAPCA on graphs, and a deep MAPCA construction extending the positioning into deep equivariant networks

</details>


### [194] [Comparative Analysis of Liquid Neural Networks and LSTM for Sequential Pattern Recognition: Robustness, Efficiency, and Clinical Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27467)
*Ye Kyaw Thu,Thazin Myint Oo,Thepchai Supnithi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比评估了液体神经网络（LNNs，特别是CfC网络）与传统RNN/LSTM在四种时序模态上的表现，发现LNNs在参数效率和应对数据缺失的鲁棒性方面显著优于传统方法，特别适用于原生时序领域和临床环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RNN和LSTM在离散时间步上运行，难以捕捉真实物理过程中流畅的时间动态特性。为解决此局限性，研究者提出了液体神经网络（LNNs），特别是闭式连续时间（CfC）网络，通过连续微分方程建模隐藏状态演化来更好地处理时序数据。

Method: 研究者在四种不同的序列模态数据集上进行了全面的基准测试：神经形态事件数据（N-MNIST）、笔划绘图数据（QuickDraw）、视觉手写数据（IAM）和生理时间序列数据（PhysioNet Sepsis-3）。同时使用时间丢弃（temporal dropout）进行严格的压力测试，以评估模型在缺失数据情况下的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验结果表明，LNNs在原生时序领域和数据稀疏性普遍存在的临床环境中，始终保持优越的参数效率，并表现出显著更高的鲁棒性。模型能够更好地处理连续时间动态和数据缺失问题。

Conclusion: 液体神经网络作为连续时间模型，在处理具有时间动态特性的真实世界数据时具有明显优势，特别适合应用于数据稀疏的临床环境和需要高参数效率的场景，为时序建模提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) units operate on discrete time steps, often failing to capture the fluid temporal dynamics of real-world physical processes. Liquid Neural Networks (LNNs), specifically Closed-form Continuous-time (CfC) networks, address this by modeling the hidden state evolution as a continuous differential equation. In this paper, we conduct a comprehensive benchmarking study across four distinct sequential modalities: neuromorphic event-based data (N-MNIST), stroke-based drawing (QuickDraw), visual handwriting (IAM), and physiological time-series (PhysioNet Sepsis-3). Furthermore, we perform a rigorous stress test using temporal dropout to evaluate model robustness against missing data. Our findings reveal that LNNs consistently provide superior parameter efficiency and significantly higher robustness in natively temporal domains and clinical environments where data sparsity is prevalent. This extended preprint provides additional background on related datasets and the LNN theoretical lineage, supplemented with a detailed appendix documenting our full implementation and experimental settings.

</details>


### [195] [Heterogeneous Parallelism for Multimodal Large Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27678)
*Yashaswi Karnati,Kamran Jafari,Akash Mehra,Li Ding,Pranav Prashant Thombre,Ali Roshan Ghias,Shifang Xu,Parth Mannan,Yu Yao,Hao Wu,Eric Harper,Ashwath Aithal,Nima Tajbakhsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了异构并行训练方法，允许多模态大语言模型的不同模块使用独立的并行布局，相比同构基线最高提升49.3%的TFLOPS/GPU性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型训练中，编码器与LLM的规模、上下文窗口存在差异。传统的单一LLM中心化并行布局（TP/CP/PP/DP/EP）迫使编码器继承LLM的分片和放置策略，导致额外通信开销、限制编码器并行度或约束LLM调度，在长上下文场景尤为严重。

Method: 提出异构并行训练抽象，允许端到端图中的模块使用独立布局和rank放置，支持共享GPU的同址执行和独立rank集的非同址执行。关键创新是边界通信器（boundary communicators）实现前向/反向布局转换，保持跨布局的边界张量语义，并扩展调度以支持两种放置模式。

Result: 在同址异构配置下，TFLOPS/GPU最高提升49.3%；在非同址异构配置下，聚合token吞吐量最高提升13.0%，TFLOPS/GPU最高提升9.6%。验证了与同构基线的损失收敛一致性。

Conclusion: 异构并行训练有效解耦了多模态模型各模块的并行布局约束，通过布局和放置自由度的提升，在多种多模态工作负载和GPU规模下找到了更优的运行点，系统已作为Megatron-LM开源扩展发布。

Abstract: Foundation model training is becoming multimodal, from post-training pipelines to large-scale pretraining. As modality coverage broadens, context windows grow, and encoder LLM scales diverge, a single LLM-centric TP/CP/PP/DP/EP layout increasingly limits throughput. This coupling forces encoders to inherit LLM-driven sharding and placement choices that can add communication, limit encoder parallelism, or constrain the LLM schedule; the mismatch is most pronounced at long contexts, where LLM context parallelism is needed for the fused multimodal sequence but encoder inputs remain bounded. We present heterogeneous parallelism for multimodal large language model training, an abstraction that lets modules in one end-to-end graph use independent layouts and rank placements, supporting colocated execution on shared GPUs and non-colocated execution on disjoint rank sets. The key challenge is preserving boundary tensor semantics across independent layouts: forward activations must be materialized for the destination layout, while backward gradients must be routed back to the source layout. We address this with boundary communicators that implement forward and backward layout transforms, plus scheduling extensions for both placement modes. We evaluate optimized homogeneous, colocated heterogeneous, and non-colocated heterogeneous configurations across multimodal workloads and GPU scales to characterize when added layout and placement freedom exposes a better operating point. Across this sweep, colocated heterogeneity improves TFLOPS/GPU by up to 49.3%, while non-colocated heterogeneity improves aggregate token throughput by up to 13.0% and TFLOPS/GPU by up to 9.6%. We validate loss convergence parity against homogeneous baselines and release the system as an open-source Megatron-LM extension.

</details>


### [196] [Temporal Hyperbolic Graph Representation Learning for Scale-Free Internet Routing and Delay Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28155)
*Yi-Ling Kuo,Hao-Yu Tien,Shih-Yu Tsai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HERMIT框架，结合双曲时序图神经网络与随机森林回归器，在2015-2024年大规模互联网数据集上实现了RTT预测的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网往返时间（RTT）预测对于路由优化、服务质量保障和流量工程至关重要，但面临长期时间依赖性、路由动态演化和重尾延迟分布等挑战。现有基于欧几里得空间的时序图神经网络（TGNN）难以有效捕捉互联网路由图的层次化和无标度结构。

Method: 提出HERMIT混合框架，结合双曲流形保持时序GNN（基于HMPTGN）与随机森林回归器。该方法引入RTT感知的边特征和可学习边编码器来改进链路状态和路由行为的建模，将生成的双曲节点表示与历史RTT统计信息结合进行延迟预测。

Result: 在2015-2024年大规模互联网数据集上，HERMIT相比仅使用历史RTT统计的强随机森林基线实现6%的RMSE提升，并减少了重尾样本的大误差。在链路预测任务上超越HMPTGN和HTGN等现有双曲TGNN模型。

Conclusion: 结合双曲时序图学习与树回归方法为真实世界互联网拓扑中的RTT预测提供了可扩展的解决方案，证明了双曲几何在捕捉互联网层次化结构方面的优越性。

Abstract: Predicting Internet round-trip time (RTT) is critical for routing optimization, quality-of-service (QoS) provisioning, and traffic engineering, yet remains challenging due to long-term temporal dependencies, evolving routing dynamics, and heavy-tailed latency distributions. While Temporal Graph Neural Networks (TGNNs) can model evolving network topologies, most existing approaches operate in Euclidean space, which poorly captures the hierarchical and scale-free structure of Internet routing graphs. Hyperbolic geometry provides a more suitable representation space.
  We propose HERMIT (Hyperbolic Edge-aware RTT Modeling via Integrated Topology), a hybrid framework combining a hyperbolic manifold-preserving temporal GNN with a Random Forest regressor for joint link prediction and RTT prediction. Built on HMPTGN, HERMIT introduces RTT-aware edge features and a learnable edge encoder to improve modeling of evolving link states and routing behavior. The resulting hyperbolic node representations are combined with historical RTT statistics for robust latency prediction.
  We evaluate HERMIT on a large-scale real Internet dataset spanning 2015-2024. HERMIT consistently outperforms a strong Random Forest baseline using only historical RTT statistics, achieving a 6% RMSE improvement while reducing large errors on heavy-tailed samples. It also surpasses prior hyperbolic TGNN models, including HMPTGN and HTGN, in link prediction performance. These results demonstrate that combining hyperbolic temporal graph learning with tree-based regression provides a scalable solution for RTT prediction in real-world Internet topologies.

</details>


### [197] [Architecture-driven Shift: towards a lightweight selector for capturing the trends of logit shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27469)
*Zhong Ye,Yu Hu,Ruilin Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出架构驱动偏移(ADS)作为轻量级代理指标,用于高效选择持续学习预训练模型,无需大量计算即可预测逻辑偏移,在175种架构上实现了强单调相关性(最低Spearman's r_s=0.731)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习需要在预训练模型中选择具有良好"可塑性-稳定性"平衡的模型,但直接计算逻辑偏移(logit shift)成本高昂。现有理论分析假设均匀隐藏层宽度,忽略了现实架构的结构异质性(可变宽度和深度),无法提供高效替代方案。因此需要建立异构架构与逻辑偏移之间的理论关系。

Method: 将逻辑偏移解耦为架构依赖和数据依赖,提出架构驱动偏移(ADS)框架。ADS基于三个机制组件推导:(1)权重矩阵梯度的谱范数随层宽度的缩放;(2)新任务的优化路径长度;(3)宽网络中的渐近任务冲突。该框架仅需少量数据样本即可计算。

Result: 在175种多样化架构上的广泛实验表明,ADS与逻辑偏移之间存在强单调相关性(最弱Spearman's r_s=0.731)。在三个数据集和六个场景中,ADS可作为期望校准误差(ECE)的轻量级代理,而ECE是可靠持续学习模型选择的广泛使用指标。

Conclusion: ADS框架成功建立了异构架构与逻辑偏移之间的理论联系,提供了一种高效的持续学习模型选择方法,能够预测模型在持续学习场景中的"可塑性-稳定性"平衡能力,为大规模模型选择提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Continual Learning (CL) is a practical paradigm to utilize power of deep pre-trained neural networks, but which pre-trained model has a better ability to balance ``Plasticity-Stability", deserving to be chosen? The logit shift serves as a natural proxy because it represents the logit shift in CL scenarios. However, obtaining the logit shift requires huge computational cost, which hinders large-scale model selection. Existing theoretical analyses fail to offer an efficient alternative because of the assumption of uniform hidden layer widths, which ignores the structural heterogeneity (variable width and depth) of real-world architectures. This raises a critical question: what theoretically relationship can be identified between heterogeneous architecture and logit shift on prior tasks (that the model has been trained on)? To answer the question, we decouple logit shift into architecture dependency and data dependency to establish our framework, which reveals that the combination of two dependency, defined as Architecture-driven Shift (ADS), that can capture the logit shift tendency well computable with few data samples. Specifically, for a well-optimized model on prior tasks, higher ADS is associated with a larger logit shift after training on the current task, which derived based on three mechanistic components: (1) spectral norm scaling of weight matrix gradients with layer width, (2) the optimization path length of the new task, and (3) the asymptotic task conflict in wide networks. Extensive empirical results across more than 175 diverse architectures demonstrate a strong monotonic correlation (the weakest Spearman's $r_s=0.731$) between ADS and logit shift. Practically, we demonstrate that ADS can serve as a lightweight proxy of the expected calibration error, which is a widely used metric for reliable CL model selection, on three datasets across six scenarios.

</details>


### [198] [Detect by Yourself: Self-Designing Agentic Workflows for Few-Shot Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27470)
*Tairan Huang,Qiang Chen,Yili Wang,Yueyue Ma,Changlong He,Xiu Su,Yi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SignGAD框架，将图异常检测任务从训练固定检测器重新表述为设计任务条件的检测工作流，通过自主选择图编码和检测器设计来利用任务特定的异常证据，在少样本设置下实现了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图异常检测方法面临两个关键挑战：(1)固定流水线限制了其在有限监督下对不同图任务的适应性；(2)弱证据阻止了其明确地将上下文和结构异常信号纳入检测过程中。

Method: 提出了一个新颖的范式，将图异常检测任务从训练固定异常检测器重新表述为设计任务条件的检测工作流。SignGAD通过构建检测工作流，选择合适的图编码和检测器设计来利用任务特定的异常证据。同时，引入了一种受保护的最终重拟合策略，通过校准重拟合接受度来优化所选工作流，增强在有限监督下的可靠性。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上进行的广泛实验表明，SignGAD在与最先进方法的对比中取得了强大的性能，突显了其在图异常检测任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: SignGAD框架通过自设计智能体工作流成功解决了图异常检测中的固定流水线和弱证据问题，为少样本图异常检测提供了一种有效的新范式。

Abstract: Graph anomaly detection aims to identify anomaly nodes in attributed graphs and plays an important role in real-world applications. However, existing graph anomaly detection methods still face two key challenges: 1) fixed pipelines, which restrict their adaptability across different graph tasks under limited supervision; 2) weak evidence, which prevents them from explicitly incorporating contextual and structural anomaly signals into the detection process. In this paper, we propose a novel framework, self-designing agentic workflows for few-shot graph anomaly detection (SignGAD). Specifically, we propose a novel paradigm that reformulates graph anomaly detection task from training a fixed anomaly detector to designing task-conditioned detection workflows. By constructing detection workflows, SignGAD selects suitable graph encodings and detector designs to exploit task-specific anomaly evidence. Meanwhile, we introduce a guarded final refit strategy to refine the selected workflow by calibrating refit acceptance, enhancing reliability under limited supervision. Extensive experiments conducted on several real-world datasets demonstrate that SignGAD achieves strong performance against state-of-the-art methods, highlighting its effectiveness on graph anomaly detection tasks.

</details>


### [199] [How Far Can Disaggregation Go? A Design-Space Exploration of Attention-FFN Disaggregation for Efficient MoE LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28302)
*Hanjiang Wu,Abhimanyu Rajeshkumar Bambhaniya,Sarbartha Banerjee,Tuhin Khare,Sudarshan Srinivasan,Suvinay Subramanian,Souvik Kundu,Madhu Kumar,Midhilesh Elavazhagan,William Won,Amir Yazdanbakhsh,Tushar Krishna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了MoE大模型推理中不同 disaggregation 层级（chunked-prefill、P/D disaggregation、AFD）的性能权衡。在严格TTFT/TPOT约束下，AFD在DeepSeek-V3.2上实现了约4k tokens/s的系统吞吐量，而其他方案不可行，为分布式AI基础设施设计提供了重要指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型推理逐步 disaggregate 以应对模型规模增长和严格的TTFT/TPOT服务目标，从chunked-prefill到P/D disaggregation，再到operator级别的Attention-FFN Disaggregation (AFD)。特别是对于MoE模型，memory-bound的attention、compute-intensive的expert FFNs以及MoE dispatch/combine通信产生了不同的资源需求。每个disaggregation层级都加深了跨工作负载特征、资源分配和互连拓扑的调度设计空间，核心问题是：每个层级何时真正有效？

Method: 系统性地刻画了MoE推理中disaggregation的权衡关系，覆盖真实工作负载（包括输入/输出序列长度、prefix-KV重用、每用户延迟约束）。以chunked-prefill和P/D disaggregation为基准，通过融合设备端kernel测量和高保真网络仿真的框架，研究AFD在规模场景下的收益和限制。

Result: 在严格的TTFT/TPOT SLO约束下，AFD在DeepSeek-V3.2上跨聊天、编码和代理编码工作负载维持约4k tokens/s的系统吞吐量，而此时非AFD部署方案不可行。研究揭示了如何根据工作负载和模型架构跨GPU划分attention和FFN。

Conclusion: 提炼了联合优化吞吐量和交互性的具体要点，包括如何根据工作负载和模型架构在GPU间划分attention和FFN，为当前机架和集群级部署以及未来disaggregated AI基础设施提供了设计原则。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) inference has progressively disaggregated to keep pace with growing model sizes and tight TTFT and TPOT service-level objectives: from chunked-prefill aggregation, to prefill-decode (P/D) disaggregation, and most recently to operator-level Attention-FFN Disaggregation (AFD). This trend is especially important for mixture-of-experts (MoE) models, where memory-bound attention, compute-intensive expert FFNs, and MoE dispatch/combine communication create distinct resource demands. AFD further exposes this heterogeneity by placing attention and MoE-FFN execution on separate GPU groups. Each level of disaggregation deepens the scheduling design space across workload characteristics, resource allocation, and interconnect topology, raising the central question: when does each level actually pay off? We systematically characterize this trade-off for MoE inference across realistic workloads spanning input/output sequence lengths, prefix-KV reuse, and per-user latency constraints. Using chunked-prefill and P/D disaggregation as baselines, we study the benefits and limits of AFD at scale through a framework that fuses on-device kernel measurements with high-fidelity network simulation. Under strict TTFT/TPOT SLOs, AFD sustains around 4k tokens/s of system throughput on DeepSeek-V3.2 across chat, coding, and agentic-coding workloads, where non-AFD deployments are infeasible. We distill concrete takeaways for jointly optimizing throughput and interactivity, including how to partition attention and FFN across GPUs as a function of workload and model architecture, providing design principles for current rack- and cluster-scale deployments as well as future disaggregated AI infrastructure.

</details>


### [200] [HEAL: Resilient and Self-* Hub-based Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27475)
*Mohamed Amine Legheraba,Stefan Galkiewicz,Maria Gradinariu Potop-Butucaru,Sébastien Tixeuil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HEAL是一种新型跨层去中心化学习框架，结合了联邦学习、Gossip学习和Epidemic学习的优势，通过优化的自组织和自愈P2P覆盖层实现动态聚合器节点选择，在无崩溃环境下性能接近联邦学习，在故障环境中优于Gossip和Epidemic学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽然流行但存在中心化聚合器的固有缺陷，包括服务器漏洞、可扩展性问题、隐私风险以及单点故障；而完全去中心化的Gossip学习和Epidemic学习虽然解决了中心化问题，但面临模型收敛速度慢的挑战。需要一种既能保持去中心化优势又能实现高效收敛的学习框架。

Method: 提出HEAL框架，这是首个跨层去中心化学习框架，采用优化的自组织和自愈底层P2P覆盖层；利用Elevator算法动态选择节点作为聚合器，结合了联邦学习的聚合机制和去中心化P2P网络的容错特性。

Result: 在无崩溃环境下，HEAL的性能与联邦学习相当；在存在崩溃和节点流失的环境中，HEAL的表现优于Gossip学习和Epidemic学习，同时保持了完全去中心化和容错性。

Conclusion: HEAL成功实现了联邦学习的高效性能与去中心化系统的容错特性的结合，为去中心化学习提供了一个既高效又鲁棒的解决方案，有效解决了现有方法在性能与去中心化程度之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Decentralized learning enhances privacy, scalability, and fault tolerance by distributing data and computation across nodes. A popular approach is Federated learning, which relies on a central aggregator, yet faces challenges such as server vulnerabilities, scalability issues, privacy risks and most importantly, the single point of failure. Alternatively Gossip Learning and Epidemic Learning offer fully decentralization through peer-to-peer exchanges of model updates, ensuring robustness and privacy, at the price of slower model convergence. In this work, we introduce a novel decentralized learning framework called HEAL. HEAL is the first cross-layer decentralized learning framework that exploits an optimized self-organizing and self-healing underlying P2P overlay combining the strengths of Federated Learning, Gossip and Epidemic Learning. Leveraging the recently proposed Elevator algorithm, HEAL promotes dynamically chosen nodes to act as aggregators. Through simulations, we demonstrate that HEAL has similar performances to that of Federated Learning in crash-free settings, while being fully decentralized and fault-tolerant. In crash and churn prone environments HEAL outperforms Gossip and Epidemic Learning.

</details>


### [201] [Balancing Fidelity and Diversity in Diffusion Models via Symmetric Attention Decomposition: Hopfield Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27476)
*Hyunmin Cho,Woo Kyoung Han,Kyong Hwan Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将Transformer中的注意力矩阵解释为联想记忆矩阵，通过对称-反对称分解揭示了对称分量控制能量景观结构、反对称分量驱动景观上的环流，并提出了基于Hopfield稳定性度量的生成质量-多样性权衡调控方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer中注意力机制的内在工作机制，特别是pre-softmax注意力矩阵QK^⊤如何编码输入特征间的成对关联，以及这种编码如何影响模型的生成特性。

Method: 将注意力矩阵QK^⊤分解为对称分量和反对称分量：对称分量被解释为控制能量景观的结构，反对称分量被解释为驱动景观上的环流动动力学。基于对称分量诱导的能量形式，推导Hopfield风格的稳定性度量来量化检索特征的稳定性。

Result: 发现Hopfield风格稳定性度量与生成中的保真度-多样性权衡之间存在有意义的相关性；提出通过修改底层动力学的环流来调节这一权衡的可控旋钮。

Conclusion: 注意力机制可以理解为联想记忆系统，其对称和反对称分量分别负责能量景观的结构和动力学环流，这为理解和控制生成模型的保真度-多样性权衡提供了新的理论框架和实用工具。

Abstract: We characterize the pre-softmax attention matrix $\mathbf{QK^\top}$ in transformers as an associative memory matrix encoding pairwise associations between input features. By decomposing this matrix into its symmetric and skew-symmetric parts, we interpret the symmetric component as governing the structure of the energy landscape, and the skew-symmetric component as driving circulation on that landscape. Leveraging the energy formulation induced by the symmetric component, we derive Hopfield-style stability measures that quantify the stability of retrieved features. We observe meaningful correlations between Hopfield-style stability measures and the fidelity-diversity trade-offs in generation. Finally, we propose a controllable knob to modulate this trade-off by modifying the circulation of the underlying dynamics. Code is available at our GitHub (https://github.com/hyeon-cho/Attention-Symmetric-Decomposition).

</details>


### [202] [Resource-Constrained Affect Modelling via Variance Regularisation Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27479)
*Kosmas Pinitas,Konstantinos Katsifis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种方差正则化剪枝框架(VR)，在模型压缩过程中显式考虑跨参与者稳定性，在80%稀疏度下仍保持竞争性性能，适用于资源受限的情感计算系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型剪枝方法仅优化稀疏性，未考虑参数移除对不同用户间鲁棒性的影响。在情感计算系统中，需要在计算效率与跨用户可靠性之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出方差正则化剪枝(VR)框架，不再仅依赖平均预测误差，而是评估每个连接对预测准确性和跨用户变异性的联合贡献，优先保留在分布差异下保持可靠的参数。在AGAIN数据集上进行验证，该数据集包含九个情感诱导游戏环境的唤醒度标注。

Result: 实验结果表明，VR方法在80%稀疏度下且无需额外微调即可保持竞争性的谐和相关系数(CCC)性能，证明了其在实际部署中的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架支持开发紧凑、鲁棒的情感模型，能够在实际交互环境中可靠运行，特别适用于资源受限的情感感知系统部署。

Abstract: Affective computing systems are increasingly embedded in pervasive and interactive environments, such as adaptive games, assistive technologies, and resource-constrained platforms, where computational efficiency must be balanced with reliability across diverse users. Model pruning offers an effective way to reduce computational demands, yet existing approaches typically optimise for sparsity alone, without accounting for how parameter removal impacts robustness across individuals. In this work, we introduce Variance-Regularised Pruning (VR), a pruning framework that explicitly incorporates cross-participant stability into the sparsification process. Rather than relying solely on average prediction error, VR evaluates each connection based on its joint contribution to both prediction accuracy and variability across users, prioritising parameters that remain reliable under distributional differences. We evaluate the proposed approach on the AGAIN dataset, which includes arousal annotations collected across nine affect-eliciting game environments. Experimental results demonstrate that VR maintains competitive Concordance Correlation Coefficient (CCC) performance even at 80\% sparsity without additional fine-tuning, highlighting its suitability for deployment in real-world, resource-limited affect-aware systems. Overall, the proposed framework supports the development of compact, robust affective models that can operate reliably in real-world interactive environments.

</details>


### [203] [Energy-Structured Low-Rank Adaptation for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27482)
*Longhua Li,Lei Qi,Qi Tian,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出E²-LoRA方法，通过将知识集中并有序排列到低秩适应的前导秩中，解决了正交子空间方法中的能量扩散问题，从而释放模型容量并提升持续学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的正交子空间方法在缓解持续学习中的任务干扰时，常面临能量在基上扩散的问题，这阻碍了知识的紧凑存储并耗尽了对未来任务的适应容量。作者观察到参数更新引起的输出特征漂移本质上是低秩的，并证明了沿此漂移的主方向保留参数可最小化输出重建误差。

Method: 提出E²-LoRA（能量集中与能量有序的低秩适应）方法。该方法显式地将知识有序地集中到低秩分解的前导秩中，从而为后续任务释放容量。此外，设计了动态秩分配策略，通过联合优化能量保留与模型可塑性来平衡稳定性与可塑性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，E²-LoRA实现了当前最优（state-of-the-art）的性能表现。

Conclusion: E²-LoRA通过能量集中与有序的低秩适应机制，有效解决了正交子空间方法中的能量扩散问题，在持续学习场景下实现了稳定性与可塑性的良好平衡，显著提升了模型性能。

Abstract: While orthogonal subspace methods try to mitigate task interference in Continual Learning (CL), they often suffer from energy diffusion across the basis, hindering knowledge compaction and exhausting capacity for future tasks. We observe that output feature drift induced by parameter updates is inherently low-rank, and theoretically prove that preserving parameters along the principal directions of this drift minimizes the output reconstruction error. Motivated by this, we propose \textbf{E}nergy-Concentrated and \textbf{E}nergy-Ordered \textbf{Lo}w-\textbf{R}ank \textbf{A}daptation (E$^2$-LoRA). By explicitly ordering and concentrating knowledge into leading ranks, E$^2$-LoRA frees capacity for subsequent tasks. Furthermore, we design a dynamic rank allocation strategy to balance stability and plasticity by jointly optimizing energy retention and model plasticity. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that E$^2$-LoRA achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [204] [Federated Learning for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection in Industrial Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27486)
*Khayyam Nosrati,Martin Uray,Saverio Messineo,Olaf Sassnick,Stefan Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习场景下的多变量时间序列异常检测，提出一个新的数据集来解决现有数据集的不足，并评估了相关异常检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习场景下的多变量时间序列异常检测基准测试面临数据方面的挑战：现有数据集无法同时提供足够规模、准确标签和无常见缺陷的数据。此外，离散工业自动化中常见的周期性过程行为在当前多变量时间序列异常检测研究中仍被忽视。

Method: 构建了一个反映离散自动化过程重复特性所产生周期性动态的数据集，并在该数据集和公共基准数据集上评估了选定的多变量时间序列异常检测方法。

Result: 本研究为文献提供了新的见解，填补了现有数据集的空白，并对多变量时间序列异常检测方法进行了系统性评估。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据集能够有效支持联邦学习场景下的多变量时间序列异常检测研究，为周期性过程行为的异常检测提供了新的研究基础。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) has broadened the horizon for multivariate time series anomaly detection (MTSAD). However, benchmarking such anomaly detection methods within FL paradigm poses data-centric challenges. The existing datasets do not counteract these challenges since they do not simultaneously provide sufficient scale, accurate labels, and freedom from common flaws. In addition, the role of cyclic process behavior, which is common in discrete industrial automation, remains underexplored for MTSAD for the current state of research. This paper aims to shed more light on the literature and address these gaps by introducing a dataset designed with cyclic dynamics arising from the repetitive nature of discrete automation processes and evaluates selected MTSAD methods on both the proposed dataset and a public benchmark dataset.

</details>


### [205] [GenSBI: Generative Methods for Simulation-Based Inference in JAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27499)
*Aurelio Amerio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了GenSBI，一个完全基于JAX实现的开源库，用于模拟推断(SBI)中的流匹配、分数匹配和去噪扩散生成模型，在标准基准测试上达到了接近理想的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前主流的模拟推断(SBI)库都基于PyTorch，而使用JAX开发前向模型和分析流程的研究人员缺乏原生支持的解决方案。流和扩散生成模型已成为SBI中广泛采用的密度估计器，但缺乏JAX生态系统的原生实现。

Method: 开发了GenSBI库，完全在JAX中实现了流匹配、分数匹配和去噪扩散。提供了三种基于transformer的架构：SimFormer、Flux1和新型Flux1Joint（将门控调制transformer块扩展到联合密度估计）。采用统一接口解耦生成方法、神经骨干网络和推断模式，支持从训练到后验收准（SBC、TARP、LC2ST）的端到端工作流程。

Result: 在标准SBI基准测试上验证了框架性能，在SBIBM任务上实现了接近理想的平均C2ST分数（0.50-0.56，其中0.50为理想值），仅需最少的每个任务调优，且在所有测试配置中都具有良好的后验覆盖率校准。

Conclusion: GenSBI成功填补了JAX生态系统中SBI工具的空白，提供了一个模块化、可扩展且性能优异的开源解决方案，支持自定义架构和特定领域的嵌入网络，代码已在GitHub公开发布。

Abstract: Flow and diffusion generative models have established themselves as widely adopted density estimators for simulation-based inference (SBI), extending naturally from neural posterior estimation to likelihood and joint density estimation. Their principled optimization objectives and freedom from architectural constraints have driven rapid adoption across the natural sciences. Yet the most widely used SBI libraries remain PyTorch-based, leaving researchers who develop their forward models and analysis pipelines in JAX without a native option. We present GenSBI, an open-source library that implements flow matching, score matching, and denoising diffusion entirely in JAX. The library offers three transformer-based architectures - SimFormer, Flux1, and a novel Flux1Joint that extends gate-modulated transformer blocks to joint density estimation - all interchangeable through a unified interface that decouples generative method, neural backbone, and inference mode. GenSBI provides an end-to-end workflow from training through posterior calibration (SBC, TARP, LC2ST) and supports custom architectures with domain-specific embedding networks. We validate the framework on standard SBI benchmarks, achieving near-ideal mean C2ST scores (0.50-0.56, where 0.50 is ideal) on SBIBM tasks with minimal per-task tuning and well-calibrated posterior coverage across all tested configurations. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/aurelio-amerio/GenSBI.

</details>


### [206] [SparseOpt: Addressing Normalization-induced Gradient Skew in Sparse Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27541)
*Mohammed Adnan,Rohan Jain,Tom Jacobs,Ekansh Sharma,Rahul G. Krishnan,Rebekka Burkholz,Yani Ioannou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了批归一化对动态稀疏训练的负面影响，并提出稀疏感知优化器SparseOpt，在ResNet模型上实现了更快的收敛速度和更好的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态稀疏训练方法虽然能够减少计算量，但其收敛速度明显慢于稠密训练，往往需要相当的训练时间才能达到相似的精度。作者旨在解决这一限制，使动态稀疏训练在实际应用中具有竞争力。

Method: 通过分析和实验证明批归一化(BN)对稀疏训练的不利影响，提出了稀疏感知优化器SparseOpt来解决这个问题。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet数据集上的ResNet模型实验表明，所提出的方法能够持续实现更快的收敛速度和更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 本研究首次系统地分析了批归一化、稀疏层和动态稀疏训练之间的相互作用，揭示了当前归一化层在稀疏训练中的局限性，为使动态稀疏训练在实际应用中与稠密训练具有竞争力迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Dynamic Sparse Training (DST) methods train neural networks by maintaining sparsity while dynamically adapting the network topology. Despite the promise of reduced computation, DST methods converge significantly slower than dense training, often requiring comparable training time to achieve similar accuracy. We demonstrate both analytically and empirically that Batch Normalization (BN) adversely affects sparse training, and propose SparseOpt, a sparsity-aware optimizer, to address this. Experiments on ResNet models across CIFAR-100 and ImageNet demonstrate consistently faster convergence and improved generalization with our proposed method. Our work highlights the limitations of current normalization layers in sparse training and provides the first systematic study of the interaction between Batch Normalization, sparse layers, and DST, taking a significant step toward making DST practically competitive with dense training.

</details>


### [207] [The Fundamental Limits of Fraud Detection in Card Payment Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27557)
*Gaurav Dhama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文认为支付生态系统的结构性信息缺陷（延迟、删失、损坏、反事实缺失反馈）是欺诈检测改进缓慢的主因，理论上证明改善报告质量比增加模型复杂度更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统将卡片支付欺诈检测建模为监督分类问题的方法改进缓慢，作者认为问题根源不在于函数逼近或优化，而是支付生态系统中固有的结构性信息缺陷影响了学习效果。

Method: 将卡片授权形式化为带有延迟、删失、损坏和反事实缺失反馈的序贯决策问题，推导出极小极大遗憾下界，分析信息缺陷对可学习性的影响。

Result: 推导的下界显示信息缺陷以乘积方式进入可达到学习率的分母；改善发行方报告质量或减少删失比增加模型复杂度更能降低遗憾下限；发行方间的异质性使学习难度超出平均缺陷率所暗示的程度。

Conclusion: 支付网络中的欺诈检测本质上比标准在线学习设置更难，生态系统信息质量是关键瓶颈；理论为优先投资报告基础设施、争议流程质量和选择性探索提供了依据。

Abstract: Card payment fraud detection is usually framed as a supervised classification problem. Although this approach has generated practical progress, improvement has remained incremental despite major advances in model architecture. We argue that this is not mainly a failure of function approximation or optimization, but a consequence of structural information impairments inherent to the payment ecosystem.
  We formalize card authorization as a sequential decision problem with delayed, censored, corrupted, and counterfactually missing feedback. We derive a minimax regret lower bound showing that these impairments enter multiplicatively in the denominator of the achievable learning rate. The bound implies that improving issuer reporting quality or reducing censorship can yield larger reductions in the regret floor than increasing model complexity. We also show that heterogeneity across issuers worsens learnability beyond what average impairment rates suggest.
  The paper contributes a theory of why fraud detection in payment networks is fundamentally harder than in standard online learning settings, identifies ecosystem information quality as the key bottleneck, and provides a theoretical basis for prioritizing investments in reporting infrastructure, dispute process quality, and selective exploration. The paper is theory-first and does not rely on proprietary transaction data.

</details>


### [208] [Information-theoretic Multimodal Representation Learning for Electrocardiogram Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27583)
*Phu X. Nguyen,Konstantinos Kontras,Wei Dai,Huy Phan,Christos Chatzichristos,Paul Pu Liang,Bert Vandenberk,Maarten De Vos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MERIT框架，通过信息论视角联合保留信号结构和整合临床语义，在ECG分类和零样本评估中显著优于现有方法，并提升了ECG条件临床文本生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态方法通过ECG信号与临床报告对齐来引入诊断语义，但临床报告往往无法保留ECG波形的丰富生理结构，尤其在从粗粒度诊断类别到细粒度形态的多个抽象层次上存在不足。

Method: 从信息论视角构建ECG表示学习目标，提出MERIT双分支预训练框架，结合掩码ECG建模(masked ECG modeling)与ECG-文本对比对齐(ECG-text contrastive alignment)，联合保留信号结构并整合临床语义。

Result: 在PTB-XL数据集上，PTB-XL All分类F1提升超过3%，SubClass分类F1提升超过5%；零样本评估中，PTB-XL SubClass的AUC提升达2.66%，F1提升达2.11%；在ECG条件临床文本生成任务中，ROUGE和METEOR等多个指标均有提升。

Conclusion: MERIT学习到更具信息量和临床意义的ECG表示，尤其在细粒度临床应用中表现突出，同时展现出在分布偏移场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Electrocardiograms (ECGs) are widely used non-invasive measurements of cardiac activity and play a central role in clinical diagnosis. Recent multimodal approaches align ECG signals with clinical reports to incorporate diagnostic semantics, but clinical reports often fail to preserve the rich physiological structure of ECG waveforms, particularly across multiple levels of abstraction ranging from coarse diagnostic categories to fine-grained morphology. To address this limitation, we formulate ECG representation learning from an information-theoretic perspective and derive a tractable objective that jointly preserves signal structure and integrates clinical semantics. Based on this principle, we propose \textbf{MERIT} (Multimodal ECG Representation via Information Theory), a dual-branch pretraining framework combining masked ECG modeling with ECG--text contrastive alignment. Extensive experiments on PTB-XL and additional benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over prior methods, including gains exceeding $3%$ F1 on PTB-XL All and $5%$ F1 on SubClass classification. In zero-shot evaluation, MERIT further improves performance by up to $ +2.66\%$ AUC and $ +2.11\%$ F1 on PTB-XL SubClass, while also demonstrating robustness under multiple distribution-shift settings. Moreover, leveraging the learned ECG representations for ECG-conditioned clinical text generation with large language models improves text quality across several metrics, including ROUGE and METEOR. Together, these results demonstrate that MERIT learns more informative and clinically meaningful ECG representations, particularly for fine-grained clinical applications.

</details>


### [209] [Gradient Transformer: Learning to Generate Updates for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27591)
*Binh-Nguyen Nguyen,Khang Tran,NhatHai Phan,Issa Khalil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Grad-Transformer框架，通过将私有数据微调的TinyLM更新向量转换为LLM更新向量，实现无需访问私有数据即可更新LLM，解决了组织缺乏计算资源微调大模型的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多组织缺乏计算资源在私有数据上微调大语言模型，而单独微调小语言模型性能较差，存在计算资源与数据隐私的双重约束。

Method: 提出数据无关的知识蒸馏框架，核心是Gradient Transformer模块，将TinyLM的参数更新向量转换为LLM的更新向量。该转换器从影子数据集中学习TinyLM与LLM更新向量之间的相关性，第三方可在不访问私有数据的情况下生成LLM更新向量，支持多组织协作更新。

Result: 在语言建模和推理任务上的大量实验表明，Grad-Transformer显著优于现有知识蒸馏基线方法，即使在严格的差分隐私保护下也能保持优异性能。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了数据无关的LLM更新，在保护数据隐私的同时提升了性能和成本效率，为资源受限组织提供了可行的LLM定制化方案。

Abstract: Many organizations lack computational resources to fine-tune large language models (LLMs) on private (unshareable) data for better utility, while fine-tuning tiny language models (TinyLMs) alone performs poorly. To address this bottleneck, we propose a data-free knowledge distillation framework that generates LLM update vectors based on TinyLMs fine-tuned on private data. An update vector is a vector of parameter changes from an initial model to its fine-tuned version on a dataset, capturing the effect of cumulative gradient steps during fine-tuning. The key idea of our framework is a novel Gradient Transformer that transforms TinyLM's update vectors into LLM's update vectors. As derived from shadow datasets, Grad-Transformer captures the correlation between TinyLM and LLM update vectors, enabling third-party providers to generate LLM update vectors given the organization's TinyLM update vectors without accessing the organization's private data. The framework supports multi-organization collaboration to jointly update LLMs, improving performance and cost-efficiency. Extensive experiments across language modeling and reasoning tasks show that Grad-Transformer remarkably outperforms state-of-the-art knowledge distillation baselines, even under strict differential privacy protection.

</details>


### [210] [Evaluating Local Explainability Metrics for Machine Learning Models on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27618)
*Tomás Pereira,João Vitorino,Eva Maia,Isabel Praça*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了复杂表格数据分类任务中局部可解释性技术（LIME、SHAP、特征消融）的可信度问题。通过对32个数据集和多种机器学习模型的基准测试，发现解释质量与模型预测性能并不总是相关，数据集复杂度和特征分布才是影响解释质量和可靠性的主要因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管可解释性技术被广泛用于理解AI行为，但生成的解释并不总是可靠的。解释可能对人类看起来合理，但未能捕捉模型的内部推理过程，特别是在处理复杂表格数据时。因此需要系统评估局部可解释性技术的可信度。

Method: 针对三种主要可解释性属性进行评估：对模型预测的忠实性、对输入数据变化的鲁棒性、以及解释本身的复杂性。对LIME、Kernel SHAP和特征消融三种技术进行基准测试，覆盖32个数据集和不同类型的机器学习模型。分析模型性能范围，识别两组样本：共识正确样本（所有模型都预测正确）和共识错误样本（所有模型都预测错误）。

Result: 实验结果表明，解释并不总是与模型的预测性能相关。相反，数据集复杂度和特征分布似乎是影响解释质量和可靠性的主要因素。研究揭示了可解释性技术在复杂表格数据上的局限性。

Conclusion: 可解释性技术的可信度受到数据集内在特性的显著影响。在使用局部可解释性方法时，需要谨慎评估其在特定数据集上的适用性，不能仅凭解释的表面合理性来判断其可靠性。数据集复杂度和特征分布应作为选择可解释性技术的重要考量因素。
