Abstract: Despite the wide use of explainability techniques to attempt to understand the behavior of Artificial Intelligence (AI), the generated explanations may not always be reliable. An explanation can appear plausible to humans but fail to capture the internal reasoning of a model, particularly when dealing with complex tabular data. This paper studies the trustworthiness of local explainability techniques when applied to complex tabular classification tasks, considering evaluated metrics for three main properties: faithfulness to the model's predictions, robustness to input data variations, and complexity of the explanation itself. A benchmark was performed for Local Interpretable Model-Agnostic Explanations (LIME), Kernel SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP), and Feature Ablation techniques, across 32 datasets and different types of machine learning models. Model performance ranges were analyzed to identify two groups: consensus-correct, which are samples that all models predicted correctly, and consensus-wrong, samples that all models predicted incorrectly. The obtained results demonstrate that that the explanations are not always correlated with a model's predictive performance. Instead, dataset complexity and feature distributions seem to be the main factors affecting explanation quality and reliability.

</details>


### [211] [Supervised Distributional Reduction via Optimal Transport and Dependence Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27619)
*Sai-Aakash Ramesh,Archit Sood,Andrew Corbett,Tim Dodwell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了监督分布约简（SDR）算法，通过融合最优传输与依赖最大化，学习同时捕获数据几何结构和任务相关监督信息的紧凑表示，并将其应用于高斯过程的非平稳核设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布约简方法（联合聚类与降维）在监督学习场景下研究不足，难以在学习表示时同时平衡数据压缩与保持预测相关性，缺乏能够显式捕获任务相关信号的方法。

Method: 提出监督分布约简（SDR）算法：基于Fused Gromov-Wasserstein (FGW)目标函数对齐输入分布与代表点的关系结构，并引入直接依赖项最大化学习嵌入中的预测信号；进一步将其扩展到高斯过程建模，通过目标感知的分布对齐重新定义距离，构建自适应非平稳核。

Result: SDR能够生成同时反映几何结构与监督信息的紧凑表示；在表示学习之外，SDR自然诱导出数据依赖的非平稳几何，能够构建对数据几何与监督局部变化敏感的自适应核。

Conclusion: SDR为监督分布约简提供了最优传输视角的解决方案，成功桥接了表示学习与高斯过程建模，为非平稳核设计提供了新的理论框架与实践方法。

Abstract: Learning representations that capture both intrinsic data geometry and target-relevant structure remains a fundamental challenge, particularly in settings where data reduction must balance compression with predictive fidelity. While distributional reduction-encompassing joint clustering and dimensionality reduction-offers a principled way to summarize data, its supervised variants remain relatively under-explored, despite the importance of retaining task-relevant signal for downstream prediction and decision-making. We propose Supervised Distributional Reduction (SDR), an algorithm for learning target-aware representations by combining optimal transport with explicit dependence maximization. SDR builds on the Fused Gromov-Wasserstein (FGW) objective to align the relational structure of the input distribution with a set of representative points, while augmenting it with a direct dependence term that encourages the learned embeddings to capture predictive signal more explicitly. This results in compact representations that reflect both geometric structure and supervision. Beyond representation learning, SDR naturally induces a data-dependent, non-stationary geometry that can be leveraged for settings such as Gaussian Process (GP) modelling. By redefining distances through target-aware distributional alignment, SDR enables the construction of adaptive kernels that respond to local variations in both data geometry and supervision, offering an optimal transport-based perspective on non-stationary kernel design.

</details>


### [212] [Hurwitz Quaternion Multiplicative Quantization for KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27646)
*Kabir Swain,Sijie Han,Daniel Karl I. Weidele,Mauro Martino,David Cox,Antonio Torralba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HQMQ，一种无需校准的大语言模型KV缓存压缩方法。利用Hurwitz四元数群和随机码本的乘积量化，在约5比特下匹配fp16性能，实现高达5.05倍压缩，且无需校准过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的KV缓存占用大量内存，现有量化方法需要校准过程且在异常值较多的现代架构上表现不佳。需要一种无需校准、对异常值鲁棒的高效KV缓存压缩方法。

Method: 1. 将K或V的每4元素块视为四元数，量化其单位方向到乘积$q_p \cdot q_s$；
2. $q_p$来自24元素Hurwitz群$2T$（24胞体的24个顶点），$q_s$来自每(层,头)的随机单位四元数二级码本；
3. 乘积组合产生$24S$个有效码字但仅存储$S$个参数，随机初始化即可；
4. 采用per-batch中值乘数异常值提取步骤($C=3$)处理异常值。

Result: 1. 在Mistral-7B和Qwen3-8B上，~5比特下与fp16差距仅0.02-0.03 ppl；
2. 在Qwen2.5-7B和Qwen3-8B上，恢复因int4崩溃的性能至fp16水平（差距0.02-0.10 ppl）；
3. 在所有5个模型上Pareto优于朴素int方法3-1900倍；
4. 3.79比特时下游零样本准确率匹配fp16；
5. 与最强校准基线KIVI-4相比，3.79比特匹配~4.5比特性能，节省16%比特且无需校准；
6. Llama-3-70B 128k上下文缓存从43GB压缩至8.5GB（5.05倍压缩）。

Conclusion: HQMQ成功实现了一种高效、无需校准的KV缓存压缩方法，通过Hurwitz四元数乘法量化在保持性能的同时大幅降低内存占用，为大规模语言模型的部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We propose \textbf{Hurwitz Quaternion Multiplicative Quantization (HQMQ)}, a \textbf{calibration-free} method for KV cache compression of large language models. HQMQ treats each 4-element chunk of K or V as a quaternion and quantizes its unit direction to the \emph{product} $q_p \cdot q_s$, where $q_p$ ranges over the 24-element Hurwitz group $2T$ (the 24 vertices of the 24-cell on $S^3$, pairwise angle $60^\circ$) and $q_s$ ranges over a per-(layer, head) secondary codebook of $S$ \emph{random} unit quaternions. The multiplicative composition yields $24S$ effective codewords at $S$ stored parameters; random initialization suffices because left-multiplication is an $S^3$ isometry, so seeded codebooks vary in end-task ppl by $<1.5\%$. A per-batch median-multiplier outlier extraction step ($C{=}3$, no calibration) handles modern outlier-heavy architectures. We evaluate on five modern open models: Mistral-7B (dense MHA), Llama-3-8B and Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen3-8B (dense GQA), and gpt-oss-20b (sparse MoE). On Mistral-7B and Qwen3-8B, HQMQ matches fp16 within $0.02$--$0.03$ ppl points at $\sim$5 bits. On Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen3-8B, where naive int4 collapses to $10^4{+}$ ppl, HQMQ + Med3$\times$ recovers fp16 quality within $0.02$--$0.10$ ppl points at $\sim$5 bits. HQMQ Pareto-dominates naive int by $3$--$1900\times$ at matched bits across all five models, and downstream zero-shot accuracy matches fp16 at $3.79$ bits on Mistral. Against the strongest calibrated KV-quantization baseline, HQMQ at $3.79$ bits matches KIVI-4 ($\sim 4.5$ bits) within ${\sim}1$ pt on CoQA, $0.6$ pts on TruthfulQA, and $2.3$ pts on GSM8K, at $16\%$ fewer bits and without a calibration pass. At the storage level, HQMQ delivers up to $5.05\times$ KV compression, shrinking a Llama-3-70B 128k-context cache from 43 GB to 8.5 GB.

</details>


### [213] [Faster Thermal Profiling of a Lunar Rover with Machine Learning Adapted Finite Difference Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27651)
*Samuel Weber,Zaki Hasnain,Souma Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息机器学习(PIML)框架，用于月球探测车的热分析。该方法通过迁移神经网络自适应确定粗网格划分，在保证物理一致性的同时，预测精度相比粗网格物理模型和纯数据驱动模型分别提升50%和39%，计算速度比高保真仿真快3倍，实现了精度与效率的有效平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空间系统在极端热环境中运行需要精确高效的热建模以支持任务前系统设计和机载自主性。对于月球探测车，大温度梯度、辐射传热和可变表面条件使得可靠的热预测特别具有挑战性。高保真物理仿真虽然准确但计算成本高昂，而简化模型和查表方法往往缺乏足够的精度。因此需要一种既能保证精度又能提高计算效率的热建模方法。

Method: 提出了一种PIML框架，集成迁移神经网络(TNN)根据热载荷和初始条件自适应确定3D有限差分节点化，实现更精确的粗网格计算。框架内嵌入可微分有限差分热模拟器以强制物理一致性并支持高效训练，同时通过上采样层从粗网格解重建高分辨率温度场。该方法结合了数据驱动模型和嵌入式物理知识。

Result: 将所提出的PIML方法与高保真细网格仿真、低保真固定粗网格模型和纯数据驱动人工神经网络(ANN)进行对比评估。结果表明，PIML框架相对于粗网格物理模型和ANN模型，预测精度分别提高了50%和39%，同时保持了物理一致的热分布。计算效率方面，该框架比高保真仿真快3倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的PIML框架成功实现了月球探测车系统热建模中精度与效率的有效平衡，为极端热环境中自主空间系统的热分析提供了一种有前景的解决方案，能够同时满足预任务系统设计和机载自主性的需求。

Abstract: Autonomous space systems operating in extreme thermal environments require accurate and efficient thermal modeling to support both pre-mission system design and onboard autonomy. For lunar rovers, large temperature gradients, radiative heat transfer, and variable surface conditions make reliable thermal prediction especially challenging. High-fidelity physics-based simulations provide accurate results but are computationally expensive, while simplified models and lookup-table approach often lack sufficient accuracy. Physics-informed machine learning (PIML) offers a promising alternative by combining data-driven models with embedded physical knowledge. This paper presents a PIML framework for thermal analysis of a simplified lunar rover with internal heat sources, where machine learning enables environment-adaptive coarse meshing. The proposed architecture integrates a transfer neural network (TNN) that adaptively determines 3D finite-difference nodalization based on thermal loads and initial conditions, enabling more accurate coarse-mesh calculations. A differentiable finite-difference thermal simulator is embedded within the framework to enforce physical consistency and support efficient training, while an upscaling layer reconstructs high-resolution temperature fields from the coarse-grid solution. The proposed PIML approach is evaluated against high-fidelity fine-mesh simulations, low-fidelity fixed coarse-mesh models, and a purely data-driven artificial neural network (ANN). Results show that the PIML framework improves prediction accuracy by 50% and 39% relative to the coarse-mesh physics model and ANN model, respectively, while maintaining physically consistent thermal distributions. Computationally, the framework is also 3x faster than high-fidelity simulations, demonstrating an effective balance between accuracy and efficiency for thermal modeling of lunar rover systems.

</details>


### [214] [Transferable Reinforcement Learning via Probabilistic Latent Embeddings and Dynamic Policy Adaptation for Sim-to-Real Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27659)
*Gengyue Han,Yiheng Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概率潜变量嵌入和动态策略自适应的新型强化学习框架，用于解决仿真到现实迁移中的性能下降和安全违规问题。该方法通过推断环境潜变量表示并动态调整策略风险级别，在保证早期部署安全的同时提高了迁移效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶等网络物理系统中，深度强化学习智能体通常先在仿真器中训练再部署到真实环境，但由于不可避免的Sim2Real差距，常出现性能下降或安全违规问题。现有的零样本方法（如鲁棒安全RL和域随机化）虽然缓解了该问题，但通常以性能降低或残留安全风险为代价，尤其在遭遇未建模系统动力学时。

Method: 提出了一种新的强化学习框架，针对不同环境语境下的约束马尔可夫决策过程族。该方法利用元RL中的潜变量语境变量，从仿真经验中推断环境的潜在表示。同时结合分布式RL公式，根据潜变量估计精度动态调整部署策略的风险级别，在部署早期促进安全性，并通过快速策略适应提高效率。

Result: 该框架成功实现了在Sim2Real差距下的安全且高效的策略迁移，通过动态调整策略风险级别平衡了部署早期安全性与后续性能优化，解决了现有方法在未建模动态下的性能下降或安全风险残留问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为网络物理系统中的Sim2Real迁移提供了新的解决方案，有效平衡了安全性与效率，为实际应用中强化学习智能体的可靠部署提供了理论和方法支撑。

Abstract: Due to limited resources and public safety concerns, deep reinforcement learning (RL) agents for many cyber-physical systems (e.g., autonomous vehicles) are first trained in simulators. However, when deployed in real world environments, they often suffer from performance degradation or safety violations because of the inevitable Sim2Real gap. Existing zero-shot approaches, such as robust safe RL and domain randomization, mitigate this issue but typically at the cost of degraded performance or residual safety risks when experiencing unmodeled system dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose a novel reinforcement learning framework that enables safe and efficient policy transfer via probabilistic latent embeddings and dynamic policy adaptation. We consider a family of Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDPs) under different environment contexts. By leveraging latent context variable in meta-RL, the proposed framework infers the latent representation of the environment from simulated experiences. Furthermore, it incorporates a distributional RL formulation, which allows risk levels of the deployed policy to be adjusted dynamically, based on the estimation accuracy of the latent context variable. This strategy promotes safety at the early deployment stage and improves efficiency through fast policy adaptation under the Sim2Real gap.

</details>


### [215] [How the Optimizer Shapes Learned Solutions in Equivariant Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27662)
*Teodor-Mihai Stupariu,Andrei Manolache*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Muon优化器在等变神经网络中表现优于Adam，能优化出更规则的损失曲面和更高秩的权重表示，揭示了优化器设计对几何归纳偏置的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变神经网络虽通过构造编码几何对称性，但往往难以优化且性能可能不如约束较少的架构。现有工作主要通过架构修改（如约束松弛或近似等变性）来解决这个问题，而优化器的作用相对未被充分探索。

Method: 在点云和分子学习设置下，比较Muon和Adam优化器在多种等变和几何架构上的表现。在ModelNet40数据集上，通过Hessian估计、损失曲面可视化和学习权重及中间表示的谱性质来分析训练好的检查点。

Result: 在ModelNet40上，Muon在所有架构上都一致优于Adam。Muon收敛的检查点具有更大的Hessian曲率摘要但更规则的损失曲面，其学习的权重和表示具有更高的稳定秩和有效秩。

Conclusion: 实验观察表明，优化器设计与几何归纳偏置之间的相互作用值得社区进一步关注和研究。

Abstract: Equivariant neural networks encode geometric symmetries by construction, yet they are often difficult to optimize and can underperform less constrained architectures. A growing body of work addresses this through architectural modifications such as constraint relaxation or approximate equivariance, while the role of the optimizer remains comparatively underexplored. We study this direction by comparing Muon and Adam across several equivariant and geometric architectures under pointcloud and molecular learning settings. On ModelNet40, where the comparison is clearest, Muon consistently improves over Adam across all architectures considered. We then analyze the trained ModelNet40 checkpoints through Hessian estimates, loss surface visualizations, and spectral properties of learned weights and intermediate representations. The checkpoints reached by Muon have larger Hessian curvature summaries but more regular loss surfaces, and their learned weights and representations have higher stable and effective ranks. These observations suggest that the interaction between optimizer design and geometric inductive bias deserves further attention from the community.

</details>


### [216] [Aligning LLMs with Human Uncertainty: A Beta-Bernoulli Calibrator for LLM Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27668)
*Hui Dai,Ryan Teehan,Parsa Torabian,Mengye Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Beta-Bernoulli Calibrator (BBC)，利用二元结果和人类预测的聚合信号校准LLM预测，显著提升校准精度与预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM概率预测方法仅从二元结果学习并输出言语化预测，未利用聚合人类预测中的群体概率估计和预测者一致性程度等丰富信息。

Method: 提出Beta-Bernoulli Calibrator (BBC)，将任意模型的初始点估计转换为事件可能性的分布。BBC建模事件可能性p~Beta(α,β)和结果y~Bernoulli(p)，均值作为校准点预测，方差作为认知不确定性，同时利用二元结果和人类预测进行监督。

Result: BBC普遍比传统事后校准方法和专门针对预测微调的模型提供更好的校准和更准确的预测，同时保持轻量级并具有良好的泛化能力。BBC捕获的认知不确定性比言语化置信度能更可靠地预测预测误差。

Conclusion: BBC是一种有效的概率预测校准方法，通过引入人类预测信号和Beta-Bernoulli建模，在保持轻量级和良好泛化的同时显著提升了LLM概率预测的校准性和准确性。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting estimates the likelihood of uncertain future events. To improve LLM forecasting, existing methods typically learn from binary outcomes to output verbalized forecasts. However, while aggregated human forecasts contain rich information in both the crowd probability estimate and the degree of agreement among forecasters, how to utilize these signals remains underexplored. To address this, we propose the Beta-Bernoulli Calibrator (BBC), which converts an initial point estimate forecast from any model into a distribution over event likelihood, using supervision from both binary outcomes and human forecasts. BBC models event likelihood $p \sim \text{Beta}(α, β)$ and outcome $y \sim \text{Bernoulli}(p)$, with the mean as the calibrated point forecast and the variance as the epistemic uncertainty. Our results show that BBC generally provides better calibrated and more accurate forecasts than both traditional post-hoc calibration methods and models fine-tuned specifically for forecasting, while remaining lightweight and having good generalization. We also show that the epistemic uncertainty captured by BBC is a more reliable predictor of forecasting error than verbalized confidence.

</details>


### [217] [When do complex-valued neural networks help? A study of representation, geometry, and optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27673)
*Ashutosh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 复值神经网络(CVNNs)并非普遍优于实值网络，其优势取决于任务特性：相位敏感任务(如PSK)受益于复数表示，幅度主导任务(如QAM)更适合实值模型。RadioML基准测试中发现的性能差距主要源于超参数设置问题，CVNNs应被视为结构化归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设复值输入自然意味着复值网络更优，但缺乏对复数算术何时真正改善学习的系统性分析。标签信号可能存在于幅度、相位、二者耦合或某种对称性中，实值模型在适当坐标系下也可能表示这些特征，因此需要通过表示优先的评估方法来理解CVNNs的真正价值。

Method: 采用表示优先的评估框架，将CVNNs与六种基线进行系统对比：笛卡尔实值、极坐标、仅相位、仅幅度、参数匹配实值、FLOP匹配实值模型。在合成射频任务、量子波函数预测和EEG分析信号实验中进行验证，并对RadioML 2018.01A数据集进行基准测试伪影分析，通过梯度分析和学习率×激活函数因子实验探究性能差异根源。

Result: 在射频任务中：PSK任务相位感知和复值模型占优，QAM任务幅度模型更优，混合PSK+QAM仅带来微弱复数优势，未见过的载波相位旋转会破坏坐标系依赖模型。量子波函数预测中动量仅可从相位恢复，EEG实验显示相位锁定、幅度爆发和相位-幅度耦合各有最优坐标系。RadioML基准测试中22.94 PP的性能差距在独立调参后缩减至2.46 PP，梯度分析揭示实值基线在高学习率首步不稳定，而复数参数耦合更稳健地分配损失信号。

Conclusion: CVNNs不应被视为普遍优越的架构，而应理解为结构化归纳偏置，其性能增益取决于三个关键因素：表示方式、对称性和优化过程。研究结果强调了任务导向的表示选择和公平超参数调优的重要性，为CVNNs的适用场景提供了明确的指导原则。

Abstract: Complex-valued Neural Networks (CVNNs) are often motivated by domains where information is naturally encoded in magnitude and phase. Yet complex-valued inputs alone do not determine when complex arithmetic improves learning: the label signal may lie in amplitude, phase, their coupling, or a symmetry that real-valued models can also represent under suitable coordinates. We study this through a representation-first evaluation of CVNNs against Cartesian real, polar, phase-only, magnitude-only, parameter-matched real, and FLOP-matched real baselines. Across synthetic RF tasks, complex representations are useful but not universally superior. PSK-only tasks favor phase-aware and complex-valued models, QAM-only tasks favor magnitude-based models, mixed PSK+QAM gives only a small complex-valued advantage, and unseen carrier-phase rotations break coordinate-dependent models without augmentation. Similar patterns appear beyond RF: in quantum-wavefunction prediction, momentum is invisible to $|ψ|$ but recoverable from phase, while EEG analytic-signal experiments show that phase locking, amplitude bursts, and phase-amplitude coupling each favor different coordinate views. We also identify a benchmarking artifact on RadioML 2018.01A. Under matched-shared-trial selection, a CReLU complex model exceeds the best real baseline by 22.94 PP; under independent per-family tuning on the same data and 16-trial search space, the gap collapses to 2.46 PP. Gradient analysis traces the inflated gap to high-learning-rate first-step instability in real baselines, while complex parameter coupling distributes the loss signal more robustly. A learning-rate $\times$ activation factorial confirms the failure is primarily hyperparameter-driven. Overall, CVNNs are best viewed as structured inductive biases whose gains depend on representation, symmetry, and optimization, not as universally superior architectures.

</details>


### [218] [Test-Time Collective Action: Proxy-Based Perturbations for Correcting Algorithmic Harms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27689)
*Meghana Bhange,Ulrich Aïvodji,Elliot Creager*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了测试时集体行动（TTCA）框架，允许用户群体在不参与平台训练循环的情况下，通过代理模型和通用扰动来纠正机器学习系统中针对特定子群体的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当机器学习系统对特定子群体表现不佳时，现有公平性方法依赖于平台端的修复，用户无法主动纠正这些差异。现有的算法集体行动机制需要平台配合重新训练，用户缺乏自主的控制手段。

Method: 提出测试时集体行动（TTCA）框架：用户群体通过共享查询访问权限提取平台的代理模型，然后针对代理模型优化每个类别的通用扰动。每个成员在提交输入时应用该扰动，无需平台配合。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和FairFace数据集上的实验表明：中等规模的集体可以消除大部分子群体准确率差距，扰动可跨架构迁移（小代理可攻击大平台），改善了最差组准确率、机会平等差距和差异性影响。资源分析显示集体池化比单独攻击成本更低。

Conclusion: 测试时集体行动为用户提供了在平台端修复不可用或延迟时的纠正性干预手段，使用户能够在不依赖平台配合的情况下主动解决算法不公平问题。

Abstract: When machine learning systems under-perform for particular subgroups, affected users typically have no way to correct these disparities without relying on platform-level fixes. Existing approaches to algorithmic fairness rely on provider-centric approaches to correct these failures, leaving users with no external lever when faced with harm. Recent work in Algorithmic Collective Action shows that coordinated users can steer an algorithmic system toward a collective goal, but the existing mechanisms require the provider to retrain on the collective's modified data which users may not have control over. We propose Test-Time Collective Action (TTCA), a framework through which a group of users who share query access to the platform, can correct disparities affecting under-served subgroup without participating in the platform's training loop. We implement this through a proxy-based mechanism where the collective pools query access to a black-box API to extract a proxy of the platform, then optimizes a per-class universal perturbation against the proxy. Each member applies this perturbation to their own inputs at submission time, requiring no cooperation from the platform. We empirically evaluate the mechanism on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and FairFace, showing that modestly-sized collectives close most of the subgroup accuracy gap, transfer across architectures (a small proxy can attack a larger platform), and improve worst-group accuracy, equal-opportunity gap, and disparate impact. A query-budget analysis comparing a per-user black-box attack baseline shows that pooling is cheaper than each subgroup member attacking alone. Test-time collective action thus offers corrective intervention to users when platform-side remediation is unavailable or delayed.

</details>


### [219] [Bayesian Deployment Approval for Learned Landing Controllers under Finite Rollout Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27720)
*Fei Jiang,Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个贝叶斯审批框架，用于在有限验证证据下评估学习型自主着陆控制器的部署就绪度，通过后验推断量化不确定性，提供比传统经验指标更可靠的部署评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习和数据驱动控制器通常使用有限仿真轨迹下的累积奖励和经验成功率进行评估，但这些经验指标无法为不确定环境下的部署就绪度提供充分的统计证据，可能导致过度自信的部署决策。

Method: 提出了基于不确定操作条件下着陆安全满足的概率着陆能力建模方法；利用贝叶斯后验推断量化学习策略真实部署能力的不确定性；引入后验审批概率和后验部署风险指标；构建支持审批/拒绝/继续决策的序贯验证框架。

Result: 使用PPO和SAC控制器的仿真实验表明，在有限验证证据下，经验成功率和奖励优化可能产生过度自信的部署解释，而贝叶斯后验审批推断能提供不确定性校准更好的部署就绪度评估。

Conclusion: 该框架在传统强化学习评估与不确定环境下的部署导向验证之间建立了实用的统计联系，并可推广到更广泛的各类学习型自主系统，为安全关键应用提供更可靠的部署决策依据。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning and data-driven autonomous controllers are commonly evaluated using cumulative reward and empirical success frequency under finite simulation trajectories. However, such empirical metrics do not necessarily provide sufficient statistical evidence regarding deployment readiness under uncertainty. This work develops a Bayesian approval framework for learned autonomous landing controllers under finite rollout evidence. A probabilistic landing capability formulation is introduced based on touchdown safety satisfaction under uncertain operating conditions, while Bayesian posterior inference is used to quantify uncertainty regarding the true deployment capability of learned policies. Posterior approval probability and posterior deployment risk are further introduced for deployment-oriented evaluation, together with a sequential validation framework supporting approve/reject/continue decisions during progressive rollout testing. Simulation experiments using PPO and SAC controllers demonstrate that empirical success and reward optimization may produce overconfident deployment interpretation under limited validation evidence, whereas posterior approval inference provides a more uncertainty-calibrated assessment of deployment readiness. The proposed framework provides a practical statistical connection between conventional reinforcement-learning evaluation and deployment-oriented validation under uncertainty and may be generalized to broader classes of learned autonomous systems.

</details>


### [220] [NUCLEUS-MoE: Unified Model of Pool Boiling for Liquid Cooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27722)
*Arthur Feeney,Xianwei Zou,Sheikh Md Shakeel Hassan,Siddhartha Rachabathuni,Aparna Chandramowlishwaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NUCLEUS——一种基于专家混合架构的池沸腾模型，能够统一建模不同流体和工况下的沸腾动力学，解决了现有模型泛化能力差的问题，在保持物理一致性的同时展现出优异的零样本/少样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两相沸腾传热速率比单相冷却高一个数量级，但由于相变、湍流和输运之间的强耦合，以及对流体属性和热力学条件的极端敏感性，建模仍然很困难。现有基于学习的代理模型要么局限于特定工况，要么局限于特定流体，限制了泛化能力且需要单独的模型，因此需要一种统一的架构来替代专用代理模型的集合。

Method: 提出NUCLEUS混合专家模型，结合了邻域注意力机制、用于界面一致性的符号距离场重初始化，以及能够在不同沸腾动力学中展现涌现专业化特性的专家路由机制。模型在高保真池沸腾仿真数据上训练，联合建模饱和沸腾和过冷沸腾，覆盖介电液体、制冷剂和低温工质三类流体。

Result: 专家路由展现出连贯的空间结构和专业化特性（无需显式监督）。定量上，NUCLEUS在异构沸腾配置中匹配或超越基线模型，同时保持物理一致性。在下游任务（如新型浸没式冷却流体Opteon 2P50）上展示了零样本和少样本泛化能力，成功解决了先前模型在极端流体上的失效问题。

Conclusion: 混合专家模型是实现沸腾动力学统一代理建模的可扩展途径，为科学机器学习领域更广泛的泛化奠定了基础，证明了单一架构可以替代多个专用模型的可行性。

Abstract: Two-phase boiling enables heat transfer rates an order of magnitude higher than single-phase cooling, but it remains difficult to model due to the strong coupling between phase change, turbulence, and transport, as well as extreme sensitivity to fluid properties and thermodynamic conditions. Existing learning-based surrogates are either condition- or fluid-specific, limiting generalization and requiring separate models. We present NUCLEUS, a mixture-of-experts model for pool boiling that replaces collections of specialized surrogates with a single architecture. NUCLEUS combines neighborhood attention, signed distance field reinitialization for interface consistency, and expert routing that exhibits emergent specialization across distinct boiling dynamics.
  Trained on high-fidelity simulations of pool boiling, NUCLEUS jointly models saturated and subcooled boiling across three fluid classes (dielectrics, refrigerants, and cryogens), resolving failure modes of prior models on extreme fluids. We show that expert routing exhibits coherent spatial structure and specialization without explicit supervision. Quantitatively, NUCLEUS matches or exceeds baselines while maintaining physical consistency across heterogeneous boiling configurations. We also show zero-shot and few-shot generalization capabilities on downstream tasks such as a new fluid (Opteon 2P50 developed for immersion cooling). These results demonstrate that mixture-of-experts models are a scalable pathway toward unified surrogate modeling of boiling dynamics and lay the groundwork for broader generalization across scientific ML.

</details>


### [221] [Can Entry-Wise Clipping Give Spectral Control of Stochastic Gradients?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27733)
*Zitao Song,Cedar Site Bai,Zhe Zhang,Brian Bullins,David F. Gleich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种简单的逐元素平滑收缩方法来控制梯度噪声的谱范数，在NanoGPT预训练中节省约7%的训练token，与Muon结合可额外节省约2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型训练中由于稀有词和多层层组合导致梯度噪声呈现重尾分布，现有方法存在权衡：向量范数裁剪忽略权重矩阵结构，谱归一化（如Muon）尊重结构但成本较高。作者希望在这种权衡中找到平衡。

Method: 发现实际梯度噪声类似逐元素重尾污染，通过一阶扰动分析揭示噪声的局部化性质。基于此，推导高斯信号先验下贝叶斯最优逐元素估计器的可处理代理，提出简单的逐元素平滑收缩方法。建立了柯西污染噪声下$O(\epsilon^{-4})$的收敛保证。

Result: 在NanoGPT预训练中，平滑收缩方法改善Adam优化器，节省约7%的训练token；将逐元素裁剪应用于谱归一化之前，在Muon基础上额外节省约2%的token。

Conclusion: 通过利用重尾梯度噪声的局部化性质，简单的逐元素方法可以实现谱控制，有效平衡了结构尊重与计算成本之间的权衡，显著提升训练效率。

Abstract: Training instabilities such as loss spikes are frequently the result of stochastic gradient noise. Because of rare expressions in language training data, and multiple layer composition, the noise impact is heavy-tailed and survives mini-batch averaging. Existing remedies trade off structure against cost: vector-norm clipping ignores the matrix structure of weight updates, while spectral normalization (e.g., Muon (Jordan et al., 2024)) respects it at additional cost. We show that this trade-off can be balanced. Real gradient noise appears to be similar to entry-wise heavy-tailed contamination, and a first-order perturbation analysis reveals a localization property of such noise, under which a simple entry-wise method achieves spectral control. Exploiting this, we derive a tractable surrogate for the Bayes-optimal entry-wise estimator under a Gaussian signal prior. We establish $O(ε^{-4})$ convergence guarantee under Cauchy-contaminated noise. Empirically, we find that smooth shrinkage improves Adam on NanoGPT pretraining, saving ${\sim}7\%$ of training tokens. We further find that applying the entry-wise clipping before spectral normalization yields a ${\sim}2\%$ token saving on top of Muon.

</details>


### [222] [Learn from your own latents and not from tokens: A sample-complexity theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27734)
*Daniel J. Korchinski,Alessandro Favero,Matthieu Wyart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了潜在表征预测方法相比传统方法能实现指数级的样本效率提升，且无需显式堆叠为层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型虽然性能优异但数据效率远低于生物学习系统。基于潜在表征预测的自监督学习方法（如data2vec、JEPA）在实证中表现良好，但其理论基础尚不完善，关键问题包括：潜在预测究竟能提升多少数据效率？多层堆叠是否有必要？

Method: 使用可处理的概率上下文无关语法作为数据模型，该语法能捕捉自然语言和图像的组合结构，通过递归应用产生式规则沿深度为L的隐符号树生成可见标记序列。通过三种方式验证：(i)层次聚类算法；(ii)端到端神经网络；(iii)对data2vec进行样本复杂度分析。

Result: 理论证明：对于监督学习或标记级自监督学习，恢复潜在树结构需要指数于L的样本数；而潜在预测方法仅需常数于L的样本数（对数因子级别），实现了指数级样本效率提升。显式堆叠（如H-JEPA）在很大程度上是冗余的，因为data2vec等方法已隐式执行层次化潜在预测。

Conclusion: 潜在表征预测从理论上解释了自监督学习方法的高数据效率，为预测编码皮层理论提供了支持。研究表明无需显式设计多层堆叠架构，现有方法已能通过隐式方式实现层次化预测。

Abstract: Generative models, from diffusion models to large language models, achieve remarkable performance but at a cost in training data orders of magnitude larger than what biological learners require. An alternative paradigm has emerged in which networks are trained to predict their \emph{own} latent representations of related views or masked regions, as in data2vec and JEPA -- an idea related to predictive-coding accounts of the cortex. Despite strong empirical results, the theoretical understanding of these methods remains limited. Central questions include: by how much does latent prediction actually improve data efficiency? Is there a benefit to stacking such methods into multi-scale hierarchies? We answer both using as data a tractable probabilistic context-free grammar that captures the compositional structure of natural language and images. Such a grammar generates strings of visible tokens by recursively applying production rules along a tree of hidden symbols of depth $L$. For such data, supervised or token-level SSL require a number of samples \emph{exponential} in $L$ to recover the latent tree; we prove that latent prediction achieves this with a number of samples \emph{constant} in $L$, up to logarithmic factors. We confirm this bound with (i) a hierarchical clustering algorithm, (ii) an end-to-end neural network whose predictor-clusterer modules predict their own latents at each level via gradient descent, and (iii) the first sample-complexity analysis of data2vec, which we show implicitly performs hierarchical latent prediction. This suggests that explicit stacking such as H-JEPA is largely redundant.

</details>


### [223] [Explicit Critic Guidance for Aligning Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27736)
*Zhengyang Liang,Qihang Zhang,Ceyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种状态对齐的潜在演员-评论家框架用于扩散模型的后训练，在单目标和多目标优化任务上均优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线强化学习方法在扩散模型对齐中存在两个主要局限性：一是在去噪轨迹上进行细粒度信用分配困难，二是基于价值的优化稳定性不足。这些缺陷阻碍了扩散模型与非可微目标的有效对齐。

Method: 提出状态对齐的潜在演员-评论家框架，其中扩散模型作为自身的时间步条件价值函数，直接在噪声潜在状态上预测价值值。该方法实现了轨迹级PPO训练，通过简单的条件化和价值预训练策略支持稳定的演员-评论家优化，并自然地将学习到的评论家重用于推理时的引导。进一步将框架扩展到多奖励优化，通过互补奖励的联合训练缓解奖励黑客问题。

Result: 在基于UNet和DiT的架构上，该方法在单奖励和多奖励基准测试中均持续优于先前的组相对RL和演员-评论家基线方法，同时在测试时引导为生成质量提供了额外的提升。

Conclusion: 状态对齐的潜在演员-评论家框架有效解决了扩散模型强化学习对齐中的信用分配和优化稳定性问题，在多种架构和任务设置下展现出优越性能，为扩散模型的后训练提供了一种高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning is becoming increasingly important for aligning diffusion models with non-differentiable objectives. However, existing methods still face limitations in assigning fine-grained credit along denoising trajectories and in realizing stable value-based optimization. We propose a state-aligned latent actor-critic framework for diffusion post-training, in which the diffusion model serves as its own timestep-conditioned value function and predicts values directly on noisy latent states. This enables trajectory-level PPO training, supports stable actor-critic optimization with simple conditioning and value pretraining strategies, and naturally allows the learned critic to be reused for inference-time steering. We further extend the framework to multi-reward optimization, where joint training with complementary rewards helps alleviate reward hacking. Across both UNet- and DiT-based backbones, our method consistently outperforms prior group-relative RL and actor-critic baselines on single-reward and multi-reward benchmarks, while test-time steering provides additional gains in generation quality.

</details>


### [224] [Worker Disagreement Reveals Sharp Directions in Local SGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27739)
*Tolga Dimlioglu,Kristi Topollai,Anna Choromanska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Local SGD中的worker分歧可以廉价估计Hessian主子空间，无需直接计算Hessian。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络训练的损失曲面呈现高度各向异性，少数尖锐的主Hessian方向与大量平坦方向共存。梯度倾向于与这些主方向过度对齐，但稳定的训练进展往往需要沿着平坦方向移动。估计主子空间很有用，但基于Hessian的直接方法成本高昂。

Method: 理论证明Local SGD中的worker-average gap协方差受随机梯度噪声和Hessian曲率影响，导致workers在尖锐的曲率敏感方向上产生分歧。利用这种worker-average gaps作为Hessian-free的主子空间估计器。

Result: 在MLP、CNN和Transformer上的实验表明，由worker-average gaps形成的子空间能够捕获位于主导Hessian特征空间中的大部分梯度成分。

Conclusion: Worker-average gaps提供了一种廉价、无需Hessian计算的主子空间估计方法，能够有效揭示损失曲面的几何结构。

Abstract: Deep neural network training often exhibits highly anisotropic loss geometry, where a few sharp dominant Hessian directions coexist with a large flatter bulk. Gradients tend to align disproportionately with these dominant directions, although stable progress often requires movement through flatter bulk directions. Estimating the dominant subspace is therefore useful but costly with direct Hessian-based methods. We show that standard Local SGD exposes this geometry through worker disagreement. We theoretically show that the worker-average gap covariance is shaped by stochastic-gradient noise and Hessian curvature, causing workers to disagree along sharp, curvature-sensitive directions. Thus, worker-average gaps provide a cheap Hessian-free estimator of the dominant subspace. Experiments on MLPs, CNNs, and Transformers show that subspaces formed by worker-average gaps capture a substantial fraction of the gradient component lying in the dominant Hessian eigenspace.

</details>


### [225] [High-Fidelity Industrial Crash Dynamics Prediction via Geometry-Aware Operator Learning with Memory-Efficient Low-Rank Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27758)
*Deepak Akhare,Mohammad Amin Nabian,Corey Adams,Sudeep Chavare,Sanjay Choudhry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoTransolver框架用于工业级汽车碰撞动力学的高精度预测，并提出了一步式时序策略和FLARE注意力机制改进，在保险杠和整车碰撞数据集上实现了最优预测精度，同时显著降低了内存开销和推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车耐撞性优化是安全关键性挑战，需要通过迭代的高保真仿真来管理大规模非线性结构变形和能量耗散。传统有限元求解器计算代价过高，而现有的算子学习框架在处理工业级碰撞分析时面临复杂几何、接触非线性和快速演化的瞬态变形等挑战。

Method: 采用GeoTransolver框架捕捉多尺度几何特征，提出并系统评估了三种时序预测策略（一步式、时间条件式和自回归展开），并引入基于快速低秩注意力路由引擎（FLARE）的改进来优化注意力主干网络，在保险杠梁和整车碰撞数据集上进行验证。

Result: 一步式策略在显著降低训练开销和推理延迟的同时达到了最优预测精度；FLARE改进将内存开销降低约2倍，并进一步提升了对O(N)长程、高频瞬态的预测准确性；框架成功捕捉了塑性变形模式和关键乘员位置的加速度曲线。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明了几何感知算子学习在复杂、安全关键的汽车动力学高保真代理建模方面的实用可行性，为工业级碰撞仿真提供了高效准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Automotive crashworthiness optimization remains a safety-critical challenge, requiring the management of large-scale nonlinear structural deformations and energy dissipation through iterative, high-fidelity simulations. While traditional finite element solvers are computationally prohibitive, emerging operator learning frameworks provide rapid surrogate predictions; however, applying them to industrial-scale crash analysis, where complex geometry, contact nonlinearities, and rapidly evolving transient deformation coexist, remains an open challenge. In this paper, we demonstrate that the GeoTransolver framework provides a viable solution for accurate, high-fidelity crash dynamics prediction at industrial scale. Benchmarked on complex bumper beam and full-vehicle crash datasets, GeoTransolver captures multi-scale geometric context and accurately resolves plastic deformation patterns as well as acceleration profiles at critical occupant locations. Beyond the architecture itself, we propose and systematically evaluate a suite of temporal prediction recipes, including one-shot, time-conditional, and autoregressive rollout strategies, demonstrating that the one-shot approach achieves state-of-the-art accuracy with significantly reduced training overhead and inference latency. As a secondary contribution, we introduce a Fast Low-rank Attention Routing Engine (FLARE)-based modification to the GeoTransolver attention backbone that reduces memory overhead by approximately 2x while further improving predictive accuracy for O(N) long-range, high-frequency transients, preserving the geometry-aware cross-attention strengths of the base framework. Our results highlight the practical viability of geometry-aware operator learning for high-fidelity surrogate modeling of complex, safety-critical automotive dynamics.

</details>


### [226] [A Paired Testing Protocol for Batch-Conditioned Refusal Robustness in LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27763)
*Sahil Kadadekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现批次条件会影响语言模型的安全评估结果，但经过严格验证后实际翻转率仅为0.16%，批次不变性内核可有效消除此问题，建议采用精确堆栈验证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型安全评估通常将服务配置视为固定的背景基础设施，但批次条件（prompt单独评估、同步批次或连续批次调度）是一个未经测试的处理变量，可能影响安全评估结果的可靠性。

Method: 提出配对测试协议，综合四项研究：Study A结合本地发现、评分者校正裁决和真实批次确认；Study B测试跨模型泛化（15个模型）；Study C测试连续批次组合；Study D进行批次不变性内核消融实验。

Result: 本地测试发现安全标签变化率高于能力标签（0.51% vs 0.14%），但经裁决后实际翻转率仅0.16%；15模型扩展未发现普遍的安全-能力偏差，翻转率接近均等（0.94倍）；启用VLLM_BATCH_INVARIANT=1后，标签翻转从22/55降至0/55。

Conclusion: 建议采用精确堆栈验证方法：在服务批次设置下评估拒绝行为，将安全提示与能力控制配对，并分别报告低率的定向翻转与总体无效效应。

Abstract: Safety evaluations of language models often treat serving configuration as fixed background infrastructure, but batch condition is an untested treatment variable whenever the same prompt may be evaluated alone, in a synchronized batch, or inside a continuous-batching scheduler. We synthesize four artifact-backed studies into a paired testing protocol: Study A combines local discovery, scorer-corrected adjudication, and true-batching confirmation; Study B tests cross-model generalization; Study C tests continuous-batch composition; and Study D runs a batch-invariant-kernel ablation. The local test finds safety-label changes more often than capability-label changes (0.51% vs. 0.14%), but adjudication of 63 candidate rows leaves only 17 genuine behavioral flips, implying a corrected full-set rate of 0.16%. The 15-model extension finds no detectable universal safety-over-capability skew: flips are near parity (0.94x), alignment type has no detectable association ($p=0.942$, $η^2=0.033$), and output instability is the strongest tested fragility screen ($r=0.909$, bootstrap 95% CI [0.65, 0.97]). In the targeted kernel ablation, standard vLLM reproduces 22/55 label flips on current score-flip candidates, while enabling VLLM_BATCH_INVARIANT=1 reduces the same test to 0/55 flips; the composition test separately finds no aggregate effect at 4.7pp sensitivity. The testing recommendation is exact-stack validation: evaluate refusal at the served batch setting, pair safety prompts with capability controls, and report low-rate directional flips separately from aggregate null effects.

</details>


### [227] [Restoring the Sweet Spot: Pass-Rate Weighted Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27765)
*Zehao Liu,Yuanpu Cao,Jinghui Chen,Vasant G. Honavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SC-SDPO，通过对SDPO损失进行难度感知的加权（权重为[p̂(1-p̂)]^½），解决了SDPO缺乏难度意识的问题，在科学推理和工具使用任务上相比SDPO取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDPO虽然提供了密集的token级信用分配，但其基于KL的优势估计缺乏内在的难度感知机制。相比之下，GRPO的组相对优势自然地将学习集中于中等难度问题上。本文旨在通过理论分析揭示这一差距，并提出改进方案。

Method: 通过将可学习性框架扩展到归一化奖励，作者证明归一化会吸收方差项p(1-p)，使不同问题的前导阶可学习性趋于一致，而√p(1-p)成为每个问题梯度的唯一残差缩放因子。基于此分析，提出了SC-SDPO方法：使用[ṗ(1-ṗ)]^½对每个问题的SDPO损失进行加权。该权重作为策略采样的零成本副产品获得，并通过批次自适应归一化实现。

Result: 在科学推理和工具使用基准测试中，SC-SDPO相比SDPO取得了一致的改进：在Qwen3-8B上提升+3.2/+4.3（mean@16/maj@16），在OLMo-3-7B上提升+1.8/+3.0，同时在优化过程中保持了稳定的训练动态。

Conclusion: 理论分析揭示了归一化在可学习性框架中的作用机制，提出的简单加权方案成功为SDPO引入了隐式的课程学习，使其能够动态跟踪模型不断演化的能力水平，在多个基准上取得了稳定的性能提升。

Abstract: Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (SDPO) provides dense token-level credit assignment for reinforcement learning with large language models by leveraging the model's own feedback-conditioned predictions as a self-teacher. Unlike GRPO, however, whose group-relative advantage naturally concentrates learning on a sweet spot of intermediate-difficulty questions, SDPO's KL-based advantage lacks an implicit notion of difficulty awareness.
  We analyze this gap through the lens of GRPO's advantage normalization. Extending the learnability framework to normalized rewards, we show that normalization absorbs the variance term $p(1-p)$, equalizing leading-order learnability across questions and leaving $\sqrt{p(1-p)}$ as the sole residual scaling factor in the per-question gradient. This analysis yields a simple prescription: weight each question's SDPO loss by $[\hat{p}(1-\hat{p})]^{1/2}$, resulting in SC-SDPO, a scale-consistent variant of SDPO.
  The proposed weights are obtained as a zero-cost byproduct of on-policy rollouts with batch-adaptive normalization, inducing an implicit curriculum that dynamically tracks the model's evolving competence. Experiments on scientific reasoning and tool-use benchmarks demonstrate that SC-SDPO consistently improves over SDPO, yielding gains of +3.2/+4.3 (mean@16/maj@16) on Qwen3-8B and +1.8/+3.0 on OLMo-3-7B, while preserving stable training dynamics throughout optimization.

</details>


### [228] [Fine-Tuning Dynamics of In-Context Factual Recall in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27774)
*Ruomin Huang,Eshaan Nichani,Jason D. Lee,Rong Ge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了上下文事实回忆(IC-recall)任务，证明单层Transformer在少量样本下即可学会通过成对注意力模式，利用MLP关联记忆检索事实知识，实现基于上下文的知识召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论工作主要关注仅利用提示中的信息进行上下文学习，但实际应用中常需要模型检索存储在参数中的事实知识。本研究旨在理解上下文学习如何利用事实知识召回，填补理论与实践之间的空白。

Method: 引入IC-recall任务：模型接收由隐藏关系生成的(主体,答案)对作为上下文，需推断隐藏关系并检索查询主体的答案。事实知识通过预构建的MLP关联记忆存储(主体,关系,答案)三元组。分析单层Transformer在IC-recall数据上的监督微调动态。

Result: 证明模型能成功执行IC-recall并收敛到特定的成对注意力模式；微调仅需少量样本（仅为知识三元组数量的多对数级）；实验验证了理论预测，且MLP层预训练而非人工构建时，成对注意力模式依然涌现。

Conclusion: 本研究为理解大型语言模型如何结合上下文信息与参数化知识提供了理论框架，证明了成对注意力模式是实现IC-recall的关键机制，为设计更高效的知识增强模型提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In-context learning \ -- performing tasks based on examples given in the prompt \ -- is an important capability that has emerged in large language models and has received significant attention in both theory and practice. Existing theoretical work often focuses on settings where the learning uses information purely from the prompt. However, many practical instances of in-context learning require the model to retrieve factual knowledge stored in the model's parameters, with the context serving to identify which knowledge is relevant. In this work, we study how in-context learning leverages factual knowledge recall. We formalize this behavior by introducing the \emph{in-context factual recall (IC-recall)} task, where a transformer is provided a context of (subject, answer) pairs generated from a hidden relation, along with a query subject, and must both infer this hidden relation and retrieve the corresponding answer. Factual knowledge is modeled by the transformer having access to a simple pre-constructed MLP associative memory storing (subject, relation, answer) triplets. We analyze the supervised fine-tuning dynamics of a one-layer transformer on IC-recall data and prove that the model successfully performs IC-recall by converging to a particular pairwise attention pattern. This fine-tuning stage requires a very small number of samples \ -- only polylogarithmic in the number of stored knowledge triplets. Experiments verify our theoretical predictions and show that the pairwise attention pattern emerges even when the MLP layer is pretrained instead of constructed.

</details>


### [229] [Revisiting ML Training under Fully Homomorphic Encryption: Convergence Guarantees, Differential Privacy, and Efficient Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27782)
*Yvonne Zhou,Mingyu Liang,Ivan Brugere,Danial Dervovic,Yue Guo,Antigoni Polychroniadou,Min Wu,Dana Dachman-Soled*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在全同态加密(FHE)环境下对机器学习训练进行了理论收敛性分析，并提出了一种适用于加密计算的差分隐私训练算法，在保持与标准差分隐私梯度下降(DP-GD)相当效用的同时提高了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全同态加密环境下进行机器学习训练时，需要使用多项式近似来替代激活函数和损失函数以确保FHE兼容性，同时还需要保护下游任务的隐私。现有的差分隐私方法依赖昂贵的逐样本梯度裁剪，导致加密学习的可扩展性受限。因此需要开发理论收敛保证和高效的隐私保护训练方法。

Method: 提出了使用激活函数和损失函数的多项式近似的近似梯度下降方法，并证明了其收敛性；集成了差分隐私机制但不依赖昂贵的逐样本梯度裁剪；提供了数据无关的超参数选择方法和理论指导的多项式近似策略。

Result: 证明了在全同态加密环境下使用多项式近似的近似梯度下降的收敛性；相比标准差分隐私梯度下降(DP-GD)实现了更高的计算效率；达到了与DP-GD相当的效用；实现了可扩展的加密学习；提供了数据无关的超参数选择和多项式近似的理论策略。

Conclusion: 本研究推进了在敏感数据上进行高效、隐私保护和安全机器学习的可行性，为全同态加密环境下的机器学习训练提供了理论基础和实用方法，多项式近似策略具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: We present the first theoretical convergence analysis of machine learning training under fully homomorphic encryption (FHE), combined with a differentially private (DP) training algorithm tailored to encrypted computation. Our approach improves computational efficiency over standard differentially private gradient descent (DP-GD) while achieving comparable utility. In particular, we prove convergence of approximate gradient descent using polynomial approximations of activation and loss functions, which are required for FHE compatibility. To preserve privacy in downstream tasks, we integrate differential privacy without relying on costly per-sample gradient clipping, enabling scalable encrypted learning. We also provide data-independent hyperparameter selection and theoretically grounded strategies for polynomial approximation which can be of independent interest. Together, these contributions advance the feasibility of efficient, private, and secure machine learning on sensitive data.

</details>


### [230] [Locality-Aware Redundancy Pruning for LLM Depth Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27786)
*Vincent-Daniel Yun,Youngrae Kim,Woosang Lim,YoungJin Heo,Minkyu Kim,Sunwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的单次深度剪枝框架LoRP，通过表征局部性指导剪枝，在保持模型性能的同时提升了推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一次性剪枝方法依赖局部层重要性或固定的冗余假设，未能考虑不同LLM架构中层间冗余可能是局部化或全局分布的特性。

Method: 提出表征局部性感知剪枝框架(LoRP)：1)引入表征局部性分数(RLS)从全局层间隐藏状态相似性中推导；2)使用小规模校准集计算层间成对相似性；3)按表征相似性对层进行聚类；4)根据簇内剩余冗余分配剪枝。

Result: 在多个LLM家族上的实验表明，LoRP在困惑度和下游任务准确性方面均取得了提升。

Conclusion: LoRP证明了层间冗余具有架构依赖性（可局部化或全局分布），基于表征局部性的剪枝方法能够有效识别和移除冗余层，为LLM的高效部署提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models are known to contain representational redundancy across network depth, making depth pruning an effective approach for improving inference efficiency. Existing one-shot pruning methods rely on local layer importance or fixed redundancy assumptions across architectures. We propose Locality-Aware Redundancy Pruning (LoRP), a training-free one-shot depth pruning framework guided by representation locality. We show that inter-layer redundancy can be either localized or globally distributed depending on the LLM architecture. To characterize this phenomenon, we introduce Representation Locality Score (RLS), derived from global inter-layer hidden-state similarity. Using a small calibration set, LoRP computes pairwise layer similarity, clusters layers by representational similarity, and allocates pruning according to residual intra-cluster redundancy. Experiments across diverse LLM families show improvements in both perplexity and downstream task accuracy.

</details>


### [231] [Knowing When to Ask: Segment-Level Credit Assignment for LLM Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27788)
*Abhijit Kumar,Zoey Wu,Mohit Suley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CARL方法，通过训练critic学习模型的能力边界，使其能够判断何时使用外部工具，在多个基准测试上显著提升性能并减少不必要的工具调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型缺乏对自身知识边界的认知能力，无法判断何时应该寻求外部工具帮助。基于提示的方法无法教会模型识别自身知识边界；传统强化学习方法使用轨迹级奖励，无法准确评估单个工具调用的贡献，也难以惩罚不必要的调用。

Method: 提出CARL（Competence-Aware Reinforcement Learning）：1）在模型自身的rollout上训练critic，学习参数化知识何时足够、何时需要外部帮助；2）在自然工具使用边界（如代码围栏分隔符和上下文块转换）处分解每个rollout；3）从单个二元结果为每个片段分配独立信用，无需外部评判或步骤级标注；4）使错误调用、错误提取和不必要调用都能获得适当符号的优势值。

Result: 1）训练的critic能有效捕捉模型领域能力，在7B模型上AUC达0.93区分参数化可解和依赖工具的问题；2）在五个基准测试（算术、多跳事实QA、金融表格数值推理）上，7B模型精确匹配准确率提升6.7分，3B模型提升9.7分；3）Musique基准提升最大（7B: +8.3 EM，3B: +9.0 EM）；4）对参数化可回答问题的工具调用减少53%，同时保持约10 EM点的更高准确率；5）小规模模型收益更大：3B模型改进是7B模型的1.4倍。

Conclusion: CARL成功赋予语言模型识别自身知识边界的能力，显著提升了工具使用的效率和准确性。研究表明，知道何时求助对小参数化记忆模型的帮助更大，证明了能力感知训练对于提升模型自主学习边界判断的重要性。

Abstract: Humans know when to reach for help e.g. $347 \times 28$ warrants a calculator while $2+2$ does not. Language models do not. Prompt-based approaches can instruct a model when to invoke tools, but this scaffolding does not teach it to recognize the boundary of its own knowledge. RL approaches that assign a single outcome reward to the whole trajectory fare no better: trajectory-level credit cannot isolate which tool call in a successful episode actually helped, nor penalize unnecessary calls. We propose \textbf{CARL} (\textbf{C}ompetence-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{R}einforcement \textbf{L}earning), which trains a critic on the model's own rollouts to learn where parametric knowledge suffices and where it needs external help. By decomposing each rollout at natural tool-use boundaries (e.g., code fence delimiters and context block transitions), CARL assigns independent credit to each segment from a single binary outcome, without external judges or step-level annotations. As a result, erroneous tool calls, incorrect extractions, and unnecessary calls each receive appropriately signed advantages. The trained critic captures the model's domain competence: it separates parametrically solvable from tool-dependent questions with AUC 0.93 at 7B. On five benchmarks spanning arithmetic, multi-hop factual QA, and numerical reasoning over financial tables, CARL improves exact-match accuracy by 6.7 points at 7B and 9.7 points at 3B over the best RL baseline, with the largest gain (+8.3 EM at 7B, +9.0 EM at 3B) on Musique. The model issues 53\% fewer tool calls on parametrically answerable questions while remaining ${\sim}10$ EM points more accurate on them. Gains are largest at small scale: the 3B improvement is $1.4\times$ the 7B improvement, suggesting that knowing when to ask disproportionately benefits models with smaller parametric memory.

</details>


### [232] [SYNAPSE: Neuro-Symbolic Visual Thought-to-Text Decoding via Topological Semantic Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27790)
*Akshaj Murhekar,Abhijit Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SYNAPSE框架，通过符号正则化方法解决了EEG到文本解码中因生物噪声导致的生成不稳定问题，无需端到端微调即可达到与资源密集型微调系统相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇EEG到想象文本的解码系统在面对生物噪声时极其脆弱，受损的神经投影会导致冻结的语言模型产生幻觉或语义不稳定的生成结果，缺乏有效的噪声处理机制。

Method: 提出SYNAPSE（符号神经对齐精确语义提取）轻量级神经符号框架，通过推理时的符号正则化来稳定神经文本生成；利用常识图结构和潜在样本净化EEG衍生的语义候选，无需端到端LLM微调即可改善语义稳定性。

Result: 在多个流行EEG解码基准和不同冻结LLM后端上的实验表明，该方法相比无约束提示基线取得一致的改进，在对象标签消融下保持鲁棒性，性能与资源密集型微调系统相当，同时通过将原始EEG处理完全本地化在编码器堆栈中来保护生物特征隐私。

Conclusion: SYNAPSE成功实现了在保护生物特征隐私的前提下，通过轻量级符号正则化方法显著提升了EEG到文本解码的语义稳定性和鲁棒性，为神经符号AI在脑机接口领域的应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have accelerated open-vocabulary EEG-to-imagined-text decoding, where non-invasive neural activity recorded during visual perception is translated into coherent natural language descriptions of viewed stimuli. However, existing systems remain highly vulnerable to biological noise, where corrupted neural projections induce hallucinated or semantically unstable generation in frozen language models. We introduce SYNAPSE (Symbolic Neural Alignment for Precise Semantic Extraction), a lightweight neuro-symbolic framework that stabilizes neural text generation through inference-time symbolic regularization. By purifying EEG-derived semantic candidates using commonsense graph structure and latent exemplars, SYNAPSE improves semantic stability without end-to-end LLM fine-tuning. Experiments across popular EEG decoding benchmarks and multiple frozen LLM backends demonstrate consistent gains over unconstrained prompting baselines, robustness under object-label ablation, and performance commensurate with substantially more resource-intensive fine-tuned systems, while preserving biometric privacy by localizing raw EEG processing entirely within the encoder stack.

</details>


### [233] [Density-aware Sample-specific Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27809)
*Qiyuan Wang,Yao Li,Raymond K. W. Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型后门攻击方法，通过将触发样本引导至干净数据分布的低密度区域，实现了对微调和剪枝等训练后防御方法的强鲁棒性，在防御后仍保持50-85个百分点更高的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后门攻击方法容易受到训练后防御（如微调或剪枝）的影响，这些防御可以通过微调或剪枝来消除后门。为了解决这一脆弱性问题，需要设计一种能够在防御后仍保持高攻击成功率的后门攻击方法。

Method: 本文基于贝叶斯最优受害者训练模型，推导出最优样本特定触发器构建的原则性标准。分析发现，当触发样本被引导至干净数据分布的低密度区域时，可同时优化攻击成功率和干净准确率保持。作者提出了一个双层优化框架，通过条件时间分数匹配估计密度比，并优化混合模型目标以将触发样本放置在这些稀疏区域。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10、GTSRB和TinyImageNet上的广泛评估表明：该方法在防御前达到99%以上的攻击成功率；在微调防御下，比最强基线保持50-85个百分点更高的防御后ASR；对神经元剪枝防御表现出完全免疫性，在所有剪枝阈值下均无神经元被识别为需要移除。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了当前防御范式中的根本性缺陷，强调了需要开发超越干净分布支持的防御方法，以有效应对此类新型后门攻击。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in backdoor attacks, existing methods remain susceptible to post-training defenses that erase the backdoor through fine-tuning or pruning. We revisit the core objectives of backdoor attacks and derive principled criteria characterizing optimal sample-specific trigger construction under a Bayes-optimal model of the victim's training. Our analysis reveals that both attack success and clean-accuracy preservation are simultaneously optimized when triggered samples are steered into low-density regions of the clean data distribution, a distributional condition that controls all moments of the poisoned distribution at once rather than a handful of input-space summary statistics. We introduce a bilevel optimization framework that estimates density ratios via conditional time-score matching and optimizes a mixture-model objective to place triggered samples in these sparse regions. Extensive evaluations on MNIST, CIFAR-10, GTSRB, and TinyImageNet demonstrate that our method achieves above 99\% attack success rate before defense and retains 50--85 percentage points higher post-defense ASR than the strongest baselines under fine-tuning defenses. Against neuron-pruning defenses, the method exhibits complete immunity, with zero neurons identified for removal across all pruning thresholds. These results expose a fundamental gap in current defense paradigms and underscore the need for defenses that operate beyond the support of the clean distribution.

</details>


### [234] [ReSAE: Residualized Sparse Autoencoders for Multi-Layer Transformer Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27819)
*Prathyush Poduval,Calvin Yeung,Neel Desai,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Residualized Sparse Autoencoders (ReSAEs)，通过拟合层间仿射映射并对未解释的残差进行训练，有效解决了多层SAE干预中的冗余和交互问题，在多层替换场景下表现优于传统SAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏自编码器逐层独立训练，忽略了Transformer残差流激活在深度方向上的强耦合关系。这导致实际问题：不同层的字典可能花费容量表示相同的传递信息，且同时替换多层时会产生单层行为无法预测的交互效应。

Method: 引入Residualized Sparse Autoencoders (ReSAEs)：在选定层之间拟合仿射映射，在未解释的残差而非完整激活上训练每层的SAE；重建通过拟合的仿射链映射回原始激活空间，从而使用与普通SAE相同的干预协议进行评估。

Result: 在Pythia-1.4B和Gemma-2-9B上，残差化减少了解码器冗余，在大多数测试设置中改善了稀疏探测和定向扰动。尽管重建的原始激活方差较少，ReSAEs在多层替换下恢复了更多的Transformer交叉熵。在教师强制和充分在线稀疏性下优势最明显。

Conclusion: 移除线性可预测的跨层结构是多层SAE干预的有用默认策略，ReSAEs能够保留与模型下游计算最相关的激活组件。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders are usually trained one layer at a time, even though transformer residual stream activations are strongly coupled across depth. This creates a practical problem for multi-layer interventions: different layerwise dictionaries can spend capacity representing the same carried-forward information, and replacing several layers at once can produce interactions that are not predicted by single-layer behavior. We introduce Residualized Sparse Autoencoders (ReSAEs), which fit an affine map between selected layers and train each later-layer SAE on the unexplained residual rather than on the full activation. Reconstructions are mapped back into the original activation space through the fitted affine chain, so ReSAEs can be evaluated with the same intervention protocols as ordinary SAEs. On Pythia-1.4B and Gemma-2-9B, residualization reduces decoder redundancy and improves sparse probing and targeted perturbation in most tested settings. Despite reconstructing less of the raw activation variance, ReSAEs recover more transformer cross entropy under multi-layer replacement. This gain is clearest under teacher-forcing and at sufficient sparsity online, indicating that ReSAEs preserve the components of the activation most relevant to the model's downstream computation. These results suggest that removing linearly predictable cross-layer structure is a useful default for multi-layer SAE interventions.

</details>


### [235] [Decentralized Parameter-Free Online Learning with Compressed Gossip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27831)
*Tomas Ortega,Hamid Jafarkhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DECO-EF算法，首次在压缩通信的去中心化在线学习场景下实现了无参数化且具有次线性网络遗憾界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的去中心化在线优化方法通常需要根据时间跨度、比较器规模等参数调整学习率，且压缩通信会引入额外的节点不一致性，需要新的方法来解决这些限制。

Method: 提出DECO-EF（基于错误反馈的去中心化投币预测）算法，将投币预测机制与基于差值的压缩Gossip协议相结合。每个代理维护清洁的累积状态和压缩跟踪器，在Gossip步骤中仅传输压缩的状态差值。

Result: 在压缩通信条件下，证明了DECO-EF的期望比较器自适应网络遗憾界限，实现了次线性网络遗憾保证。

Conclusion: DECO-EF是首个在压缩通信下去中心化在线学习中实现无参数化并具有期望次线性网络遗憾保证的算法，无需调节学习率、时间跨度或比较器范数等参数。

Abstract: We study decentralized online convex optimization when agents communicate over a graph and messages may be compressed. Classical decentralized online methods typically require learning-rate choices that depend on the horizon, comparator scale, or other problem parameters, while compressed communication introduces additional disagreement that must be controlled. We propose DECO-EF (DEcentralized COin-betting with Error Feedback), a decentralized parameter-free online learning algorithm that combines coin-betting predictions with compressed difference-based gossip. Each agent maintains a clean accumulated state and a compressed tracker, and communicates only compressed state differences during gossip steps. The method is parameter-free in the online-learning sense: it does not tune to the horizon, the comparator norm, or the learning rate. We prove expected comparator-adaptive network-regret bounds for DECO-EF under compressed communication. To the best of our knowledge, this gives the first expected sublinear network-regret guarantees for parameter-free decentralized online learning under compressed communication.

</details>


### [236] [Reward Transfer from Inverse Reinforcement Learning: A Coupled Minimax Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27834)
*Guang-Yuan Hao,Lars van der Laan,Aurélien Bibaut,Nathan Kallus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在环境变化时，如何将逆强化学习得到的奖励函数迁移到新环境中。提出了耦合方法解决跨环境的Bellman方程组，相比传统的序贯方法能有效消除源环境Bellman残差误差的一阶影响，并提供了理论分析和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，专家演示数据通常在受控环境中收集，但需要在不同的目标环境中应用强化学习。这种环境变化下的奖励迁移问题具有重要的实际意义，但现有方法在处理环境差异时存在误差累积问题。

Method: 将问题建模为源环境和目标环境的联合Bellman方程组，开发了目标软q函数的极小极大估计器。提出了两种解决方案：序贯方法（先估计源奖励再应用到目标控制问题）和耦合方法（联合求解源和目标方程组）。通过理论分析比较了两种方法的性能。

Result: 证明了耦合方法相比序贯方法能够消除源Bellman残差误差的一阶影响。推导了有限样本下软q函数的误差界限，并证明了所得软控制策略的后悔度保证。在脓毒症模拟器上的实验验证了理论比较的正确性。

Conclusion: 耦合方法在跨环境奖励迁移任务中显著优于传统的序贯方法，为逆强化学习在实际环境变化场景中的应用提供了更稳健的理论基础和有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We study the transfer of rewards learned using inverse reinforcement learning from expert demonstrations in one environment to reinforcement learning in a new, different environment. This arises naturally when demonstrations are collected in a controlled environment. We formulate the problem as a joint system of Bellman equations across the source and target environments and develop minimax estimators for the target soft-$q$-function. Whereas a sequential solution approach first estimates the source reward and then plugs it into the target control problem, a coupled approach solves the source and target system of equations jointly. We show that, in contrast to the sequential approach, the coupled approach removes the first-order influence of source Bellman residual error. We characterize the local behavior of each approach, develop finite-sample soft-$q$-function error bounds, and prove regret guarantees for the resulting soft-control policy. An empirical investigation using a sepsis simulator validates the theoretical comparison.

</details>


### [237] [CAREF: Calibration-Aware Regularization for Explanation Faithfulness Without Rationale Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27835)
*Naphat Nithisopa,Teerapong Panboonyuen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAREF通过校准感知正则化联合优化预测准确性和解释忠实度，在仅使用6.43%可训练参数的情况下，在四个NLE基准测试中实现了最佳平均准确率(89.04)和解释对齐度(81.00 nBERT)，超越了LoRA和AdaLoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调方法在优化预测准确性时，往往忽视了解释的忠实度问题。缺乏对解释忠实度的显式优化可能导致模型生成的解释不可靠，因此需要一种无需理由标注监督就能同时优化预测准确性和解释忠实度的统一框架。

Method: 提出CAREF框架，通过校准感知解释忠实度正则化损失(LSCED)将基于熵的校准与token级稀疏性控制耦合在单一统一损失函数中。该方法无需理由标注监督，引入了CAREF-AQ轻量级变体，实现了参数高效的微调。使用Flan-T5作为基础模型进行实验验证。

Result: 在COS-E、ECQA、ComVE、e-SNLI四个自然语言解释基准测试上，CAREF-AQ仅使用6.43%的可训练参数，达到了最佳平均准确率89.04和最佳解释对齐度81.00 nBERT，显著优于LoRA和AdaLoRA基线方法。

Conclusion: CAREF是首个将熵正则化和稀疏性正则化统一在单一训练目标中用于可解释大语言模型微调的方法，证明了无需理由标注监督即可有效提升预测准确性和解释忠实度的可行性，为可解释AI领域提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We introduce CAREF, a parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework that jointly optimizes predictive accuracy and explanation faithfulness via calibration-aware regularization. At its core, CAREF couples entropy-based calibration with token-level sparsity control through a single unified loss, the Calibration-Aware Regularization for Explanation Faithfulness (LSCED), without requiring rationale supervision. Evaluated on four NLE benchmarks (COS-E, ECQA, ComVE, e-SNLI) with Flan-T5, our lightweight CAREF-AQ variant attains the best average accuracy (89.04) and explanation alignment (81.00 nBERT) using only 6.43% of trainable parameters, outperforming LoRA and AdaLoRA. To our knowledge, CAREF is the first method to unify entropy and sparsity regularization in a single training objective for interpretable LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [238] [From Detection to Mechanism: Cross-Attention Graph Neural Networks Enable Drug-Drug Interaction Type Prediction An Ablation Study with Acetylsalicylic Acid Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27861)
*Juergen Dietrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较了三种GNN架构用于药物相互作用预测，发现跨注意力机制显著提升多分类性能（+45%），但对二分类提升有限（+1.3%），证实原子级分子间通信对机制类型分类的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测药物是否相互作用（二分类检测）与预测相互作用机制类型（多分类）是本质不同的任务。现有研究缺乏对不同GNN架构在这两类任务上性能差异的系统比较，需要明确架构设计如何影响不同预测任务的性能。

Method: 在包含86种相互作用类型的38,337个正样本对基准数据集上，对三种GNN架构进行消融研究：拼接式孪生双MPNN（Concat）、四头跨注意力双MPNN（CrossAtt）和融合交互图的三元MPNN（Ternary）。三种架构在完全相同的训练条件下（n=61,339对）进行比较。

Result: CrossAtt相比Concat在多分类F1-macro上提升+0.186（+45%），在二分类AUC上仅提升+0.012（+1.3%），证实原子级分子间通信专门促进机制类型分类。Ternary架构性能不佳，归因于训练不稳定性。在10个ASA药物对的留出验证中，CrossAtt实现10/10准确预测，而Ternary为0/10。所有架构存在两个一致性失败案例，与并行毒性研究中的结构限制相关。

Conclusion: 跨注意力机制是实现准确DDI机制类型分类的关键架构选择，原子级分子间通信对此任务至关重要。三元架构存在训练不稳定问题导致性能下降。架构选择对多分类任务影响显著大于二分类任务，不同预测任务需要针对性的架构设计。

Abstract: Predicting whether two drugs interact (binary detection) is a substantially dif- ferent task from predicting the mechanism type of that interaction (multi-class classification). This study presents a systematic ablation study of three Graph Neural Network (GNN) architectures for drug-drug interaction (DDI) prediction on a publicly available benchmark dataset comprising 38,337 positive pairs across 86 interaction types. Three architectures are compared under identical training conditions (n = 61,339 pairs): a siamese dual Message Passing Neural Network (MPNN) with concatenation (Concat), a dual MPNN with four-head cross-attention (CrossAtt), and a ternary MPNN incorporating an interaction graph (Ternary). CrossAtt improves multi-class F1-macro by +0.186 absolute (+45%) over Concat, while improving binary AUC by only +0.012 (+1.3%) - confirming that atom-level inter-molecular communication specifically enables mechanism-type classification. The ternary architecture underperforms despite equivalent training data, with its failure consistent with a training instability hypothesis. Validation on ten acetylsali- cylic acid (ASA) drug pairs, held out prior to training, demonstrates 10/10 correct DDI-type predictions for CrossAtt versus 0/10 for Ternary. Two consistent failure cases are identified across all architectures, linking to structural limits established in a companion toxicity study.

</details>


### [239] [SPAR: Support-Preserving Action Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27877)
*Jiaxin Zhao,Weihang Pan,Xun Liang,Binbin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPAR框架，通过局部残差修正解决离线策略优化中价值最大化与数据分布拟合的冲突，在D4RL基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线策略优化面临固有冲突：样本内加权回归虽然稳定但过于保守，会抑制分布尾部的高价值动作；而基于梯度的方法常出现拟合-优化梯度冲突，导致策略偏离数据流形。

Method: 提出支持保留动作修正（SPAR）框架，将全局学习重构为锚定冻结纯行为克隆策略的局部残差修正，在残差空间进行细粒度拟合和局部策略改进；引入潜在自模仿机制，利用潜在采样加权回归解决残差空间中的拟合-改进梯度冲突。

Result: 理论上证明该机制消除了标准价值梯度的流形法向漂移；在D4RL实验中，SPAR从次优基线中提取显著增益，达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: SPAR通过局部残差修正有效解决了离线强化学习中的保守性与偏离数据流形问题，为离线策略优化提供了新的解决思路，在标准基准上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Offline policy improvement faces an inherent conflict between maximizing value and fitting the data distribution. While in-sample weighted regression is stable, it suffers from over-conservatism that suppresses high-value actions in the distribution tail; conversely, gradient-based approaches often exhibit a fitting-optimization conflict of gradients, which drives the policy off the data manifold. To address this, we propose Support-Preserving Action Rectification (SPAR), which reframes global learning as a local residual rectification anchored to a frozen pure behavior cloning policy. This framework performs fine-grained fitting and local policy improvement in the residual space, thereby contracting the search space. We further introduce Latent Self-Imitation, utilizing a latent-sampling weighted-regression mechanism to address fitting-improvement gradient conflict in the residual space. Theoretically, we prove this mechanism eliminates the manifold-normal drift of standard value gradients, while extensive D4RL experiments show SPAR extracts significant gains from suboptimal baselines to achieve state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [240] [FedEHR-Gen: Federated Synthetic Time-Series EHR Generation via Latent Space Alignment and Distribution-Aware Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27892)
*Jun Bai,Ziyang Song,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FedEHR-Gen，首个用于跨医院分布式时间序列电子健康记录(EHR)生成的联邦学习框架，通过两阶段学习范式（联邦自编码器+联邦时序条件变分自编码器）解决了EHR数据高维稀疏性和跨医院异质性导致的联邦建模崩溃问题，在生成保真度、下游实用性和隐私风险方面达到与集中式训练相当的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EHR生成模型多为集中式，需要汇聚各医院数据，但在隐私受限的医疗环境中数据共享往往不可行；而直接的联邦EHR生成由于数据的高维性、稀疏性和跨医院异质性，常导致模型崩溃或发散，缺乏有效的联邦框架来解决这一挑战。

Method: 提出两阶段学习范式：第一阶段采用联邦自编码器将高维稀疏EHR特征投影到紧凑潜在空间，设计分层匹配聚合机制确保跨医院语义一致性；第二阶段在已对齐的潜在空间上训练联邦时序条件变分自编码器(TCVAE)，采用分布感知聚合机制，在严重跨医院异质性下实现稳定的时序生成建模。

Result: 在eICU和MIMIC-III数据集上的大量实验表明，FedEHR-Gen在生成保真度、下游实用性和隐私风险方面达到与集中式训练相当的性能，同时显著优于标准联邦学习基线方法。

Conclusion: FedEHR-Gen成功解决了联邦EHR生成中的关键挑战，为隐私受限的医疗环境提供了一个有效的数据增强和跨医院建模解决方案，填补了联邦时间序列EHR生成的空白。

Abstract: Synthetic Electronic Health Record (EHR) generation provides a promising avenue for data augmentation and cross-hospital modeling in privacy-constrained healthcare settings. However, most existing EHR generative models are centralized and require pooling data across hospitals, which is often infeasible when real-world data sharing is restricted. While federated EHR generation offers a natural solution, direct federated modeling often collapses or diverges due to the high dimensionality, sparsity, and cross-hospital heterogeneity of EHR data. In this work, we propose FedEHR-Gen, the first federated framework for synthetic time-series EHR generation across distributed hospitals. FedEHR-Gen uses a two-stage learning paradigm. First, we introduce a federated autoencoder that projects high-dimensional and sparse EHR features onto a compact latent space. To ensure semantic consistency across hospitals, we develop a layer-wise matching aggregation mechanism that aligns local encoders into a unified global latent space. Second, operating on this aligned latent space, we train a federated temporal conditional variational autoencoder (TCVAE) with distribution-aware aggregation, enabling stable temporal generative modeling under severe cross-hospital heterogeneity. Extensive experiments on the eICU and MIMIC-III datasets demonstrate that FedEHR-Gen achieves generation fidelity, downstream utility, and privacy risk comparable to centralized training, while consistently outperforming the standard federated baseline.

</details>


### [241] [Where LLM Annotators Fail: Label-Free Learning on Graphs with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27913)
*Safal Thapaliya,Jiatan Huang,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CANE框架，通过估计聚类条件下的LLM标注可靠性来处理图节点分类中的标签噪声，在无需真实标签的情况下显著提升了分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图节点分类需要大量标注节点，但获取标签成本高昂。虽然LLM可以为具有语义内容的节点属性提供低成本标注，但这些标注存在噪声，且现有无标签图学习方法通常将噪声视为全局或类别条件噪声。作者发现LLM标注错误不仅依赖于类别，还依赖于区域：在同一类别内，不同特征空间聚类的可靠性差异显著。

Method: 提出聚类感知噪声估计（CANE）框架，该框架无需真实标签即可估计聚类条件下的LLM可靠性。具体而言，CANE首先识别特征空间中的聚类，然后估计每个聚类中LLM标注的可靠性，最后根据这些估计决定信任哪些伪标签以及纠正哪些标签。

Result: 在各种图基准测试和GNN骨干网络上，CANE优于最强的无标签基线方法。在表现出更强聚类条件噪声的数据集上，CANE取得了最大的性能提升。

Conclusion: LLM标注噪声具有聚类条件特性，考虑这种细粒度的噪声模式对于提升无标签图学习性能至关重要。CANE框架成功利用了这一洞察，为利用LLM进行图节点标注提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Node classification on graphs often requires labeled nodes, yet obtaining labels at graph scale is expensive. When node attributes contain semantic content, such as paper abstracts, web pages, or product descriptions, large language models (LLMs) can provide low-cost supervision by annotating a small subset of nodes. However, these LLM-generated labels are noisy, and existing label-free graph learning methods usually treat this noise as either global or class-conditional. We find that LLM annotation errors are not only class-dependent but also region-dependent: within the same class, reliability can vary sharply across feature-space clusters. In light of this, we propose Cluster-Aware Noise Estimation (CANE), a label-free learning framework that estimates cluster-conditional LLM reliability without ground truth labels, and uses this estimate to decide which pseudo-labels to trust, and which labels to correct. Across various graph benchmarks and GNN backbones, CANE improves over the strongest label-free baselines, with the largest gains on datasets exhibiting stronger cluster-conditional noise.

</details>


### [242] [Cyclical Entropy Eruption: Entropy Dynamics in Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27954)
*Wendi Li,Shawn Im,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了智能体强化学习中存在的“周期性熵爆发”现象，并提出了SEAL方法通过分离正确和错误轨迹的表示来稳定训练，从而提升智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式大语言模型在解决实际任务时越来越依赖强化学习框架，但其训练动态仍缺乏深入理解，限制了诊断训练不稳定性和设计更有效训练算法的能力。

Method: 识别并分析了代理强化学习特有的“周期性熵爆发”现象，将其分解为三个阶段进行理论和实证分析；提出了SEAL（分离增强的智能体学习）方法，这是一种轻量级辅助损失函数，通过在表示空间中分离正确和错误轨迹来直接针对熵爆发的根本原因。

Result: 实验表明SEAL能够稳定训练过程，并在多个基准测试、模型和强化学习算法上实现了更强的下游智能体性能。同时揭示了句子重复和幻觉等退化模式一旦在熵爆发期间获得，会在各个周期中持续存在并累积。

Conclusion: 本文为理解代理强化学习的训练动态提供了新视角，所提出的SEAL方法有效地解决了熵爆发问题，为设计更稳定和有效的代理训练算法奠定了基础。

Abstract: Agentic large language models are increasingly used to solve real-world tasks by reasoning over goals, invoking tools, and interacting with external environments. Reinforcement learning provides a natural framework for improving these behaviors, and recent agent RL methods have achieved strong results across domains. However, the training dynamics of agent RL remain poorly understood, limiting our ability to diagnose instabilities and design more effective training algorithms. In this work, we identify a previously underexplored phenomenon in agent RL, which we term cyclical entropy eruption. Unlike single-turn reasoning RL, where entropy typically collapses and stays low, agent RL training exhibits unique recurring cycles of sharp entropy eruption and gradual subsidence. We decompose this dynamic into three phases and provide theoretical and empirical analyses of each, explaining the mechanisms underlying its cyclical oscillation. We further show that degenerate patterns such as sentence duplication and hallucination, once acquired during eruption, can persist and accumulate across cycles. Motivated by these findings, we propose SEAL (Separation-Enhanced Agent Learning), a lightweight auxiliary loss that separates correct and incorrect trajectories in representation space, directly targeting the root cause of entropy eruption. Experiments across multiple benchmarks, models, and RL algorithms demonstrate that SEAL stabilizes training and yields stronger downstream agent performance.

</details>


### [243] [Continual Learning in Modern Hopfield Networks with an Application to Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27975)
*Ken Takeda,Masafumi Oizumi,Ryo Karakida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过现代Hopfield能量视角分析了生成模型在持续学习中的遗忘机制，发现高能量离群样本更容易被遗忘，且回放对这类样本特别有效，并在Stable Diffusion和DDPM上验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型（如扩散模型）越来越多地作为基础模型并通过顺序微调进行适配，但持续学习在这些模型中的机制仍不清楚：任务切换后，学习分布的哪些方面最容易丢失，应该优先回放哪些样本？

Method: 利用现代Hopfield网络（MHN）与扩散模型之间的理论联系，将MHN中的分析迁移到扩散模型。引入内在遗忘概念（任务变化后Hopfield能量的增加），在MHN可处理设置中证明高能量、离群样本比较大的能量增加；进一步分析记忆回放并基于能量选择回放样本。

Result: 理论证明：位于尖锐、孤立盆地的高能量离群样本更容易被遗忘；回放对高能量样本特别有效。在MHN、Stable Diffusion和像素空间DDPM的持续学习实验中验证：Hopfield能量能够追踪基于重构的遗忘，且回放实验揭示了与MHN分析一致的能量依赖性遗忘缓解机制。

Conclusion: 现代Hopfield能量为生成模型持续学习中的遗忘问题提供了有效的理论框架，能量指标可用于识别易遗忘样本并指导回放样本的选择，为生成模型的持续学习策略设计提供了理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: Generative models, including diffusion models, are increasingly used as foundation models and adapted through sequential fine-tuning, making continual learning an essential problem setting. However, continual learning in such generative models remains poorly understood: after a task change, what aspects of the learned distribution are most easily lost, and what replay samples should be prioritized? We address these questions through the modern Hopfield energy. Recent links between modern Hopfield networks (MHNs) and diffusion models allow analyses in MHNs to be transferred to diffusion models. We introduce intrinsic forgetting as an increase in Hopfield energy after the task change. In tractable settings in an MHN, we prove that high-energy, outlier-like samples undergo a larger energy increase than cluster-like samples, implying that samples located in sharp, isolated basins are more forgettable. We further analyze memory replay and show that replay is particularly effective for high-energy samples, enabling an energy-based selection of replay samples. We validate these predictions in experiments on MHNs and two diffusion models under continual-learning settings: Stable Diffusion and a pixel-space DDPM. In these diffusion models, Hopfield energy tracks reconstruction-based forgetting, and replay experiments reveal energy-dependent mitigation of forgetting that is consistent with the MHN analysis.

</details>


### [244] [Law of Neural Interaction: Depth-Width Shape, Interaction Efficiency, and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27989)
*Wenjie Sun,Jinning Yang,Shuai Zhang,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了模型深度宽度比($R_{D/W}$)通过影响神经交互效率进而影响泛化性能，为LLM架构设计提供了理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型在缩放定律指导下资源需求不断增加，但在固定预算下模型是否有效利用资源仍存疑问。已有研究证明叠加效应是损失的关键因素，本文旨在扩展这一研究以优化资源利用效率。

Method: 利用神经特征假设将叠加效应从参数空间扩展到梯度空间，定义神经交互概念。通过调整模型的深度宽度比($R_{D/W}$)，研究在固定预算下神经交互效率与泛化性能的关系，并在预算扩展时验证高效交互区间的稳定性。

Result: 发现在固定预算下，良好泛化性能通常伴随高效的神经交互；通过调整深度宽度比可将模型置于高效交互区间；随着预算增加，高效交互区间保持相对稳定；在MMLU-Pro基准测试中，接近该区间操作的模型表现更优。

Conclusion: 深度宽度比($R_{D/W}$)通过影响资源利用效率进而影响模型泛化性能，这为模型形状初始化和理解模型泛化机制提供了重要见解，有助于在有限资源下设计更优的LLM架构。

Abstract: The guidance of scaling laws has increased the resource demands of modern large language models (LLMs), yet it remains questionable whether these models utilize resources effectively under a fixed budget. Previous research has proved superposition as a key contributor to loss. By leveraging the Neural Feature Ansatz, we extend superposition from parameter space to gradient space and define it as neural interaction. We find that under a fixed budget, good generalization is usually accompanied by efficient neural interactions, and the model can be placed in an efficient interaction interval by adjusting its depth-width ratio ($R_{D/W}$). In addition, as the budget scales up, the efficient interaction interval of the model remains relatively stable. By comparing existing small scale dense LLMs, we observe that models operating near this interval tend to perform better on the MMLU-Pro benchmark. Our findings reveal that the $R_{D/W}$ influences resource utilization efficiency and thereby affects generalization, providing insights into model shape initialization and the understanding of model generalization mechanisms. Code for Neural Interaction Law is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Neural_Interaction_Law-D788

</details>


### [245] [Geometry-Correct Diffusion Posterior Sampling with Denoiser-Pullback Curvature Guidance and Manifold-Aligned Damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27990)
*Seunghyeok Shin,Minwoo Kim,Dabin Kim,Hongki Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于阻尼高斯-牛顿校正的扩散后验采样方法，用每噪声水平的校正替代标量引导权重，在FFHQ和ImageNet上实现了有竞争力的重建质量和更快的速度，在加速MRI重建中取得了最佳PSNR/SSIM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散后验采样方法使用手工调节的标量引导权重进行数据一致性更新，在刚性、算子依赖曲率条件下会导致采样不稳定，需要一种更鲁棒的校正机制。

Method: 在扩散状态坐标系中计算每噪声水平的阻尼高斯-牛顿校正：通过去噪器反向拉回似然梯度，采用单边曲率模型避免前向去噪器雅可比矩阵，应用与去噪器残差对齐的扩散校准秩一阻尼；使用无矩阵GMRES和自动微分求解校正，采用具有闭式漂移/噪声分裂的保方差朗之万转移进行采样。

Result: 在FFHQ和ImageNet的多种逆问题上，该方法取得了有竞争力的PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS指标，且运行速度明显快于大多数对比基线；在加速MRI重建任务中，取得了所有对比基线中最优的PSNR/SSIM指标。

Conclusion: 通过引入阻尼高斯-牛顿校正替代标量引导权重，有效解决了扩散后验采样中的稳定性问题，实现了重建质量和计算效率的良好平衡，证明了该方法在医学成像等实际应用中的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion posterior sampling conditions diffusion priors on measurements, but data-consistency updates are typically scaled by hand-tuned guidance weights and can destabilize sampling under stiff, operator-dependent curvature. We replace scalar guidance with a per-noise-level damped Gauss--Newton correction computed in diffusion-state coordinates. The correction pulls likelihood gradients back through the denoiser, uses a one-sided curvature model that avoids forward denoiser Jacobians, and applies diffusion-calibrated rank-one damping aligned with the denoiser residual. Each correction is solved with matrix-free GMRES using automatic differentiation, and sampling proceeds with a variance-preserving Langevin transition with a closed-form drift/noise split. On FFHQ and ImageNet across inverse problems, it achieves competitive PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS while running markedly faster than most of the compared baselines; on accelerated MRI reconstruction, it achieves the best PSNR/SSIM among the compared baselines.

</details>


### [246] [Patched-DeltaNet: Token-Level Event-Driven Memory for Linear-Time Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27992)
*Tae-Gyun Lee,Junyoung Park,Kyu Won Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Patched-DeltaNet，一种融合时间序列分块与门控Delta网络的新型架构，在SMD数据集上实现ROC-AUC 0.957和PA-F1 0.822，计算复杂度从O(L²)降至理论最优O(L/P)，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的时间序列异常检测方法（如PatchTST）虽然性能优异，但其O(L²)计算复杂度严重限制了在资源受限环境中的部署，亟需开发既保持高性能又具备低计算复杂度的新型架构。

Method: 提出Patched-DeltaNet架构，结合时间序列分块机制与门控Delta网络。分块机制提取局部语义块，DeltaNet基于误差驱动更新循环状态，仅在检测到显著物理变化（delta）时更新，形成token级事件驱动记忆机制，有效过滤背景噪声并捕获异常漂移。

Result: 在Server Machine Dataset (SMD)基准数据集上，在统一评估约束和相同计算预算下严格超越近期架构，实现ROC-AUC 0.957和PA-F1 0.822，同时将计算复杂度大幅降低至理论最小值O(L/P)，证明了模型的结构优越性和样本效率。

Conclusion: Patched-DeltaNet成功解决了Transformer架构的计算瓶颈，通过分块与误差驱动更新的协同作用实现了高效的时间序列异常检测，为资源受限环境下的部署提供了可行方案，兼具理论创新与实用价值。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection is critical for maintaining the reliability of mission-critical systems. While Transformer-based models like PatchTST have shown remarkable performance, their $\mathcal{O}(L^2)$ computational complexity severely limits deployment in resource-constrained environments. In this paper, we propose Patched-DeltaNet, a novel architecture combining time-series patching with Gated Delta Networks.
