### [285] [Dimensionality Reduction for Robust Federated Learning: A Theoretical Analysis and Convergence Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28335)
*Shiyuan Zuo,Jiashuo Li,Rongfei Fan,Han Hu,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PDR框架，通过稀疏随机投影压缩梯度到低维子空间，将鲁棒聚合的计算复杂度降至理论最优O(Mp)，在几乎不损失精度的情况下实现了数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习容易遭受Byzantine攻击，现有的鲁棒聚合方法虽然能防御攻击，但在高维梯度聚合时计算开销巨大，且该开销随模型规模增长而恶化，成为训练瓶颈。

Method: 提出Projected Dimensionality Reduction (PDR)框架：利用稀疏随机投影将梯度压缩到极小的子空间，在子空间中高效计算可靠性权重，然后应用于原始梯度聚合；基于子空间嵌入定理保证投影质量。

Result: 将服务端计算复杂度降至最优O(Mp)，达到理论下界；证明收敛率在非凸函数下为O(1/√T)，强凸函数下为O(1/T)；Byzantine误差底线仅膨胀有界的可调因子(1+ε)/(1-ε)；实验显示获得数量级加速且保持竞争性收敛性能。

Conclusion: PDR是一个通用的加速框架，可集成到现有向量级距离鲁棒聚合器中，以几乎无代价的方式实现大规模加速，为大规模联邦学习的Byzantine鲁棒训练提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables multiple clients to collaboratively train models without sharing raw data, but it is highly vulnerable to Byzantine attacks. Existing robust approaches can neutralize these threats but incur substantial computational overhead during high-dimensional gradient aggregation, an overhead that scales poorly with model size and increasingly dominates the training cost as modern models grow larger. To address this computational bottleneck, we propose Projected Dimensionality Reduction (PDR), a universal acceleration framework for vector-level distance-based robust aggregators, which performs robust aggregation by compressing gradients into a drastically smaller subspace via sparse random projection to efficiently compute reliability weights. This approach reduces the server computational complexity to an optimal $ \mathcal{O}(Mp) $, where $ M $ is the number of clients and $ p $ is the model dimension, matching the theoretical lower bound required merely to read the gradients. We establish convergence guarantees under standard FL assumptions in prior Byzantine-robust FL analyses. By leveraging the Subspace Embedding Theorem, we show that PDR achieves optimal convergence rates of $ \mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T}) $ for non-convex functions and $ \mathcal{O}(1/T) $ for strongly convex functions, where $ T $ denotes the number of iterations. Crucially, we mathematically demonstrate that this massive acceleration comes almost for free, merely inflating the inherent Byzantine error floor by a bounded, tunable factor of $ \frac{1+ε}{1-ε} $. Experimental results on benchmark datasets confirm that integrating PDR with existing aggregators yields orders of magnitude speedups in time efficiency while maintaining highly competitive convergence performance.

</details>


### [286] [Detecting Diffusion-Generated Time Series Under Generator Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28355)
*Zhi Wen Soi,Aditya Shankar,Gert Lek,Abele Mălan,Daniel Neider,Jian-Jia Chen,Lydia Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统比较了扩散生成时间序列的白盒与黑盒检测方法，发现简单的黑盒分类器显著优于从图像域迁移的白盒重构检测器（F1提升22.1%），揭示了该问题不能直接从图像域迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实与扩散生成时间序列的界限日益模糊，但在生成器未知的情况下，该领域的检测研究仍然不足，缺乏系统性探索。

Method: 对比两种检测范式：（1）白盒检测：基于重构的检测器，需访问生成器；（2）黑盒检测：直接使用现成分类器处理原始信号，无需生成器信息。

Result: 白盒方法在分布内表现良好，但在生成器偏移下失效；黑盒分类器表现优异，平均F1达79.2（相对提升22.1%），TPR@1%FPR达57.2。

Conclusion: 扩散生成时间序列检测不能直接迁移图像域方法，简单黑盒分类器在此任务上更有效，为该方向开辟了新的研究空间。

Abstract: The boundary between real and diffusion-generated time series is becoming increasingly difficult to draw, yet detection in this domain remains underexplored, especially when the generator is unknown. We compare white-box detection, which requires access to the generator, against black-box detection, which operates on the raw signal alone. The white-box approach, a reconstruction-based detector adapted from the image domain, works well in in-distribution but breaks down under generator shift: reconstruction-based detection in images succeeds because large generic generators provide a near-universal reconstruction prior, and no analogous generator exists for time series. In contrast, a simple off-the-shelf classifier used as a black-box detector performs remarkably well, achieving an average F1 of 79.2, a 22.1% relative improvement over the white-box approach, and a TPR@1%FPR of 57.2. Diffusion-generated time series detection is therefore not a direct transfer of the image domain problem. This work provides the first systematic exploration of white-box and black-box detection for diffusion-generated time series. We close by identifying several open and promising directions.

</details>


### [287] [Score Based Error Correcting Code Decoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28358)
*Alon Helvits,Eliya Nachmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SB-ECC，一种基于分数的纠错码软解码器，将解码建模为连续时间去噪过程，在42个测试设置中39个达到最佳BER，平均SNR增益0.17dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纠错码的实用软解码在不同码族和码块长度上仍具挑战性，现有方法需要SNR估计且难以平衡延迟与精度。

Method: 将解码问题转化为连续时间去噪过程，使用神经网络去噪器定义概率流ODE，将噪声信道观测逐步更新为有效码字，通过奇偶校验约束引导，在无时间/SNR条件下跨噪声级别训练。

Result: 在42个码/SNR设置中39个取得最佳BER，平均SNR增益0.17dB，最大增益0.46dB；将ODE求解器从Euler替换为DPM，在保持-ln(BER)的同时平均减少8.86%的端到端解码时间（最高12.82%）。

Conclusion: SB-ECC成功实现了无需SNR估计的统一解码框架，支持通过ODE求解器预算直接控制延迟-精度权衡，在性能和效率上均优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Error-correcting codes enable reliable communication, yet practical soft decoding remains challenging across code families and block lengths. We propose SB-ECC, a score-based decoder that casts decoding as continuous-time denoising. A neural denoiser defines a probability-flow ordinary differential equation (ODE) that iteratively updates the noisy channel observation toward a valid codeword, guided by parity constraints. The model is trained across noise levels without time/SNR conditioning, enabling inference without SNR estimation and supporting a direct latency accuracy trade off controlled by the ODE solver budget. We use the raw signed channel observation as input for learning a continuous denoising field. Across 42 code/SNR settings, SB-ECC achieves the best BER in 39/42 entries, with an average SNR gain of 0.17dB and a maximum gain of 0.46dB over the strongest competing baseline, we showed that swapping the solver from Euler to DPM preserves -ln(BER) while reducing end-to-end decoding time by 8.86% on average (up to 12.82%).

</details>


### [288] [LEIA: Learned Environment for Interactive Architected Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28368)
*Haiqian Yang,Yuan Cao,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了LEIA，一种面向工程材料设计的物理世界模型，可实时预测三维材料的变形和应力场，并引入MicroPlate基准进行评估，实现了高效的材料设计搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型虽在游戏环境和机器人操作中取得成功，但在物理工程领域仍面临挑战：真实材料具有非线性本构律、历史依赖的内部状态、惯性动力学特性，以及跨多个长度尺度的分层结构，这些复杂特性使得物理工程问题难以被现有的世界模型有效处理。

Method: 提出了LEIA（Learned Environment for Interactive Architected materials）世界模型，该模型能够处理大型三维非结构化网格，并生成对用户指定载荷的自回归响应。同时引入了MicroPlate基准数据集，涵盖两类微结构建模方式：显式解析三维几何的架构化晶格，以及通过内部自由度隐式建模微结构变化的均质板。使用该基准对LEIA和四种基线方法进行了系统评估。

Result: LEIA成功实现了工程师逐步施加载荷并实时观察变形和应力场的能力。在MicroPlate基准测试中，LEIA在两种微结构建模方式下均表现良好。更重要的是，LEIA能够实现高效的候选生成和排序，用于架构化材料的从头设计，并通过有限元仿真验证了应力准确的候选排序能力。

Conclusion: LEIA证明了世界模型在物理工程领域的可行性，特别是在架构化材料的交互式设计和快速搜索方面展现出巨大潜力。该工作为材料科学中的代理模型引导设计提供了新的解决方案，填补了世界模型在复杂物理系统应用方面的空白。

Abstract: World models have enabled interactive exploration of game environments and robotic manipulation, but physical engineering remains beyond their reach: real materials exhibit nonlinear constitutive laws, carry history-dependent internal state, undergo inertial dynamics, and may possess hierarchical structures spanning multiple length scales. We present LEIA (Learned Environment for Interactive Architected materials), a world model that lets engineers apply boundary conditions step by step and observe the resulting deformation and stress fields in real time. LEIA handles large three-dimensional unstructured meshes and generates autoregressive responses to user-specified loading. We introduce MicroPlate, a benchmark of architected plates spanning two regimes of microstructure modeling: architected lattices that resolve microstructure explicitly through three-dimensional geometry, and a homogeneous plate where microstructural change is modeled implicitly through internal degrees of freedom. MicroPlate is used to assess LEIA alongside four baseline methods across both regimes. Finally, we demonstrate that LEIA enables efficient candidate generation and ranking for fast surrogate-guided search for de novo designs of architected materials, with stress-accurate candidate ranking validated by finite element ground truth.

</details>


### [289] [Teacher-Student Representational Alignment for Reinforcement Learning-Driven Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28372)
*Meraj Mammadov,Pedro Zuidberg Dos Martires,Johannes Andreas Stork*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新算法，通过学习共享嵌入空间来训练可模仿的教师策略，从而减少模仿学习中由于教师使用特权状态信息而产生的不可约模仿差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人领域的高维观测空间中，当教师策略可以使用学生无法从观测中推断的特权状态信息时，单独训练教师和学生会导致不可约的模仿差距。现有的通过强化学习微调来改善学生性能的方法通常需要全新的训练设置。

Method: 提出一种新算法，学习一个共享嵌入空间，该空间隐藏智能体特定的观测信息，从而通过构造训练出可模仿的教师策略。在训练教师策略的同时，使用自监督对比学习训练共享嵌入空间，并通过限制梯度更新编码器网络来防止提取私有信息。

Result: 在多个示例领域进行评估，并与最先进的基线方法进行比较，结果表明该算法能够实现更高的学生性能，并显著减少模仿差距。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法通过构造可模仿的教师策略，有效解决了模仿学习中的不可约模仿差距问题，为高维观测空间中的模仿学习提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) from a state-based reinforcement learning (RL) policy is a common approach to overcome the curse of dimensionality in complex and high-dimensional observation spaces prevalent in robotics. This paper addresses the irreducible imitation gap that emerges when teacher and student are learned in isolation, and the teacher policy has the liberty to rely on privileged state information that the student cannot infer from its observations. Instead of improving poor student performance with RL finetuning after IL, which often requires a whole new training setup, we propose a novel algorithm which learns a shared embedding space that hides agent-specific observations and thus trains imitable teacher policies by construction. We train the shared embedding space with self-supervised contrastive learning in parallel to the teacher policy and prevent it from extracting private information by limiting its gradients from updating the encoder networks. We perform evaluations on several example domains and compare to state-of-the-art baselines showing that our algorithm enables higher student performance with substantially reduced imitation gap.

</details>


### [290] [Meta-Attention: Bayesian Per-Token Routing for Efficient Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28384)
*Alan Ferrari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Meta-Attention框架，通过贝叶斯元控制器动态路由每个token到最优注意力机制（全注意力、线性注意力或滑动窗口注意力），在保持性能的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer架构对所有token和序列位置统一应用单一注意力机制，忽略了局部上下文和计算预算的差异，导致计算效率低下。现有路由方法存在路由坍塌问题且缺乏对计算成本与任务性能的权衡。

Method: 提出Meta-Attention框架，核心是贝叶斯Meta-Controller：将每个token的机制选择建模为计算感知的Dirichlet先验下的后验推断问题；路由权重通过摊销变分后验q(alpha|x_t;phi)生成，采用ELBO目标联合优化任务性能和注意力机制成本；产生原理性的路由不确定性估计，指导软到硬路由转换。

Result: Tiny LM基准测试Phase 1实验表明：贝叶斯控制器的学习路由分布暗示硬路由下归一化FLOP成本为25.1%，相比无先验基准的59.3%降低34.2个百分点；路由熵从55.8%降至43.3%（降低12.5个百分点），证明Dirichlet先验有效防止路由坍塌，而非贝叶斯模型默认退化为全注意力。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现原理性的路由决策，在不使用临时负载均衡损失的情况下缓解路由坍塌，获得比确定性或无先验学习路由更好的计算-性能权衡，且开销可忽略不计，为Transformer架构的自适应注意力选择提供了新的贝叶斯解决方案。

Abstract: Standard transformer architectures apply a single attention mechanism uniformly across all tokens and sequence positions, irrespective of local context or computational budget. We propose Meta-Attention, a framework that dynamically routes each token to the most appropriate attention strategy -- full softmax attention, linear (kernel) attention, or sliding-window local attention -- via a Bayesian Meta-Controller. Unlike prior routing approaches that use deterministic or prior-free learned routing, the Meta-Controller treats per-token mechanism selection as posterior inference under a compute-aware Dirichlet prior: routing weights are the output of an amortised variational posterior q(alpha | x_t; phi) trained with an Evidence Lower Bound (ELBO) objective that jointly encodes task performance and attention-mechanism cost. This design produces principled routing uncertainty estimates that govern the soft-to-hard routing transition, mitigates routing collapse without ad hoc load-balancing losses, and yields better compute-performance trade-offs than deterministic or prior-free learned routing at negligible overhead. Phase 1 empirical results on a Tiny LM benchmark confirm core predictions: the Bayesian controller's learned routing distribution implies a projected normalised FLOP cost of 25.1% under hard routing, vs. 59.3% for the prior-free baseline (-34.2 pp), and reduces routing entropy from 55.8% to 43.3% (-12.5 pp), demonstrating that the Dirichlet prior prevents routing collapse while the non-Bayesian model defaults to full attention. We present the Bayesian architecture, ELBO training objective, and a Phase 1 PyTorch prototype validating forward-pass correctness, posterior diversity, and a controlled ablation against a prior-free baseline. Code available at: https://github.com/KFEAL/meta-attention

</details>


### [291] [CLANE: Continual Learning of Actions on Neuromorphic Hardware from Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28387)
*Elvin Hajizada,Michael Neumeier,Edward Paxon Frady,Yulia Sandamirskaya,Axel von Arnim,Bing Li,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLANE是首个在神经形态硬件（Intel Loihi 2）上实现事件相机动作持续学习的完整系统，在50类真实数据集上达到70.4%准确率，相比边缘GPU基线实现100倍以上能耗降低和16倍延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴AR/VR和机器人应用需要在设备端进行持续学习以识别新颖人类动作且不遗忘已知类别，这对隐私保护和低延迟适应至关重要。事件相机具有稀疏异步输出特性，天然适配神经形态处理，但此前尚无系统在神经形态硬件上部署事件驱动的动作持续学习流程。

Method: 在Intel Loihi 2上端到端部署CLANE系统，结合脉冲2D CNN进行时空特征提取，采用CLP-SNN作为片上学习头部，并通过新颖的时序聚合层和定点归一化层扩展至动作片段处理。在THU E-ACT-50数据集上进行跨平台基准测试验证。

Result: 在50类真实场景数据集THU E-ACT-50的持续学习任务中达到70.4%准确率；相比CNN+GRU+CLP边缘GPU基线，在相同算法条件下实现超过100倍能耗降低和16倍延迟降低。

Conclusion: CLANE证明了在神经形态硬件上实现端到端事件相机动作持续学习的可行性，为隐私保护和低延迟的设备端学习应用提供了高效解决方案，显著优于传统边缘GPU实现。

Abstract: Recognizing and continuously learning novel human actions without forgetting prior classes is a requirement for emerging AR/VR and robotics applications. For these applications, both on-device processing and learning are essential for privacy and low-latency adaptation. Event cameras address the efficiency of visual sensing with sparse, asynchronous output that is naturally compatible with neuromorphic processing. Yet no prior system has deployed a continual on-device learning pipeline for event-based action recognition using neuromorphic hardware. We present CLANE, Continual Learning of Actions on Neuromorphic Hardware from Event Cameras, deployed end-to-end on Intel Loihi 2. CLANE combines a spiking 2D CNN for spatiotemporal feature extraction with CLP-SNN as its on-chip learning head, extended to action clips via a Temporal Aggregation Layer and a fixed-point Normalization Layer, both novel Loihi 2 modules. On THU E-ACT-50, a 50-class dataset captured under real-world conditions, CLANE achieves 70.4% accuracy in a continual learning task while delivering more than 100x energy reduction and 16x lower latency over a sequential CNN+GRU+CLP edge GPU baseline, validated through iso-algorithm cross-platform benchmarking across three evaluation levels.

</details>


### [292] [ADWIN: Adaptive Windows for Horizon-Aware On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28396)
*Kun Liang,Chenming Tang,Clive Bai,Weijie Liu,Saiyong Yang,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADWIN提出了一种自适应窗口的在线策略蒸馏框架，通过动态决定训练轨迹长度，在保持或提升准确率的同时将训练成本降低了高达4.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线策略蒸馏（OPD）采用完整回滚训练存在两个问题：每次更新都需要昂贵的完整计算，且过度监督边际价值较低的后期位置。研究发现学生生成的轨迹可能偏离教师偏好的延续，而对齐的前缀可能已经保留了长视界的更新方向。

Method: 提出ADWIN自适应窗口框架，将回滚长度视为在线可接受性决策：在短的教师锚定前缀上训练，同时使用延迟的完整回滚探测来审计前缀与完整轨迹的对齐程度，并结合陈旧性控制来适应下一个视界。

Result: 在单任务、多任务和强到弱设置的数学和代码推理基准测试中，ADWIN在准确率-计算权衡上优于完整回滚OPD和基于前缀的基线方法，将端到端训练成本降低多达4.1倍，同时达到相当或更好的准确率。

Conclusion: ADWIN通过自适应调整训练视界，成功解决了标准OPD中计算成本高和监督分配不当的问题，为在线策略蒸馏提供了一种更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) transfers reasoning behavior by training a student on teacher feedback along student-generated trajectories, but standard full-rollout training ties every update to a costly completion and can over-allocate supervision to late positions with low marginal value for the current student. We revisit this assumption through the useful supervision horizon: student-induced rollouts can drift from teacher-preferred continuations, while aligned prefixes may already preserve the long-horizon OPD update direction. We propose ADWIN, an adaptive-window framework for OPD that treats rollout length as an online admissibility decision, training on short teacher-anchored prefixes while using delayed full-rollout probes to audit prefix--full alignment and adapt the next horizon with staleness control. Across math and code reasoning benchmarks in single-task, multi-task, and strong-to-weak settings, ADWIN improves the accuracy--compute trade-off over full-rollout OPD and prefix-based baselines, reducing end-to-end training cost by up to 4.1 times while achieving comparable or better accuracy.

</details>


### [293] [Revisiting Metafeatures to Explain Model Differences on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28418)
*Markus Herre,Andrej Tschalzev,Sascha Marton,Christian Bartelt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现，利用数据集元特征难以稳健地解释表格数据预测任务中不同模型家族之间的性能差距，且无法有效预测最佳模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着表格基础模型的兴起以及传统模型在许多任务上依然表现良好，为表格数据集选择合适的模型仍然是一个困难的问题。研究者希望探究数据集元特征是否能够解释表格预测任务中不同模型家族之间的性能差距。

Method: 使用TabArena基准测试结果，分析数据集级别的性能差距，并将其与模型无关的数据集描述符相关联。采用严格统计检验并进行错误发现率控制，同时进行了留一数据集分析来评估元特征预测器的泛化能力。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 对于神经网络与树模型的差距，没有元特征能通过错误发现率控制；(2) 对于非基础模型与基础模型的差距，存在一个稳健的关联但在留一数据集预测中无法泛化；(3) 对于TabICLv2与TabPFN-2.6的比较，一个稳健的关联确实改善了保留数据集的预测。此外，元特征预测器未能显著优于简单基线。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明表格数据集具有高度异质性，全局元特征方法在TabArena的51个数据集上不够稳健，无法提供有效的解释。这揭示了当前基于元特征的模型选择方法的局限性。

Abstract: With the rise of tabular foundation models alongside traditional models still performing well on many tasks, choosing the right model for a tabular dataset remains difficult. We investigate whether dataset meta-features can explain performance gaps between model families on tabular prediction tasks. Using the TabArena benchmark results, we analyze dataset-level performance gaps and relate them to model-agnostic dataset descriptors. After strict statistical tests with false discovery control, we find that (1) for neural network vs. tree gaps, no meta-feature survives false discovery control, (2) for non-foundation vs. foundation model gaps, one association is robust but does not generalize when tested in leave-one-dataset-out prediction, and (3) for TabICLv2 vs. TabPFN-2.6, one robust association also improves held-out prediction. Furthermore, we conduct a leave-one-dataset-out analysis and find that meta-feature predictors fail to improve meaningfully over a simple baseline. Overall, our results show the heterogeneity of tabular datasets and that global meta-feature approaches are not robust enough to offer explanations on the 51 TabArena datasets.

</details>


### [294] [Conveyance: A Versatile Framework for Learning in Structured Class Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28420)
*Yasser Taha,Grégoire Montavon,Nils Körber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了\textsc{Conveyance}方法，一种针对结构化类别空间的分类方法和损失函数，通过最大化不同类别分区上的两个独立边际，有效利用类别间的结构关系，在层级分类、有序回归和多实例学习任务中达到或超越专用基线模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习架构虽然能够处理复杂数据，但常用的损失函数（如交叉熵）大多是结构无关的，这种"类别对称"的性质限制了模型利用类别间结构关系的能力，特别是在面对结构化噪声时。因此需要一种能够有效编码类别间图状关系的新方法。

Method: 提出\textsc{Conveyance}分类方法及其关联损失函数。该损失函数通过在两个不同的类别分区上最大化独立的边际来工作，同时保持单调性和部分凸性等形式化性质。用户可以编码类别间的图状关系，而无需定义复杂的联合分布或手动调整效用矩阵。

Result: 在层级分类、有序回归和多实例学习三个任务上验证了方法的有效性和通用性。\textsc{Conveyance}在这些任务中要么匹配、要么超越了专用基线模型的性能。

Conclusion: \textsc{Conveyance}为结构化类别空间提供了一个统一的解决方案，能够有效利用类别间的结构关系，在多个任务上展现出良好的通用性和性能，避免了定义复杂联合分布或手动调参的需求。

Abstract: While machine learning (ML) architectures have evolved rapidly to account for complex data, loss functions like cross-entropy remain mostly structure-agnostic in many real-world applications. However, the `class-symmetric' nature of these standard losses fundamentally limits the ability of ML models to exploit structural relationships between classes, particularly when facing structured noise. We propose \textsc{Conveyance}, a new classification approach and associated loss function tailored to structured class spaces. It allows users to encode graph-like relations between classes without having to define complex joint distributions or manually tune utility matrices.Technically, our loss function operates by maximizing two separate margins over distinct class partitions, while preserving formal properties such as monotonicity and partial convexity. We demonstrate the versatility and effectiveness of our method by applying it to hierarchical classification, ordinal regression, and multiple instance learning. Across these tasks, \textsc{Conveyance} either matches or exceeds the performance of specialized baselines, thereby offering a unified solution for structured class spaces.

</details>


### [295] [Latent Diffusion for Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28427)
*Alberte Heering Estad,Ignacio Peis,Jes Frellsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段潜在扩散模型用于缺失数据填补，在高达50%缺失率下仍保持高质量生成，显著优于像素空间扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的缺失数据填补方法直接在数据空间操作，当训练数据严重不完整时性能显著下降。本文旨在探究将扩散过程迁移到学习的潜在表示空间是否能提升对缺失完全随机（MCAR）损坏的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段使用鲁棒VAE填补器从不完整观测中学习紧凑语义特征；第二阶段在所得潜在空间中训练扩散模型。在不同训练缺失率下，与相同不完整数据设置下的像素空间扩散模型进行受控对比实验。

Result: 潜在扩散模型在高达50%缺失率下仍保持高样本质量并维持稳定，而像素空间扩散模型随缺失率增加性能逐步退化。在下游填补任务中，潜在扩散模型相比像素空间扩散模型取得持续更优性能。潜在空间建模有效缓解了零填补输入引起的伪影放大问题。

Conclusion: 潜在空间建模为不完整数据学习提供了更鲁棒的生成先验，缓解了零填补输入导致的伪影放大。潜在扩散模型是缺失数据问题中像素空间扩散的强力且实用的替代方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as powerful generative approaches for missing-data imputation, yet most existing methods operate directly in data space and degrade when training data are heavily incomplete. We investigate whether shifting diffusion to a learned latent representation improves robustness under missing-completely-at-random (MCAR) corruption. To this end, we propose a two-stage framework: a robust VAE-based imputer first learns compact semantic features from incomplete observations, and a diffusion model is then trained in the resulting latent space. Across training missing rates, we perform a controlled comparison against pixel-space diffusion models under the same incomplete-data setting. The latent diffusion model maintains high sample quality and remains stable up to 50\% missingness, while pixel-space diffusion degrades progressively as missingness increases. For downstream imputation, latent diffusion also achieves consistently better performance than pixel-space diffusion. These findings indicate that latent-space modeling mitigates artifact amplification from zero-imputed inputs and provides a more robust generative prior for incomplete-data learning. Overall, our results support latent diffusion as a strong and practically useful alternative to pixel-space diffusion for missing-data problems.

</details>


### [296] [Bilinear Coordinate Alignment for Training-Free Task-Vector Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28444)
*Jungyong Son,Jinwook Jung,Minhee Park,Sungyong Baik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BiCo，一种无需训练的任务向量迁移框架，通过对齐双线性坐标来实现跨模型的知识迁移，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练模型经过微调后获得的专业知识无法直接迁移到新版本的基础模型上，因为任务向量与原模型的参数化绑定，重新微调成本高昂。现有方法通过匹配激活值或梯度来迁移任务向量，但由于仅利用部分对应关系，性能仍显著落后于直接微调。

Method: 本文重新审视任务向量的形成过程，证明了任务向量可表示为输入侧激活值与输出侧梯度的双线性交互累积。基于此发现，将任务向量迁移建模为双空间对齐问题，提出BiCo框架。该框架在两个空间中利用单个前向-反向传播（在小校准集上）估计正交Procrustes映射，无需任何参数更新即可实现迁移。

Result: 在广泛的计算机视觉和自然语言处理基准测试中，BiCo在模型宽度、深度和预训练配置不同的各种模型上，一致性地超越了现有的任务向量迁移方法。

Conclusion: 通过双线性坐标对齐进行任务向量迁移是高效且有效的，BiCo作为无需训练的方法，成功缩小了迁移方法与直接微调之间的性能差距，为跨模型知识迁移提供了新思路。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large-scale pre-trained models is a recent prevalent paradigm for adapting general representations to specialized tasks. However, when a new version of a pre-trained model becomes available, expertise acquired through fine-tuning cannot be directly reused because it is tied to the parameterization of the original model, requiring another costly fine-tuning. To address this inefficiency, recent work uses task vectors, defined as the parameter difference between a fine-tuned model and its base model, to transfer expertise across models. While existing methods bridge disparate models by matching activations or gradients, a significant performance gap remains relative to direct fine-tuning, suggesting that these partial correspondences are insufficient. In this work, instead of viewing a task vector merely as a parameter offset, we revisit the formation of task vectors and show that they can be derived as accumulated bilinear interactions between input-side activations and output-side gradients. Motivated by this observation, we formulate task-vector transfer as a dual-space alignment problem and propose BiCo, a training-free framework for transferring task vectors through Bilinear Coordinate alignment. BiCo estimates orthogonal Procrustes mappings in both spaces using a single forward-backward pass on a small calibration set, without any parameter update. Across extensive computer vision and natural language processing benchmarks, BiCo consistently outperforms existing transfer methods across models that differ in width, depth, and pre-training configuration.

</details>


### [297] [Mitigating Adaptive Attacks against Reasoning Models with Activation Consistency Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28467)
*Avidan Shah,Jannik Brinkmann,Rico Angell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一致性训练作为推理模型对抗性防御的方法，发现激活级一致性训练（ACT）在防御越狱和提示注入方面表现优异，其防御机制在激活空间中呈现线性可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型推理能力增强，其扩展的思维链为对抗性越狱攻击和提示注入防御带来了新的复杂性，需要开发更有效的防御方法。

Method: 研究一致性训练方法，包括输出级（BCT）和激活级（ACT）两种变体，通过在干净提示和对抗性重写上强制相同行为来构建防御机制，仅需自监督的配对数据。

Result: ACT与其他基于训练的防御方法相比具有竞争力，对自适应攻击更具鲁棒性。ACT的防御机制在助手轮次边界处编码为激活空间中大致线性的偏移，可恢复单一控制方向来控制推理模型的拒绝行为，且对良性输入影响最小。即使模型的思维链被未防御基础模型的合规轨迹替换，ACT仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 监督内部表示是推理模型安全训练中一种出人意料有效且可解释的方法，为多种形式的安全训练提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: As LLMs gain stronger reasoning capabilities, their extended chain-of-thought introduces new degrees of complexity for defending against adversarial jailbreaks and prompt injection. We study consistency training, a family of fine-tuning objectives that enforce identical behavior on clean prompts and adversarial rewrites, and evaluate its two main variants, output-level (BCT) and activation-level (ACT), across five reasoning models. We formulate both methods as a prompt injection defense and find ACT to be competitive with other training-based defenses while requiring only self-supervised pairs of clean and wrapped prompts. Our experiments also generalize both techniques within the jailbreak setting, demonstrating that ACT remains more robust to adaptive attacks. We also provide mechanistic evidence that ACT's defense against jailbreaks is encoded as a roughly linear shift in activation space at the assistant-turn boundary. After ACT training, we can recover a single steering direction that controls refusal on reasoning models with minimal effect on benign inputs. We find that ACT remains robust even when the model's chain-of-thought is replaced with a compliant trace from the undefended base model, pivoting to refuse prefilled jailbreaks. Together, these results suggest that supervising internal representations is a surprisingly effective and interpretable approach to various forms of safety training in reasoning models.

</details>


### [298] [Fitting Unknown Number of Hyperplanes with Manifold Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28501)
*Zhiqin Cheng,Yu Zhan,Mingjin Zhang,Lingbo Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流形优化的两阶段算法，用于解决未知数量超平面拟合问题，在几何精度和鲁棒性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟合未知数量的超平面是机器学习中的一个基础且具有挑战性的问题，该问题具有非凸性、非可微性和模型阶数未知等特点。现有方法常常陷入局部最优或缺乏几何一致性，因此需要一种新的方法来克服这些限制。

Method: 提出了一种基于流形优化的新型框架。首先将问题重构为单位球面流形上的无监督学习任务，有效处理非凸约束并线性化距离测量。然后提出两阶段流形优化算法：第一阶段采用带有重尾核的黎曼期望最大化过程来鲁棒地估计后验概率；第二阶段在软估计收敛后，概率权重退化为硬匹配，生成严格满足几何定义的精确局部最优。此外，引入投影密度估计策略进行初始化以促进全局收敛。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在几何精度和鲁棒性方面均优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将超平面拟合问题转化为流形优化问题并采用两阶段算法，成功解决了传统方法面临的局部最优和几何一致性问题，为该领域提供了一个有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Fitting an unknown number of hyperplanes to data is a fundamental yet challenging problem in machine learning, characterized by its non-convexity, non-differentiability, and unknown model order. Existing approaches often struggle with local optima or lack geometric consistency. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework based on Manifold Optimization. We reformulate the problem as an unsupervised learning task on the unit sphere manifold $\mathcal{S}^{\textbf{dim}-1}$. This formulation effectively handles the non-convex constraints and linearizes the distance measurement, rendering the gradient descent tractable. We propose a Two-Stage Manifold Optimization algorithm. In Phase I, we employ a Riemannian Expectation-Maximization process with a heavy-tailed kernel to robustly estimate posterior probabilities, effectively resolving the ambiguities of point distribution between intersecting hyperplanes. In Phase II, upon convergence of the soft estimates, the probabilistic weights degenerate into hard matching, generating a precise local optimum that strictly satisfies the geometric definition. Furthermore, we introduce a projected density estimation strategy for initialization to facilitate global convergence by significantly reducing the feature description space and search complexity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both geometric accuracy and robustness.

</details>


### [299] [Universal Time Series Generation with Neural Controlled Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28507)
*Torben Berndt,Elyes Farjallah,Leif Seute,Raeid Saqur,Benjamin Walker,Jan Stühmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将状态空间模型(SSMs)的序列普遍性理论扩展到生成式时间序列建模，证明了结构化线性受控微分方程(SLiCEs)是通用的时间序列生成器，并提出了生成式SLiCEs(G-SLiCEs)模型用于路径空间上的流匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于状态空间模型序列普遍性的研究主要集中在判别式场景，而本文旨在将这一视角扩展到生成式时间序列建模领域，填补了连续时间方法在生成式建模方面的理论空白。

Method: 基于结构化线性受控微分方程(SLiCEs)，理论上证明其作为通用时间序列生成器的能力，可以逼近紧潜在集上连续因果前推的诱导路径法则；在此基础上提出生成式SLiCEs(G-SLiCEs)模型，实现路径空间上的流匹配。

Result: 实验证明表达能力提升能够改善概率预测和下游任务的性能，同时保持连续时间模型的优势，如能够泛化到任意观测网格；在不规则网格情况下表现尤为突出，而固定网格模型在此场景下往往表现不佳。

Conclusion: G-SLiCEs成功地将SSMs的理论优势扩展到生成式建模领域，在保持连续时间模型灵活性的同时提升了表达能力，为不规则采样时间序列的生成式建模提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Recent work on the sequence universality of State Space Models (SSMs) has introduced efficient, maximally expressive continuous-time approaches for time-series modelling. While these works focus on discriminative settings, we extend this perspective to generative time-series modelling by proving that maximally expressive Structured Linear Controlled Differential Equations (SLiCEs) are universal time-series generators, in the sense that they can approximate the induced path laws of continuous causal pushforwards on compact latent sets in $W_\infty$. Building on these theoretical results, we propose Generative SLiCEs (G-SLiCEs), a maximally expressive continuous-time model for flow matching on path-space. Empirically, we show that expressivity improves performance in probabilistic forecasting and downstream tasks, while retaining the advantages of continuous-time models such as generalising to arbitrary observation grids. This is particularly beneficial for irregular grids, where fixed-grid models often struggle.

</details>


### [300] [Learning Theory of the SVRG: Generalization and Convergence Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28513)
*Yunwen Lei,Zimeng Wang,Xiaoming Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次通过算法稳定性视角对代表性方差缩减方法SVRG进行了非平凡的泛化分析，建立了凸和强凸设定下的尖锐稳定性界，并得到了最优的超额总体风险界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的方差缩减方法理论研究主要集中于收敛性分析，而泛化行为研究严重不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过算法稳定性的视角研究SVRG方法的泛化性能。

Method: 将SVRG更新分解为类似SGD的步骤加上一个零均值校正项，引入新颖的李雅普诺夫函数来吸收由参考点引起的额外梯度项，利用算法结构建立依赖于数据（融入沿轨迹的训练误差）的稳定性界。

Result: 在凸和强凸设定下建立了SVRG的尖锐稳定性界，这些界是数据依赖的（融入了沿轨迹的训练误差），并推导出了两种设定下的最优超额总体风险界。该分析框架可推广至其他VR方法（如SAGA）。

Conclusion: 本文阐明了优化与泛化之间的相互作用，开发了可推广至其他方差缩减方法的分析框架，并通过SAGA方法验证了框架的通用性，为理解VR方法的泛化行为提供了新视角。

Abstract: Variance reduction (VR) methods employ stochastic gradients with decreasing variance, and they have been widely applied to solve large-scale optimization problems in machine learning because of their efficiency. Existing theoretical studies of VR methods are mainly focused on the convergence analysis, leaving the generalization behavior largely unexplored. In this paper, we bridge this gap by developing the first non-vacuous generalization analysis of the representative VR method: Stochastic Variance Reduced Gradient (SVRG), through the lens of algorithmic stability. In particular, we establish sharp stability bounds of the SVRG in both convex and strongly convex settings by exploiting its algorithmic structure. The obtained bounds are data-dependent, because the training errors are incorporated along the trajectory. Our analysis clarifies the interplay between optimization and generalization, leading to optimal excess population risk bounds in both settings. Our approach differs substantially from existing analyses of stochastic algorithms in the sense that we decompose the SVRG update as an SGD-like step plus a zero-mean correction term and then introduce novel Lyapunov functions to absorb the additional gradient terms induced by the reference points. Our analytical framework can be generalized to other VR methods, and we demonstrate the generalization by the well-known Stochastic Average Gradient Accelerated (SAGA) method.

</details>


### [301] [Stochastic Gradient Descent with Momentum is Algorithmically Stable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28517)
*Yunwen Lei,Zimeng Wang,Xiaoming Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从算法稳定性角度对带动量的随机梯度下降（SGDM）进行了全面的泛化分析，建立了紧的平均模型稳定性界限，并推导出了Polyak和Nesterov动量方案的最优超额总体风险界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGDM是机器学习中最广泛使用的优化算法之一，但其泛化性质尚不充分理解。特别是，虽然动量可以加速训练，但存在猜想认为它可能降低泛化能力。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究SGDM是否以及何时能够良好地泛化到未见数据。

Method: 引入了一个广义SGDM框架，涵盖Polyak和Nesterov两种动量方案；通过算法稳定性的视角建立平滑凸问题的紧的平均模型稳定性界限；进一步推导广义SGDM的优化误差界限；结合稳定性分析和优化误差界限获得最优超额总体风险界限。

Result: 建立了紧的平均模型稳定性界限，该界限利用了轨迹上的小优化误差界限，适用于[0,1)区间内的任意动量参数，且不要求通常假设的损失函数的Lipschitz性质。推导出了Polyak和Nesterov动量方案的最优超额总体风险界限。

Conclusion: 本文提供了SGDM的全面泛化分析，证明了动量方法在适当的条件下可以实现良好的泛化性能，为理解动量在优化和泛化中的作用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent with momentum (SGDM) is one of the most widely used optimization algorithms in machine learning. While optimization properties of SGDM have been extensively studied in the literature, it remains insufficiently understood whether and when SGDM can generalize well to unseen data. In particular, it has been conjectured that while momentum accelerates training, it may degrade generalization. In this paper, we close this gap by developing a comprehensive generalization analysis of SGDM through the lens of algorithmic stability. More specifically, we introduce a generalized SGDM framework that encompasses both Polyak's and Nesterov's momentum schemes, and establish tight on-average model stability bounds for smooth and convex problems. Notably, the obtained bounds exploit small optimization error bounds along the trajectory, apply to any momentum parameter in the interval $[0, 1)$, and do not require the commonly assumed Lipschitzness of loss functions. We further derive optimization error bounds for the generalized SGDM, and combine them with our generalization analyses to obtain optimal excess population risk bounds for SGDM with both Polyak's and Nesterov's momentum.

</details>


### [302] [Stabilizing distribution-free probabilistic forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28531)
*Jente Van Belle,Honglin Wen,Wouter Verbeke,Pierre Pinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在概率时间序列预测模型训练中同时考虑预测质量和稳定性的方法，通过使用神经网络参数化的回归样条生成稳定的预测条件分位数函数，有效降低预测不稳定性且不明显损失预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步 ahead 预测通常会随着新观测数据的获得而更新，因为较短的预测视界通常能提高预测质量。然而这种改进以预测不稳定性为代价，即对同一目标时期的预测变异性。这种不稳定性可能触发基于预测制定的计划的代价高昂的变更，并可能削弱对预测系统的信任。因此需要在预测质量和稳定性之间进行权衡控制。

Method: 提出一种使用回归样条生成稳定预测条件分位数函数的方法，该样条由神经网络参数化。这种方法允许质量和稳定性的联合优化，因为它能够直接惩罚由预测更新引起的不相似性。此外，它允许对稳定预测分布的不同部分（如中心部分与尾部）分配不同的重要性，以专注于与预期下游用途最相关的部分（如库存管理的上尾部分）。

Result: 在两个具有不同统计属性的数据集上进行经验评估，结果表明该方法可以有效降低预测不稳定性而不会明显损失预测质量，并且可以将稳定化努力定向到预测分布的特定部分。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法成功实现了预测质量与稳定性之间的可控权衡，能够根据下游应用需求有针对性地稳定预测分布的关键部分，为实际应用中的预测系统提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-step-ahead forecasts are often updated as new observations become available, since shorter forecast horizons typically improve forecast quality. However, such improvements come at the cost of forecast instability, i.e., variability in forecasts for the same target period. This instability can trigger costly changes to plans formulated based on the forecasts and may erode trust in the forecasting system. In this work, we integrate forecast stability alongside forecast quality into the training of distribution-free probabilistic time-series forecasting models, allowing us to control this trade-off. We propose a method for generating stabilized forecasted conditional quantile functions using regression splines parameterized by a neural network. This approach enables joint optimization of quality and stability, as it allows us to directly penalize dissimilarities arising from forecast updates. Furthermore, it allows assigning varying importance to stabilizing different parts of the forecast distributions (e.g., central parts vs. tails) to focus on the parts most relevant for the intended downstream use (e.g., the upper tail for inventory management). We empirically evaluate the proposed method on two datasets with different statistical properties and show that it can effectively reduce forecast instability without a substantial loss in forecast quality, and that it can target stabilization effort toward specific parts of the forecast distributions.

</details>


### [303] [Semi-Supervised Hypothesis Testing by Betting on Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28533)
*Yaniv Tenzer,Elad Tolochinsky,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于博弈的测试框架，利用未标记数据的预测来增强序贯假设检验的功效，在标签偏移或概念偏移条件下保持随时有效性，即使预测不准确也能获得比基线方法（包括预测驱动推断）更高的检验功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，我们往往只能获得有限的标记样本，但有大量未标记数据可用。传统方法无法有效利用这些未标记数据来增强关于Y分布或条件分布Y|X的假设检验。本文旨在解决如何利用未标记数据和预测模型来提升序贯假设检验的功效这一关键问题。

Method: 提出测试-博弈框架，构建e-统计量并基于此设计序贯检验方法。该方法结合有限的标记数据和额外的未标记数据，利用对未标记数据的预测来增强检验。在标签偏移或概念偏移等标准分布假设下，证明了检验的随时有效性（anytime validity）。关键地，该方法在预测不准确时仍保持其理论性质。

Result: 对于二元数据，证明e-统计量具有非平凡的功效。通过仿真实验和大语言模型评估应用，展示了相比基线方法（包括预测驱动推断）的功效提升。这些优势在未标记数据相对有限以及预测准确度较低（由于X和Y之间相关性弱）的情况下仍然存在。

Conclusion: 所提出的测试-博弈框架成功地将未标记数据整合到序贯假设检验中，在保持统计有效性的同时显著提升了检验功效。该方法对预测误差具有鲁棒性，为实际应用中有限标记数据场景下的统计推断提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce a testing-by-betting framework that leverages predictions on unlabeled data to enhance the power of sequential hypothesis testing. Given limited samples from the joint distribution of $(X,Y)$, and additional unlabeled samples from the marginal of $X$, we ask how unlabeled data can be used to hypothesize about the distribution of $Y$, and the conditional distribution of $Y\mid X$. We introduce an e-statistic and use it to construct a sequential test. Under standard distributional assumptions -- label shift or concept shift -- we establish that the test is anytime valid. Furthermore, we show that for binary data, the e-statistic has non-trivial power. Crucially, our approach retains these properties even when the underlying predictions are inaccurate. Through simulations and applications to large language models evaluation, we demonstrate power gains over baseline approaches, including prediction-powered inference. These gains persist even with relatively limited unlabeled data and when predictions have low accuracy due to weak correlation between $X$ and $Y$.

</details>


### [304] [High Performance, Low Reliability: Uncertainty Benchmarking for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28554)
*José Lucas De Melo Costa,Fabrice Popineau,Arpad Rimmel,Bich-Liên Doan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 表格基础模型虽然预测性能优越，但不确定性量化能力不足，存在性能与可靠性的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管表格基础模型(TFMs)在预测性能上已超越梯度提升决策树(GBDTs)，但其可信赖性，特别是不确定性量化方面被长期忽视。本研究旨在填补这一空白，系统比较不同模型的不确定性量化能力。

Method: 在TALENT基准的112个数据集上，对TFMs、GBDTs和经典基线方法进行广泛比较研究，使用AUC评估预测性能，使用SSCS(条件覆盖)评估保形预测下的不确定性量化能力，并通过合成数据集补充实验进一步刻画影响因素。

Result: 发现存在性能-不确定性权衡现象：TFMs虽然实现了最高的预测性能(AUC)，但在保形预测下表现出较低的条件覆盖(SSCS)，相比GBDTs其不确定性量化能力较弱。合成数据集实验进一步揭示了这种效应加剧的情境。

Conclusion: 虽然TFMs推进了预测性能的前沿，但实现良好校准的不确定性仍然是其可靠应用面临的主要开放挑战，需要在该领域进行更多研究。

Abstract: Recent Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) have demonstrated state-of-the-art predictive performance, often surpassing Gradient-Boosted Decision Trees (GBDTs). However, the trustworthiness of these models, particularly their uncertainty quantification, has been largely overlooked. We investigate this gap through an extensive study comparing TFMs, GBDTs, and classical baselines on the 112 datasets of the TALENT benchmark. Our results reveal a performance-uncertainty trade-off: although TFMs achieve the highest predictive performance, measured by AUC, they exhibit lower conditional coverage under conformal prediction, measured by SSCS, compared to GBDTs. Complementary experiments on synthetic datasets further characterize the regimes in which this effect intensifies. We conclude that while TFMs advance predictive frontiers, achieving well-calibrated uncertainty remains a major open challenge for their reliable adoption. Code is available at: https://github.com/jose-melo/high-performance-low-reliability

</details>


### [305] [A Multi-dimensional Framework for Evaluating Generalization in EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28563)
*Aditya Kommineni,Emily Zhou,Kleanthis Avramidis,Tiantian Feng,Shrikanth Narayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多维度评估框架，在低资源条件下评估EEG基础模型。发现基础模型在长上下文任务上表现优异，但在短窗口BCI任务上与监督模型相当，且对通道受限设置鲁棒性有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型评估通常采用全微调设置，未反映生物医学领域的实际约束条件，如标注数据有限、传感器覆盖减少或参数高效适应等现实低资源场景。

Method: 提出多维度评估框架，在现实低资源条件下评估EEG模型。对监督EEG模型和近期EEG基础模型（LaBraM、CSBrain、CBraMod）在6个不同数据集上进行实证分析。

Result: EEG基础模型在长上下文任务（如睡眠阶段预测、心理健康状态分类）上持续提供性能提升；在短窗口BCI任务上，监督模型以显著更少的参数达到可比性能；当前基础模型对短窗口任务和通道受限设置提供的鲁棒性有限。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明需要采用多维度评估协议来刻画模型在现实使用约束下的行为特征，以更好地理解基础模型在不同任务类型和资源条件下的适用性。

Abstract: Evaluating foundation models under appropriate adaptation settings is essential for understanding the quality and transferability of the learned representations. Recent EEG foundation models have demonstrated promising transfer capabilities across tasks and datasets, motivating their growing use in neurotechnology and clinical applications. However, these models are typically evaluated under full fine-tuning on well-curated downstream datasets, a setting that does not reflect biomedical domain constraints such as limited labeled data, reduced sensor coverage, or parameter-efficient adaptation. In this work, we propose a multi-dimensional evaluation framework for assessing EEG models under realistic low-resource conditions. Empirical analysis of both supervised EEG models and recent EEG foundation models, including LaBraM, CSBrain, and CBraMod, across 6 different datasets is performed under the proposed multi-dimensional evaluation framework. We find that EEG foundation models consistently provide performance gains on long-context tasks such as sleep stage prediction and mental health state classification. In contrast, for short-window Brain Computer Interface style tasks, supervised models achieve comparable despite having substantially fewer parameters. Additional analyses demonstrate that current foundation models provide limited robustness to short-window tasks and channel constrained settings. Together, these findings motivate the use of multi-dimensional evaluation protocols that characterize model behavior under realistic use constraints.

</details>


### [306] [Semantic Optimal Transport for Sparse Autoencoder Feature Matching and Circuit Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28567)
*Tue M. Cao,Nguyen Do,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于分布的框架，通过将SAE特征表示为激活加权分布并使用Wasserstein距离进行比较，统一解决了跨层特征匹配和电路压缩两个难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析中，跨多层匹配语义相似特征和将大型特征电路压缩为可解释的超节点这两个任务难以扩展，且被视为独立问题。本文指出这两个问题本质上都是估计位于不同激活流形上的SAE特征之间的语义距离这一根本性挑战。

Method: 提出了一个分布框架，将每个特征表示为表达该特征的隐藏状态上的激活加权分布（而非文献中的单一解码器向量）。通过将这些分布投影到一个共享参考空间并使用Wasserstein距离进行比较，为跨层特征比较提供了统一的语义度量。

Result: 理论证明表明该方法对激活重缩放具有不变性、在扰动下保持稳定，并在有限样本边际条件下能恢复真实匹配。实验上，该方法优于解码器向量和基于LLM的基线方法，能够捕捉相关特征之间的细微功能区别，并能自动将大型特征电路压缩为可解释的超节点。

Conclusion: 本文成功将跨层特征匹配和特征电路压缩统一到一个框架下，提供了一种鲁棒且有效的跨层语义距离度量方法，显著提升了SAE特征的可解释性分析能力。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a central tool for interpreting language models. However, two key SAE analyses that remain difficult to scale are (1) matching semantically similar features across multi-layers and (2) compressing large feature circuits into interpretable supernodes. Although these have been treated as separate problems, we show that both are instances of a more fundamental challenge, which we frame as the estimation of semantic distances between SAE features that lie on different activation manifolds. We introduce a distributional framework for this problem, in which each feature is represented not by a single decoder vector like in the literature, but by an activation-weighted distribution over the hidden states that express it. By projecting these distributions into a shared reference space and comparing them with Wasserstein distance, our method provides a unified semantic metric for cross-layer feature comparison. We prove that our representation is invariant to activation rescaling, stable under perturbations, and recovers true matches under finite-sample margin conditions. Empirically, our method outperforms decoder-vector and LLM-based baselines and captures subtle functional distinctions between related features. Notably, our method compresses large feature circuits into interpretable supernodes automatically.

</details>


### [307] [Efficient Pre-Training of LLMs through Truncated SVD Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28573)
*Kaivan Kamali,Kajetan Schweighofer,Hormoz Shahrzad,Olivier Francon,Babak Hodjat,Risto Miikkulainen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TSVD框架，在LLM预训练中同时保持低秩和严格正交性，实现性能匹配但计算成本显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM大规模扩展导致预训练成本高昂；现有低秩方法依赖静态秩选择且因计算成本高而无法强制权重正交性。

Method: 提出TSVD框架，利用基于谱能量的启发式方法进行自适应秩选择，并通过缓存机制维持正交性。

Result: 理论分析证明了方法在预训练动力学中的优势；跨多种模型规模的实验表明，TSVD在匹配或超越全参数基线性能的同时显著降低计算需求。

Conclusion: TSVD为高效高性能LLM预训练提供了一个理论基础扎实、实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: The massive scaling of Large Language Models (LLMs) has made pretraining increasingly cost-prohibitive. While low-rank representation and orthonormal weight matrices could in principle reduce parameter counts and computational overhead, most existing methods rely on static rank selection and do not enforce weight orthonormality due to high computational cost. This paper introduces TSVD, a framework that maintains low rank and strict orthonormality throughout the training process. It utilizes a spectral energy-based heuristic for adaptive rank selection, and a caching mechanisms to maintain orthonormality. Theoretical analysis justifies the advantage of the approach in pretraining dynamics and experiments across various model scales demonstrate that it is effective empirically. TSVD matches or exceeds the performance of full-parameter baselines while significantly reducing compute requirements. The approach thus offers a well-founded, practical, and scalable path toward efficient high-performance LLM pretraining.

</details>


### [308] [A Generalized Tikhonov Layer for Interpretable-by-design Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28578)
*Nicolas Tremblay,Benjamin Ricaud,Filippo Maria Bianchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Tikhonov层，一种可解释的图神经网络层，其学习参数直接揭示预测所依赖的节点特征和图拓扑。在保持性能的同时提供内在解释，缓解过平滑和过压缩问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络模型缺乏可解释性，其预测所依赖的节点特征和图拓扑难以直接从模型中获知，需要额外的解释方法。研究动机是设计一种内在可解释的GNN层，使模型本身就能揭示其工作机理。

Method: 提出Tikhonov层，传播矩阵为R = (p(L)+Q)^{-1}Q，其中L为归一化拉普拉斯矩阵，Q为可学习的对角正节点重要性矩阵，p(·)为可学习多项式。层输出Rx是广义图Tikhonov问题的精确最小化解，平衡节点级数据保真度与拓扑驱动的正则化惩罚。通过专门的Q网络生成重要性分数，保持表达能力的同时使Tikhonov层本身保持透明。

Result: 实验证明模型在标准图分类基准上匹配甚至优于不透明基线模型。证明了不同的节点重要性矩阵产生不同的传播算子，从结构上将解释与计算耦合。该层在单层中提供全局感受野，同时缓解过平滑和过压缩问题。

Conclusion: Tikhonov层成功实现了可解释性与性能的统一，学习的{qi, p}参数对提供内置解释：大的qi表明节点自身特征驱动预测，小的qi表示依赖局部图拓扑；p的形状揭示利用的是同质性、异质性还是带通响应。为可解释图神经网络提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: We propose the Tikhonov layer, a graph neural network layer that is interpretable by design: once trained, its learned parameters directly reveal which node features and which aspects of the graph topology were leveraged for prediction. In practice, the layer's propagation matrix takes the closed-form $R = (p(L)+Q)^{-1} Q$, where $L$ is the normalized graph Laplacian, $Q = diag(q_1,...,q_n)$ a learnable diagonal matrix of positive node-importance scores, and $p(\cdot)$ a learnable polynomial. For any input feature $x$, the layer output $Rx$ is the exact minimizer of a generalized graph Tikhonov problem that trades off node-level data fidelity against a topology-driven regularization penalty. The learned pair $\{\{q_i\},p\}$ constitutes a built-in explanation: large $q_i$ indicates that node $i$'s own features drive the prediction, while small $q_i$ signals reliance on the local graph topology; the shape of $p$ reveals whether homophily, heterophily, or a band-pass response is exploited. Expressivity is preserved by routing complexity through a dedicated, arbitrarily deep Q-network that produces the importance scores, while the Tikhonov layer itself remains transparent. We prove that distinct node-importance matrices yield distinct propagation operators, structurally coupling the explanation to the computation. Additionally, the Tikhonov layer provides, in a single layer, a global receptive field, mitigating both oversmoothing and oversquashing. Experiments on standard graph classification benchmarks confirm that the model matches (and sometimes outperforms) opaque baselines while producing interpretable and faithful explanations.

</details>


### [309] [Outer-Momentum Restarting in High-Dimensional Two-Phase Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28585)
*Kristi Topollai,Allan Ma,Tolga Dimlioglu,Sui Jiet Tay,Anna Choromanska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了在DiLoCo等通信高效的分布式优化器中，对外部动量进行周期性重启的方法。理论和实验表明，重启机制能够利用相位抵消效应，在保留内部循环进展的同时丢弃过时动量，从而扩大了外部学习率和动量值的稳定范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在DiLoCo等分布式优化器中，工作者在同步之前执行许多本地更新，外部优化器控制着本地更新的进展如何在通信轮次之间累积。近期理论表明外部优化器作用于由内部优化循环诱导的有效谱，外部动量的选择控制着这种外部记忆。作者希望找到一种简单的方法来控制这种外部记忆，以便更好地管理同步成本和优化性能。

Method: 1. 研究外部动量的周期性重启作为一种控制外部记忆的简单补充机制。2. 在线性化平方损失模型中进行分析，其中预测空间残差在经验神经正切核(NTK)下演化。3. 推导模式级重启收缩公式，表明重启通过丢弃过时动量同时保留内部循环进展来利用相位抵消。4. 通过玩具实验验证预测的收缩行为。5. 在语言模型预训练实验中测试周期性重启的效果。

Result: 1. 在线性化平方损失模型中推导出模式级重启收缩，证明重启机制能够利用相位抵消效应。2. 玩具实验验证了预测的收缩行为。3. 语言模型预训练实验表明，周期性重启扩大了外部学习率和动量值在不同通信周期中的稳定范围。重启机制能够在丢弃过时动量的同时保留内部循环的优化进展。

Conclusion: 周期性重启外部动量是一种简单而有效的机制，用于控制分布式优化中的外部记忆。该方法在理论和实践上都显示出优势：理论上揭示了重启的相位抵消机制，实践中扩大了超参数的稳定范围。这为通信高效的分布式优化提供了一个实用的改进方向。

Abstract: Communication-efficient distributed optimizers such as DiLoCo reduce synchronization costs by letting workers perform many local updates before aggregating their progress with an outer momentum optimizer. Recent theory suggests that the outer optimizer acts on an effective spectrum induced by the inner optimization loop, and that the choice of outer momentum controls how progress from local updates is accumulated across communication rounds. We study periodic restarting of the outer momentum as a simple complementary mechanism for controlling this outer memory. In a linearized squared-loss model where prediction-space residuals evolve under the empirical NTK, we derive a mode-wise restart contraction showing that resets exploit phase cancellation by discarding stale momentum while preserving inner-loop progress. Toy experiments verify the predicted contraction behavior, and language-model pretraining experiments show that periodic restarts widen the stable range of outer learning rates and momentum values across communication periods.

</details>


### [310] [Thinned Mean Field Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28589)
*Zonghao Chen,Heishiro Kanagawa,François-Xavier Briol,Chris J. Oates,Lester Mackey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KT-MFLD算法，通过核细化技术将平均场Langevin动力学的计算复杂度从O(N²)降至O(N^1.5)，同时保持相同的收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟平均场Langevin动力学的交互粒子系统具有O(N²)的计算复杂度，在大规模应用中成为瓶颈。受核细化研究启发，作者旨在降低计算复杂度。

Method: 提出KT-MFLD算法，每个粒子仅与大小为O(N^0.5)的细化粒子核心集交互，而非与所有N个粒子交互，从而将复杂度降至O(N^1.5)。

Result: 在温和正则条件下，KT-MFLD实现与原始MFLD相同的收敛保证（对数因子差异），并通过学生-教师神经网络训练、最大均值差异量化、后贝叶斯框架下的预测后验计算等任务得到实证验证。

Conclusion: KT-MFLD成功解决了熵正则化优化任务中MFLD的计算瓶颈问题，在显著降低复杂度的同时保持了理论收敛性和实证性能。

Abstract: Several important learning tasks can be formulated as minimizing an entropy-regularized objective over an appropriate space of probability distributions. Mean-field Langevin dynamics (MFLD) facilitate computation in this general context, casting the minimizer as the invariant distribution of a McKean--Vlasov process, which can be numerically discretized using $N$ particles and thus simulated. However, simulating this interacting particle system has computational complexity of order $N^2$. Motivated by recent research into \emph{kernel thinning}, we propose \texttt{KT-MFLD}, in which each particle interacts only with a thinned particle coreset of size $\mathcal{O}(N^{\frac{1}{2}})$. \texttt{KT-MFLD} thus reduces the computational complexity to order $N^{\frac{3}{2}}$ while, under mild regularity conditions, achieving the same convergence guarantees (up to logarithmic factors) as MFLD. Our theoretical analysis is empirically confirmed on tasks including the training of student-teacher neural networks, quantization with maximum mean discrepancy, and computation of predictively-oriented posteriors in a post-Bayesian framework.

</details>


### [311] [PLS in the Mirror of Self-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28592)
*Jiangsheng,You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出偏最小二乘法（PLS）可以被重新表述为线性化的自注意力机制，从而将PLS纳入神经网络框架进行研究，同时揭示了自注意力可能隐含降维和特征选择的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立偏最小二乘法（PLS）与神经网络自注意力机制之间的理论联系，使PLS能够在神经网络范式下进行研究，并从PLS的特性反向理解自注意力机制的工作原理。

Method: 通过数学推导将偏最小二乘法（PLS）重新表述为线性化的自注意力机制形式，建立两者之间的对应关系。

Result: 成功将PLS表示为线性化自注意力的形式，证明了两者在数学结构上的等价性；发现自注意力机制中包含了类似PLS的降维和预测变量选择功能。

Conclusion: PLS可以作为线性化自注意力在神经网络框架下被研究和理解；自注意力机制天然的降维特性有助于改善学习效果，这一发现为理解自注意力的工作机制提供了新的视角。

Abstract: This note provides an interesting observation on casting partial least square (PLS) as a linearized self-attention so that PLS may be studied within the neural network paradigm. On the other hand, the dimensionality reduction and selection of predictors in PLS may indicate that self-attention includes certain degree of dimensionality normalization toward improved learning.

</details>


### [312] [Transformers Provably Learn to Internalize Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28600)
*Yixiao Huang,Hanlin Zhu,Zixuan Wang,Jiantao Jiao,Stuart Russell,Somayeh Sojoudi,Song Mei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Log-ICoT方法，通过几何级数方式移除思考token，将训练阶段数从线性降至对数级，在保持显式CoT样本效率的同时消除了推理开销，首次为隐式思维链提供了理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式Chain-of-Thought (CoT)虽然显著提升了transformer的样本效率，但在推理时生成显式推理步骤计算代价高昂；而隐式CoT (ICoT)虽是有效的经验性解决方案，但其理论基础尚不清晰，需要理论分析来理解其工作机制和优化方法。

Method: 提出Log-ICoT课程学习方法：1) 训练L层transformer，采用log₂k个训练阶段学习k-奇偶性任务；2) 与标准ICoT逐个移除思考token不同，Log-ICoT以几何级数方式分块移除思考token；3) 通过多阶段训练逐步将推理能力内化到隐藏状态中。

Result: 证明了L层transformer在Log-ICoT课程下仅需poly(n)样本即可学习k-奇偶性，且训练阶段数L=log₂k；该结果匹配显式CoT的样本效率同时消除了推理开销；实验证实理论预测并可视化展示推理过程如何逐步被吸收到更深层中。

Conclusion: 本文首次为隐式思维链提供理论分析，证明Log-ICoT能够实现与显式CoT相当的样本效率，同时将训练阶段数从线性降至对数级，为理解和发展隐式推理方法提供了理论基础和实证支持。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting substantially improves the sample efficiency of transformers, reducing the complexity of tasks like parity learning from exponential to polynomial in the input length. However, generating explicit reasoning steps at inference is computationally expensive. Implicit Chain-of-Thought (ICoT) has emerged as a promising empirical remedy that trains models to internalize intermediate steps within their hidden states, but its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. We give the first theoretical analysis of ICoT, proving that an $L$-layer transformer trained under our proposed Log-ICoT curriculum learns $k$-parity with $\mathsf{poly}(n)$ samples and $L = \log_2 k$ training stages. This matches the sample efficiency of explicit CoT while eliminating its inference overhead, and extends prior one-layer parity guarantees to multi-layer architectures. Compared to standard ICoT, which removes thinking tokens one at a time, Log-ICoT removes them in geometric chunks, reducing the number of stages from linear in $k$ to logarithmic. Experiments on multi-layer transformers confirm the theory and visualize how reasoning is progressively absorbed into deeper layers.

</details>


### [313] [Online Irregular Multivariate Time Series Forecasting via Uncertainty-Driven Dual-Expert Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28603)
*Haonan Wen,Hanyang Chen,Songhe Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Under-Cali框架，通过不确定性驱动的双专家校准机制解决在线不规则多变量时间序列预测问题，实现高效稳定的在线自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线方法在在线部署时因数据分布动态变化导致性能显著下降。不规则采样破坏了时间连续性和周期性，无法利用常规多变量时间序列的在线学习特性，因此需要专门针对在线IMTS预测问题设计自适应方法。

Method: 提出不确定性驱动的双专家校准框架(Under-Cali)，包含三个核心组件：(1)不确定性估计器作为核心控制信号；(2)双专家校准模块，包含可靠专家和不可靠专家；(3)自适应路由模块。不确定性估计器评估每个批次的不确定性，路由模块将高不确定性样本导向不可靠专家校准，低不确定性样本保留在可靠专家。系统保持源预测模型冻结，仅通过轻量级模型无关校准模块进行自适应。

Result: 在IMTS基准测试上进行广泛实验，证明该方法能够以较低的计算成本实现一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: Under-Cali框架通过不确定性引导的双专家校准机制，实现了在线不规则多变量时间序列预测的稳定高效自适应，为动态场景下的时序预测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Irregular multivariate time series forecasting is critical in many real-world applications, where time series are irregularly sampled and exhibit dynamically evolving missingness patterns. Although existing methods perform well in offline settings, they often suffer from significant performance degradation when deployed online due to dynamic shifts in data distribution. Maintaining forecasting capability in such dynamic scenarios typically necessitates online adaptation techniques. Since irregular sampling fundamentally undermines temporal continuity and periodicity, we cannot leverage these widely studied characteristics from regular MTS for online learning. To this end, we study the problem of online IMTS forecasting and propose Under-Cali, an uncertainty-driven dual-expert calibration framework consisting of three core components: an uncertainty estimator, a dual-expert calibration module, and an adaptive routing module. We design an uncertainty estimator that serves as the core control signal to jointly manage inference and adaptation processes. In our framework, the uncertainty estimator first assesses uncertainty for each incoming batch. The adaptive routing module then directs samples with high uncertainty to the unreliable expert for calibration, while low uncertainty samples remain with the reliable expert. Subsequently, the system updates the reliable expert and the uncertainty estimator using well-calibrated reliable samples, and updates the unreliable expert with challenging samples, enabling stable and efficient online learning. Under-Cali keeps the source forecasting model frozen and performs adaptation only through a lightweight, model-agnostic calibration module, enabling efficient adaptation. Extensive experiments on IMTS benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements with low computational cost. Our code is available at https://github.com/HaonanWen/Under-Cali.

</details>


### [314] [Learning High-Dimensional Parity Functions with Product Networks using Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28612)
*Guillaume Larue,Louis-Adrien Dufrène,Quentin Lampin,Hadi Ghauch,Ghaya Rekaya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了通过紧凑的乘积型神经网络架构结合随机数据稀疏性（伯努利输入），可以高效学习高维奇偶函数，理论保证收敛并在高达10万维的实验中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇偶函数是机器学习、密码学和纠错等领域的关键布尔运算，但在高维情况下，标准神经网络需要指数级样本复杂度，导致基于梯度的优化难以处理，亟需解决方案。

Method: 提出紧凑的乘积型神经网络架构，结合随机数据稀疏性（满足$p_e \leq 1/N$的伯努利输入），并选择合适的超参数（$p_e$和学习率$α$）来实现高效奇偶学习。

Result: 理论证明了方法的收敛性保证；实验验证了高达$N = 100,000$维度的有效性；发现了超参数$p_e$和$α$的最优选择，并展示了多项式复杂度的扩展规律。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了架构归纳偏置与数据稀疏性之间的基本联系，为神经算术、结构化推理、二值神经网络以及应用于自动化协议发现的机器学习开辟了新可能。

Abstract: Parity functions are fundamental Boolean operations with critical applications across machine learning, cryptography, and error correction. Yet, learning high-dimensional parity functions poses significant challenges: in a general setting, standard neural network architectures typically require exponential sample complexity, making gradient-based optimization intractable for large number of inputs $N$. We demonstrate that compact product-based neural architectures combined with stochastic data sparsity (Bernoulli inputs with $p_e \leq 1/N$) and appropriate hyperparameter choice enable efficient parity learning, with theoretical guarantees of convergence. Experiments validate our theory across dimensions up to $N = 100{,}000$, with empirical evidence showing optimal hyperparameter choices for $p_e$ and learning rate $α$, as well as polynomial complexity scaling laws. This work establishes fundamental connections between architectural inductive bias and data sparsity, opening new possibilities for neural arithmetic, structured reasoning, binary neural networks, and machine learning applied to automated protocol discovery.

</details>


### [315] [Random Process Flow Matching: Generative Implicit Representations of Multivariate Random Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28625)
*Julien Lalanne,David Picard,Lionel Boillot,Lina-María Guayacán-Carrillo,Leon Barens,Jean-Michel Pereira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RP Flow框架，基于Flow Matching和神经隐函数，仅从稀疏观测中学习单个场的分布，实现了高质量样本生成和校准的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成模型依赖大量训练数据来学习复杂分布，但在许多实际重建任务中，仅有单个观测场且测量数据稀疏。如何在这种数据稀缺场景下学习分布并提供可追溯的不确定性估计是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出Random Process Flow框架：1) 使用随机傅里叶特征学习隐式信号表示，可从有限观测中查询任意位置；2) 采用流匹配方法，将向量场表示为神经隐函数；3) 在源空间通过高斯过程回归构建贝叶斯后验生成样本；4) 通过集成采样编码不确定性。

Result: 实验表明RP Flow能够生成高质量样本并提供校准的不确定性估计，在高频率、高稀疏性或高维度等挑战性条件下均表现良好。

Conclusion: RP Flow为数据稀缺且需要保持不确定性可追溯的重建任务提供了一种有效的生成建模方案，是该领域的重要进展。

Abstract: Generative modeling provides a powerful framework for learning data distributions. These models initially relied on probabilistic methods such as Gaussian Processes (GP) for uncertainty-aware predictions and shifted towards larger trainable models to learn more complex distributions. In this work, we introduce Random Process (RP) Flow, a Flow Matching-based framework that represents the vector field as a neural implicit function. Unlike modern generative methods, our setting involves a single observed field, from which only sparse measurements are available. RP Flow uses Random Fourier Features to learn an implicit signal representation that can be queried at any arbitrary location from a limited set of observations, while encoding uncertainty through ensemble sampling. We propose constructing a Bayesian posterior by GP regression in the source space to generate high-quality samples. Our empirical results demonstrate that this framework generates realistic samples along with calibrated uncertainty estimates, even under challenging conditions such as high frequency, high sparsity, or high dimensionality. These findings position RP Flow as a milestone towards generative models for reconstruction tasks where data is scarce and uncertainty must remain traceable.

</details>


### [316] [When Interpretability Is Unequally Distributed: Fairness in Hybrid Interpretable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28626)
*Ziba Jabbar Zare,Ulrich Aïvodji,Julien Ferry,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了可解释性覆盖差异(ICD)的概念，用于衡量混合可解释模型在路由决策中存在的程序公平性问题——即不同人群群体被系统性分配到可解释组件或黑盒组件的差异程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合可解释模型通过将部分样本分配给透明组件、其余样本交给黑盒模型来平衡准确性与可解释性。然而这种设计引发了程序公平性担忧：某些群体可能系统性地获得可解释决策，而其他群体则不成比例地被路由到黑盒模型。

Method: 将ICD形式化为一种应用于混合可解释模型路由决策的人口均等式度量指标。利用预测多重性工具，在四种混合可解释学习方法、三个标准公平性基准数据集及多个敏感属性上研究ICD现象。同时探索通过简单的覆盖差异约束来缓解ICD问题。

Result: 实验发现在中等透明度条件下存在显著的ICD，此时可解释组件和黑盒组件都被活跃使用。简单的覆盖差异约束能够显著降低精确混合学习方法中的ICD，且对准确性和稀疏性影响甚微。在多个设置中，ICD缓解措施还改善了标准算法公平性指标。

Conclusion: 混合可解释模型不仅需要审查预测公平性，还需要审查其在个体和群体间如何分配可解释性。这为混合模型的公平性审计提供了新的视角和实用方法。

Abstract: Hybrid interpretable models combine a transparent component with a black-box model by assigning some examples to the former and deferring the rest to the latter. While this design enables flexible tradeoffs between accuracy and interpretability, it also raises a distinct procedural fairness concern: some demographic groups may systematically receive interpretable decisions, while others are disproportionately routed to a black box.
  We formalize this issue as Interpretability Coverage Disparity (ICD), a demographic-parity-style measure applied to the routing decision of hybrid interpretable models. Using tools from predictive multiplicity, we study ICD across four hybrid interpretable learning methods, three standard fairness benchmark datasets, and multiple sensitive attributes. Our experiments reveal substantial ICD in intermediate transparency regimes, where both the interpretable and black-box components are actively used. We further show that simple coverage-disparity constraints can significantly reduce ICD in exact hybrid learning methods, with marginal impact on accuracy and sparsity. In several settings, ICD mitigation also improves standard algorithmic fairness metrics. These results show that hybrid interpretable models should be audited not only for predictive fairness, but also for how they allocate interpretability across individuals and groups.

</details>


### [317] [Single-Rollout Hidden-State Dynamics for Training-Free RLVR Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28631)
*Jianghao Wu,Jianfei Cai,Weiqiang Wang,Jin Ye,Daniel F. Schmidt,Yasmeen George*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SHIFT，一种无需训练的强化学习数据选择方法，通过推理过程中的隐藏状态动态变化来高效筛选训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中的数据选择对模型性能影响显著，但现有方法依赖训练过程或需要大量标注数据，在专业领域成本高昂且往往不可行。

Method: 提出SHIFT方法，通过单次确定性推理rollout计算推理诱导的表示偏移（RIRS），以其幅度作为实例效用代理，并通过质量加权的最远优先核心集程序实现覆盖性。

Result: 在数学推理和医疗问答基准测试中，SHIFT在超低预算下持续优于无训练基线方法，提升了领域内准确性和向更难评估设置的迁移能力。

Conclusion: SHIFT为强化学习数据选择提供了一种高效、无需训练的解决方案，RIRS驱动的覆盖和质量加权共同贡献了性能提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) can yield large reasoning gains from very few training instances, yet its strong sensitivity to which instances are used makes data selection a central bottleneck. Most existing selection pipelines rely on training-time optimization signals and/or require access to verifiable rewards or ground-truth answers over large candidate pools, which is costly and often infeasible in specialized domains. We study RLVR data selection in a setting where selection must be performed before any RL training and without labels or reward evaluation on the full pool. We propose SHIFT, a one-shot, training-free selector based solely on inference-time hidden-state dynamics. For each candidate instance, SHIFT runs a single deterministic reasoning rollout and computes a reasoning-induced representation shift (RIRS) as the start-to-end hidden-state delta. SHIFT uses the RIRS magnitude as a lightweight proxy for instance utility and enforces coverage via a quality-weighted farthest-first CoreSet procedure in an RIRS-augmented feature space, producing compact subsets that scale to large unlabeled pools. Across mathematical reasoning and medical QA benchmarks under ultra-low budgets, SHIFT consistently outperforms training-free diversity and difficulty/uncertainty baselines, improving both in-domain accuracy and transfer to harder evaluation settings. Ablations show that RIRS-based coverage and quality-weighting contribute complementary gains, and analyses indicate that RIRS is not explained by simple input/output length statistics. Code is available at github.com/JianghaoWu/SHIFT.

</details>


### [318] [Augmenting Attention with Exponentially Decaying Memory Improves Query-Aware KV Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28640)
*Xiuying Wei,Caglar Gulcehre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现RAT+的记忆模块能持续提升查询感知稀疏推理方法（Quest、MoBA、SnapKV）在海捞针任务上的准确性，并在OLMo2-7B上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究RAT+引入的指数衰减记忆模块是否能改进现有的查询感知稀疏推理方法，以提升长上下文语言模型的推理效率和准确性。

Method: 将RAT+的记忆模块与代表性查询感知稀疏推理方法（Quest、MoBA、SnapKV）结合，在八个海捞针任务上进行系统测试，并对OLMo2-7B模型进行100亿token的持续预训练以验证效果。

Result: 实验表明，在不同稀疏预算下，RAT+在所有测试的稀疏推理方法上均比标准注意力机制获得更高的准确性，且在原始RAT+检查点和经持续预训练的OLMo2-7B模型上均验证了这一优势。

Conclusion: RAT+的记忆模块能够有效增强查询感知稀疏推理方法的性能，作者提出了两个假设来解释其作用机制，并通过针对性实验提供了支持。

Abstract: Efficient inference is critical for long-context language models, where attention computation and KV-cache access dominate the cost. Recent work RAT+, introduces a recurrence-augmented attention backbone that enables flexible dilated attention at inference time. In this paper, we investigate whether this exponentially decaying memory can also improve existing query-aware sparse inference methods. Using representative methods including Quest, MoBA, and SnapKV, we show that RAT+ consistently improves accuracy over standard attention across sparse budgets on eight needle-in-a-haystack tasks. We validate these gains both on the released checkpoints from the RAT+ paper and on OLMo2-7B, which we continue pretraining with the added memory module for 10B tokens. Finally, we propose two hypotheses explaining why this memory module benefits query-aware sparse inference and design targeted experiments to support them.

</details>


### [319] [Interpretability-Guided Layer Selection over Subspace Projection: SAEs as Stethoscopes, Not Scalpels, for Raw Task Vector Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28649)
*Li Lei,Madalina Ciobanu,Qingqing Mao,Ritankar Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现将任务向量投影到稀疏自编码器(SAE)特征子空间会因几何错位丢失97%的修改能量导致无效；转而提出将SAE作为"听诊器"用于层诊断而非"手术刀"进行干预，在数学推理任务上显著提升性能（如数论准确率从29.6%升至39.4%），无需额外推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要外科手术式的模型编辑以增强特定领域能力，同时避免全量微调带来的计算成本和灾难性遗忘问题。稀疏自编码器(SAE)被认为是实现特征级干预的有前景工具，但其实际效果缺乏严格评估。

Method: 严格评估SAE引导的编辑流程，发现投影方法存在根本失效模式；提出范式转变——将SAE作为"听诊器"而非"手术刀"，使用SAE衍生的特异性分数识别关键层，仅在这些层注入未过滤的原始任务向量，而非在特征层面进行过滤干预。

Result: 在Gemma-3-4B-IT的Minerva Math基准测试中，数论准确率从29.6%提升至39.4%(z=+3.41, p=0.0007)；7个数学主题中5个显著改善，无显著下降；方法完全确定性且无额外推理成本。

Conclusion: SAE更适合作为层级诊断工具而非干预级过滤器；通过特异性分数引导的层选择性编辑提供了一个有原则的可解释性引导模型编辑框架，避免了因激活空间SAE方向与权重空间任务向量几何错位导致的信息瓶颈问题。

Abstract: LLMs increasingly require surgical model editing to enhance domain-specific capabilities without incurring the computational cost or catastrophic forgetting associated with full fine-tuning. Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a promising tool in this setting, in principle allowing for feature-level identification of where to intervene. In this work, we rigorously evaluate an SAE-guided editing pipeline for mathematical reasoning on Gemma-3-4B-IT and uncover a fundamental failure mode: the intuitively appealing approach of projecting task vectors onto SAE feature subspaces acts as an information bottleneck that discards approximately 97% of the modification energy, yielding no statistically significant improvements across seven math subjects. We show that this failure stems from a geometric misalignment between activation-space SAE directions and weight-space task vectors. We then propose a shift in perspective: SAE as a Stethoscope, Not a Scalpel, where SAEs are used for layer-level diagnosis rather than intervention-level filtering. By injecting unfiltered raw task vectors only into layers identified by an SAE-derived specificity score, we improve Number Theory accuracy from 29.6% to 39.4% (z=+3.41, p=0.0007) on the Minerva Math benchmark; 5 of 7 math subjects significantly improved and none significantly degraded. Our method is fully deterministic, requires no additional inference cost, and provides a principled framework for interpretability-guided model editing.

</details>


### [320] [Applications of temporal graph learning for predicting the dynamics of biological systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28659)
*Manuel Dileo,Andrea Sottoriva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时序图的方法来建模细胞发育动态过程，通过伪时间解析的基因调控网络和时序图神经网络来预测生物状态，在多个任务上超越了现有的静态基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生物基础模型主要基于静态设置，直接将Transformer架构应用于基因表达矩阵，但没有显式建模细胞发育程序的时序演化过程。理解细胞状态在发育或疾病进程中如何逐步涌现、分化和重组需要建模这种动态特性。

Method: 提出时序图视角：从单细胞转录组数据推断伪时间轨迹，将细胞离散化为发育快照，为每个快照重构一个基因调控网络，应用时序图神经网络预测生物状态。该方法将细胞状态表示为持久基因标识上的演化图结构。

Result: 在两个公开的小鼠发育数据集（红细胞原肠胚形成和胰腺内分泌发生）上评估，考虑基因表达预测、链路预测和出度中心性预测三个任务。图模型在所有任务上均优于scGPT和scFoundation等知名基础模型。时序图学习能够捕获非平凡的调控动态并识别时序重要的基因枢纽。

Conclusion: 显式建模演化的调控结构提供了静态预训练表示之外的有用信息，时序图学习是建模动态生物系统的有前景方向，可作为单细胞生物学中当前基础模型方法的补充范式。

Abstract: Biological foundation models have shown strong performance in single-cell representation learning by applying transformer architectures directly to gene-expression matrices. However, these approaches predominantly operate in static settings and do not explicitly model the temporal evolution of developmental programs in the cell. Modeling such dynamics is important for understanding how cellular states progressively emerge, differentiate, and reorganize during development or disease progression. In this work-in-progress paper, we investigate an alternative temporal graph-based perspective in which cellular states are represented through pseudotime-resolved gene regulatory networks and modeled as evolving graph structures over persistent gene identities. Starting from single-cell transcriptomic data, we infer pseudotime trajectories, discretize cells into developmental snapshots, reconstruct one gene regulatory network per snapshot, and apply temporal graph neural networks to forecast biological states. We evaluate this framework on two publicly available mouse developmental datasets, erythroid gastrulation and pancreatic endocrinogenesis, considering three complementary tasks: gene-expression forecasting, link prediction, and out-degree centrality prediction. Our results show that graph-based models outperform well-known foundation-model such as scGPT and scFoundation, suggesting that explicitly modeling evolving regulatory structure provides useful information beyond static pretrained representations. For link prediction and centrality forecasting, temporal graph learning captures non-trivial regulatory dynamics and enables the identification of temporally important gene hubs. Overall, our findings support temporal graph learning as a promising direction for modeling dynamic biological systems and as a complementary paradigm to current foundation model approaches in single-cell biology.

</details>


### [321] [Activation Steering for Synthetic Data Generation: The Role of Diversity in Downstream Safety Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28664)
*Vijeta Deshpande,Tootiya Giyahchi,Veena Padmanabhan,Leman Akoglu,Anna Rumshisky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究激活引导(AS)生成安全检测训练数据的效果，发现增加引导强度会降低多样性，且仅30%的AS配置优于提示方法；提出的成功率-连贯性-多样性调和均值可作为下游任务性能的有效预测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全检测模型需要HHH违规样本进行训练，但此类数据稀缺。激活引导(AS)作为数据高效方法可生成目标概念对齐的响应，但其能否生成高质量训练数据集用于下游分类器尚待验证。

Method: 进行双重评估研究：内在评估引入样本级和集合级多样性这一新质量维度，外在评估用AS生成数据替换训练数据中的HHH违规样本并微调检测分类器。实验覆盖4个概念×2个模型×4种引导方法。

Result: AS生成数据在4个概念中的3个上优于提示生成数据，但136个AS配置中仅41个超越提示方法。成功率、连贯性和多样性的调和均值比仅用成功率和连贯性更一致地与下游AUROC相关。

Conclusion: AS在合成数据生成改进安全检测方面具有潜力，多样性是调优AS的关键但此前被忽视的维度。下游效用存在于同时满足成功性、连贯性和多样性的狭窄区间。

Abstract: Safety detection models require examples of HHH (Helpful, Harmless, Honest)-violating outputs for robust generalization, however such examples are scarce. Activation Steering (AS) has emerged as a data-efficient method for generating target-concept-aligned responses. We investigate whether AS can generate high-quality training datasets for downstream classifiers, a question that remains untested. We present a two-fold study with intrinsic and extrinsic evaluation across $4$ concepts $\times\,2$ models $\times\,4$ steering methods. Intrinsically, beyond the field-standard rubric of steering success (concept alignment) and coherence, we introduce sample- and set-level diversity as a quality axis previously absent from the literature, and find that increasing steering strength reduces response diversity. Extrinsically, we replace HHH-violating examples in the available training data with steered generations and fine-tune detection classifiers. AS-generated data results in a better classifier than the prompting-generated data on $3$ of $4$ concepts. However, only $41$ of $136$ AS configurations outperform prompting, indicating that downstream utility lies in a narrow regime that jointly satisfies success, coherence, and diversity. The harmonic mean of these three axes correlates with downstream AUROC more consistently across concepts than success and coherence alone, providing a practical heuristic target for practitioners tuning AS hyperparameters. Together, our results highlight the potential of AS in synthetic data generation for improving safety detection and identify diversity as a critical, previously overlooked axis for tuning AS.

</details>


### [322] [Optimal Data Acquisition for Reinforcement Learning: A Large Deviations Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28675)
*Mingjie Hu,Jian-Qiang Hu,Enlu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对无限视界强化学习中的数据获取效率问题，提出了一种基于大偏差理论的统一框架，通过指数衰减率度量效率并设计了近鲁棒最优的自适应数据获取策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在商业和医疗运营中部署强化学习时，数据获取效率是一个核心挑战，因为交互成本高、速度慢，且通常涉及人在环路的场景。传统方法在这些受限环境中难以有效平衡探索与利用，因此需要一种新的理论框架来优化数据获取过程。

Method: 作者引入策略选择误差概率的指数衰减率作为效率度量指标，利用马尔可夫链的大偏差理论推导出该速率的变分表征，形成一个嵌套优化问题。基于此，形式化了两种互补的最优性概念。由于原问题隐式且难处理，提出了一个带显式约束的可处理凸松弛。随后开发了一种惰性一步投影次梯度方法求解松弛问题，并利用其迭代构造自适应数据获取策略。该框架还扩展到线性函数近似以提升可扩展性。

Result: 证明了所提出的强化学习算法在作者定义的最优性准则下是近鲁棒最优的（常数因子范围内）。数值实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一个统一的大偏差理论框架用于强化学习中的数据获取问题，为无限视界场景提供了理论基础，并通过凸松弛和自适应策略设计实现了实际可用的算法，为成本敏感的强化学习应用提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Data acquisition efficiency is a central challenge in deploying reinforcement learning in business and healthcare operations, where interactions are costly, slow, and often involve humans in the loop. This paper develops a unified large deviations framework for data acquisition in infinite-horizon reinforcement learning. We introduce the exponential decay rate of the policy-selection error probability as a principled efficiency metric and derive a variational characterization of this rate via large deviations theory for Markov chains, yielding a nested optimization problem. Based on this characterization, we formalize two complementary notions of optimality in terms of the optimal solution of the nested problem. Because the resulting program is implicit and generally intractable, we propose a tractable convex relaxation with explicit constraints. We then develop a lazy one-step projected subgradient method to solve the relaxed problem and use its iterates to construct an adaptive data acquisition policy. We prove that the resulting reinforcement learning algorithm is near-robustly optimal under our optimality criterion, up to a constant factor. Finally, we extend the framework to linear function approximation to improve scalability, and numerical experiments support the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [323] [Optimal ridge regularization revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28679)
*Jack Timmermans,Sergio A. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG
