TL;DR: 本文提出了一种迭代程序来数值计算岭回归中的最优正则化强度，在固定-X设置下证明了其在有限噪声水平下的收敛性，实验表明该方法在合成数据上能够在各种样本大小、宽高比和噪声水平下实现接近最优的随机-X泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限数据样本上进行的L^2正则化线性（岭）回归中，如何从生成参数数值计算出最优正则化强度是一个关键问题，特别是在固定-X设置下需要考虑有界协方差、线性预测目标以及具有有限方差的各向同性加性噪声的情况。

Method: 提出了一种迭代程序来从固定-X设置中的生成参数数值计算最优正则化强度，并在有限噪声水平下证明了该程序的收敛性。该方法结合基于样本的参数估计，在参数不足情况下需要相当于一次初步岭回归的额外计算成本，在过参数化情况下需要两次。

Result: 在合成数据上的实验评估表明，所提出的程序结合基于样本的参数估计，在广泛的样本大小、宽高比和噪声水平范围内实现了接近最优的随机-X泛化性能。额外的计算成本在参数不足情况下相当于一次初步岭回归，在过参数化情况下相当于两次。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了一种计算岭回归最优正则化强度的有效迭代方法，该方法具有理论收敛保证，在实际应用中能够在保持较低计算成本的同时实现接近最优的泛化性能，为岭回归正则化参数选择提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We consider $L^2$-regularized linear (ridge) regression over a finite data sample $X$ with bounded covariance and linear prediction targets $y$ with additive isotropic noise of finite variance. We present an iterative procedure to compute the optimal regularization strength numerically from the generative parameters in the fixed-$X$ setting and prove its convergence at limited noise levels. Our experimental evaluation over synthetic data shows that the proposed procedure combined with sample-based parameter estimates attains near-optimal random-$X$ generalization across a wide range of sample sizes, aspect ratios, and noise levels, at an added computational cost equivalent to one preliminary ridge regression in the underparameterized regime and two in the overparameterized case.

</details>


### [324] [History-aware adaptive reduced-order models via incremental singular value decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28684)
*Amirpasha Hedayat,Ali Mohaghegh,Laura Balzano,Cheng Huang,Karthik Duraisamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于增量奇异值分解（iSVD）的自适应降阶模型框架，通过在线更新基向量有效解决了在线动力学偏离训练数据范围时的精度下降问题，并在多个非线性问题中验证了其优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降阶模型（ROMs）可以加速高维动力学模拟，但当在线动力学偏离离线训练数据所代表的区域时，其精度往往会下降。为了解决这一局限性，需要开发能够在线自适应更新的ROM框架。

Method: 开发了一种基于投影的自适应ROM框架，核心是增量奇异值分解（iSVD）。该方法通过偶尔的全阶算子评估提供校正快照用于在线基向量更新。由于所考虑的侵入式ROM完全由基向量参数化，每次更新自然传播到降阶算子和超降阶机制中。iSVD通过其演化的奇异结构保留了所观察到的动力学的编码历史，具有历史感知特性。

Result: 在三个复杂度递增的非线性问题（一维粘性Burgers方程、Sod激波管和刚性一维十物种旋转爆震发动机）上进行了研究。Burgers问题表明历史感知更新优于瞬时更新，且iSVD表现最佳。Sod和RDE案例证明了这些优势在更具挑战性的可压缩流环境中持续存在。对于RDE问题，iSVD自适应ROM在预测精度和计算效率方面均优于当前最先进的Direct adaptive ROM基线。成本分析显示，主要在线成本来自与全阶模型交互获取校正快照，而iSVD更新本身的成本可忽略不计。

Conclusion: iSVD被证实为在线学习降阶子空间的有效机制，为开发能够在初始训练窗口数个数量级更长时间范围内保持预测能力的ROMs指明了方向。

Abstract: Reduced-order models (ROMs) can accelerate high-dimensional dynamical simulations, but their accuracy often deteriorates when online dynamics leave the regime represented by offline training data. We develop a projection-based adaptive ROM framework based on incremental singular value decomposition (iSVD), in which occasional full-order operator evaluations provide correction snapshots for online basis updates. The intrusive ROMs considered here are fully parameterized by the basis, so each update naturally propagates to reduced operators and hyper-reduction machinery. Through its evolving singular structure, iSVD retains an encoded history of the observed dynamics and is history-aware in this sense. We study the method on three nonlinear problems of increasing complexity: the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation, the Sod shock tube, and a stiff one-dimensional ten-species rotating detonation engine (RDE). The Burgers problem is used to analyze the method and compare iSVD with alternative basis adaptation rules, showing that history-aware updates outperform instantaneous updates and that iSVD gives the strongest overall performance. The Sod and RDE cases demonstrate that these advantages persist in more challenging compressible-flow settings. For the RDE problem, the iSVD adaptive ROM improves upon the current state-of-the-art Direct adaptive ROM baseline in both predictive accuracy and computational efficiency. A cost analysis shows that the dominant online cost comes from interacting with the full-order model to obtain correction snapshots, while the iSVD update itself is negligible. These results identify iSVD as an effective mechanism for online learning of reduced subspaces and suggest a path toward ROMs that remain predictive over horizons several orders of magnitude longer than their initial training window.

</details>


### [325] [Expressive Power of Floating-Point Neural Networks with Arbitrary Reduction Orders and Inexact Activation Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28704)
*Yeachan Park,Geonho Hwang,Wonyeol Lee,Sejun Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种区分性框架，用于刻画浮点数神经网络的表达能力，证明了在更现实的浮点执行模型下，包括Sigmoid、ReLU等在内的多种实用激活函数都能实现通用表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经网络表达能力理论大多基于精确实数算术，但实际神经网络是在有限精度浮点算术下执行的，且具有依赖于实现的执行语义。近期工作虽开始研究浮点神经网络的表达能力，但现有结果局限于高度受限的激活函数和理想化假设（如固定的归约顺序和正确舍入的激活实现）。

Method: 引入了一个通用的区分性框架，研究在广义浮点执行语义（包括任意归约顺序和具有有界ULP误差的不精确激活实现）下浮点神经网络的表达能力。通过分析第一层区分每对不同输入的能力，建立了通用可表示性的充要条件。

Result: 证明了区分每对不同输入的能力是实现通用可表示性的必要条件，这揭示了广泛类别的激活实现不是通用表示器；同时证明了在激活实现的温和条件下，适当的区分性形式也是通用可表示性的充分条件。基于此框架，为包括Sigmoid、tanh、ReLU、ELU、SeLU、GeLU、Swish、Mish和sin在内的广泛实用激活函数建立了通用可表示性结果。

Conclusion: 该研究显著扩展了浮点神经网络表达能力理论，在比先前已知的更现实的浮点执行模型下，建立了广泛的实用激活函数的通用可表示性结果，为理解实际神经网络的计算能力提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Most existing expressivity theories for neural networks assume exact real arithmetic, whereas practical neural networks are executed under finite-precision floating-point arithmetic with implementation-dependent execution semantics. Recent works have begun studying the expressive power of floating-point neural networks, but existing results are limited to highly restricted activation functions and idealized assumptions such as fixed left-to-right reduction orders and correctly rounded activation implementations. In this work, we study the expressive power of floating-point neural networks under generalized floating-point execution semantics, including arbitrary reduction orders and inexact activation implementations with bounded ulp errors. We investigate when floating-point neural networks can represent arbitrary functions between floating-point domains exactly. To this end, we introduce a general distinguishability framework and show that the ability to distinguish every pair of distinct inputs in the first layer is necessary for universal representability. This characterization yields broad classes of activation implementations that are not universal representators, extending previous isolated counterexamples such as the correctly rounded cosine activation. We further prove that a suitable form of distinguishability is also sufficient for universal representability under mild conditions on the activation implementation. Using this framework, we establish universal representability results for a broad class of practical activation functions, including implementations of $\mathrm{Sigmoid}$, $\tanh$, $\mathrm{ReLU}$, $\mathrm{ELU}$, $\mathrm{SeLU}$, $\mathrm{GeLU}$, $\mathrm{Swish}$, $\mathrm{Mish}$, and $\sin$, under significantly more realistic floating-point execution models than previously known.

</details>


### [326] [Understanding Generalization and Forgetting in In-Context Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28705)
*Guangyu Li,Meng Ding,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个上下文持续学习理论框架，揭示了标准注意力机制在处理多任务序列时会产生不可避免的跨任务干扰，导致系统性偏差和性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论主要研究单任务设置下的上下文学习(ICL)，但实际应用中提示词往往包含异构任务序列，缺乏对大语言模型是否在推理过程中隐式执行持续学习的理论理解。

Method: 提出了一个上下文持续学习理论框架，通过共享注意力机制建模预训练Transformer如何在单个提示词中处理多个顺序任务。重点研究线性和掩码线性自注意力，推导了顺序任务提示下的模型预测误差表达式。

Result: 发现标准注意力机制通过均匀或因果聚合历史上下文，不可避免地引起跨任务干扰，导致系统性偏差。提供了预测误差的偏差-方差-干扰分解，刻画了历史上下文信息何时产生正迁移或可证明的负迁移。

Conclusion: 揭示了基于注意力的持续推理的根本限制，为长提示词中的顺序敏感性和性能退化提供了理论解释，指出标准注意力机制在多任务序列处理中存在固有缺陷。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) derives its power from enabling Large Language Models to adapt to new tasks via prompt-based reasoning alone, entirely bypassing the need for parameter updates. Existing theories primarily study ICL in single-task settings, while real-world prompts often contain sequences of heterogeneous tasks, leaving a gap in understanding whether Large Language Models implicitly perform continual learning during inference. To bridge this gap, we propose the first theoretical framework for in-context continual learning, modeling how a pretrained Transformer processes multiple sequential tasks within a single prompt through shared attention mechanisms. Focusing on linear and masked linear self-attention, we derive error expressions for model predictions under sequential task prompts and analyze their generalization and forgetting behavior. Our results reveal that standard attention mechanisms inevitably induce intertask interference by uniformly or causally aggregating historical contexts, leading to systematic bias. We further provide a bias-variance-interference decomposition of prediction error, characterizing when historical in-context information yields positive transfer or provable negative transfer. This analysis exposes fundamental limits of attention-based continual inference and offers theoretical explanations for order sensitivity and performance degradation in long prompts.

</details>


### [327] [Stage-wise Distortion-Perception Traversal in Zero-shot Inverse Problems with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28711)
*Jiawei Zhang,Ziyuan Liu,Leon Yan,Zhenyu Xiao,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MAP-RPS框架，通过阶段化策略（MAP估计+重噪声后验采样）在零样本逆问题中实现失真-感知权衡的灵活遍历，并扩展至潜空间版本LMAP-RPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在贝叶斯逆问题中存在失真-感知权衡现象，实际应用需要在推理时灵活遍历该权衡。尽管扩散模型在零样本逆问题求解中取得成功，但扩散算法中D-P遍历的高效、原理性策略仍未得到充分研究。

Method: 提出阶段化框架MAP-RPS：第一阶段为MAP估计阶段，近似MMSE解并提供低失真初始化；第二阶段为重噪声后验采样阶段，逐步提升感知质量。提供两阶段的理论分析验证设计有效性。进一步扩展至潜空间，利用大规模预训练潜扩散模型骨干，提出LMAP-RPS方法。

Result: MAP-RPS和LMAP-RPS在多种任务上实现了更有效的D-P遍历，同时作为高效求解器在真实世界逆问题中展现出强性能。实验验证了方法的有效性和广泛适用性。

Conclusion: MAP-RPS提供了一种原理性、高效的方法在扩散模型中实现D-P权衡遍历，理论分析支持其设计，实验证明其在多种逆问题任务中的优越性和实用性。

Abstract: The distortion-perception (D-P) tradeoff is a fundamental phenomenon of Bayesian inverse problems, which characterizes the inherent tension between distortion performance and perceptual quality. Enabling flexible traversal of the D-P tradeoff at inference time is crucial for practical applications. Despite the recent success of diffusion models in zero-shot inverse problem solving, efficient and principled strategies for D-P traversal in diffusion-based inverse algorithms remain inadequately characterized. In this paper, we propose a stage-wise framework for realizing D-P traversal using a single diffusion model in zero-shot inverse problems. Our proposed method, termed MAP-RPS, starts with an MAP estimation stage that approximates the MMSE solution and provides a low-distortion initialization, followed by a re-noised posterior sampling stage that progressively improves perceptual quality. We provide theoretical analyses for both stages, establishing the validity and effectiveness of the proposed design. Furthermore, we extend MAP-RPS to the latent space, yielding LMAP-RPS, which enjoys broader applicability by leveraging large-scale pre-trained latent diffusion backbones. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MAP-RPS and LMAP-RPS enable more effective D-P traversal on various tasks, while also exhibiting strong performance as efficient solvers for real-world inverse problems.

</details>


### [328] [BIRDNet: Mining and Encoding Boolean Implication Knowledge Graphs as Interpretable Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28739)
*Tirtharaj Dash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BIRDNet，通过挖掘布尔蕴涵关系构建稀疏神经网络，在生物数据集上以极少参数达到与稠密基线相当的性能，同时保持可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识密集型领域的表格数据中蕴含特征间的布尔蕴涵关系（BIRs），现有神经符号模型依赖外部规则库，本文旨在从数据中挖掘这些关系并构建稀疏、可解释的神经网络。

Method: 用稀疏异常二项检验挖掘布尔蕴涵关系，形成类型化有向图（等价于命题规则库），将其编码为分层神经网络BIRDNet的连接结构，每个隐藏单元对应一条规则且仅绑定两个特征。

Result: 在六个转录组学和蛋白质组学基准上，BIRDNet与最强稠密基线AUROC差距在0.02以内，参数量减少高达96倍；第一层规则恢复多种癌症亚型和组织类型的已知生物标记。

Conclusion: BIRDNet成功实现了结构稀疏性（每层仅2/d权重活跃）和模型可解释性（无需代理模型即可读取规则），在保持性能的同时大幅降低参数量。

Abstract: Tabular data in knowledge-rich domains often carries a latent prior in the form of Boolean implication relationships (BIRs) between pairs of features. We mine such relationships with a sparse-exception binomial test. The mined implications form a typed directed graph, equivalent to a propositional rule base of 2-literal clauses. We encode this graph as the connectivity of a layered neural network, called BIRDNet, in which each hidden unit corresponds to one mined rule and binds only to its two features. We show two consequences of this design: First, the architecture is sparse by construction: at most $2/d$ of the weights in each BIR layer are active, where $d$ is the input dimension. Second, the model is interpretable: every trained unit keeps a stable symbolic identity, so rules can be read off the network without surrogate models. Unlike most neurosymbolic models, BIRDNet does not consume an external rule base; its structural prior is mined from the data. We evaluate BIRDNet on six transcriptomic and proteomic benchmarks. Our results show that BIRDNet stays within 0.02 AUROC of the strongest dense baseline, at a small accuracy cost, while using up to $96\times$ fewer active parameters than an architecture-matched dense MLP. First-layer rules recover known biological signatures across multiple cancer subtypes and tissue types, including canonical amplicons, lineage-defining co-expression modules, and immune-infiltration markers. Data and code are available at: https://github.com/MAHI-Group/BIRDNet.

</details>


### [329] [Extrapolative Weight Averaging Reveals Correctness-Efficiency Frontiers in Code RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28751)
*Kunhao Zheng,Pierre Chambon,Juliette Decugis,Jonas Gehring,Taco Cohen,Benjamin Negrevergne,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过嵌套单元测试覆盖率训练RL检查点，发现并构建了正确性与效率之间的前沿，利用外推权重平均扩展该前沿，在推断时集成互补策略，显著提升了竞争编程任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性插值微调检查点能追踪竞争目标间的帕累托前沿，但外推权重平均是否能在无需额外RL训练的情况下扩展该前沿并生成有用的推断检查点尚不明确。本文针对竞争编程中的正确性与效率权衡问题展开研究。

Method: 从共享初始化出发，在嵌套单元测试覆盖率下训练检查点：低覆盖率奖励仅需通过小规模输入测试，高覆盖率奖励要求通过逐步增大的测试直至完整测试套件。研究插值与外推权重平均在三种推断设置（纯推理、工具使用、智能体编码）和两种模型规模（32B、7B）下的效果。

Result: 揭示了正确性-效率前沿的出现：高覆盖率奖励降低优化失败但增加正确性失败；插值恢复该前沿，外推扩展其至训练端点之外；沿前沿移动改变解题分布，使外推检查点成为互补策略；集成方法在LCB/hard上将pass@250提升3.3%。

Conclusion: 嵌套单元测试覆盖率在代码RL中诱导出正确性-效率前沿，外推权重平均能够有效导航、扩展并利用该前沿，为无需额外训练的推断时策略优化提供了新途径。

Abstract: Linear interpolation between fine-tuned checkpoints has been shown to trace the Pareto front between competing objectives, but whether extrapolative weight averaging can extend such frontiers to new checkpoints useful at inference time, without additional RL training, remains unclear. We study this question in RL for competitive programming, where hidden unit tests under time and memory limits enforce both functional correctness and computational efficiency. Starting from a shared initialization, we train checkpoints under nested unit-test coverage: low-coverage rewards require passing smaller-input tests, while high-coverage rewards require passing progressively larger tests up to the full suite. This sweep reveals the emergence of a correctness-efficiency frontier: on hard problems, higher-coverage reward reduces optimization failures but increases correctness failures, leaving solve rate nearly unchanged. Interpolation between low- and high-coverage checkpoints recovers this frontier, while extrapolation extends it beyond the trained endpoints. Both the frontier and its extrapolative continuation appear across three inference settings, pure reasoning, tool use, and agentic coding, and across two model scales, 32B and 7B. At the problem level, moving along the frontier changes which problems are solved, making extrapolated checkpoints complementary policies in inference-time scaling. Ensembles with extrapolative weight averaging broaden coverage and improve pass@250 on LCB/hard by 3.3% over the best single checkpoint at matched sample budget. These results show that nested unit-test coverage in code RL induces a frontier that extrapolative weight averaging can navigate, extend, and exploit.

</details>


### [330] [LLM Zeroth-Order Fine-Tuning is an Inference Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28760)
*Zelin Li,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将LLM零阶微调从训练循环重构为推理运行时，在OPT-13B上实现8.13倍加速，提出了推理时训练的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零阶微调虽用前向评估替代反向传播，但仍运行于传统训练循环中，导致工作负载与运行时失配：算法需要结构化的推理式评分，而系统暴露的是碎片化的训练步骤序列。

Method: 识别LLM ZO微调为推理主导的工作负载，将重复评分阶段迁移至vLLM服务运行时执行，并将ZO更新表示为动态适配器状态。

Result: 在OPT-13B SST-2上，20k步LoZO运行仅需0.51预估训练小时（基线4.15小时），加速8.13倍，最终评估准确率0.922；OPT-1.3B至OPT-13B核心步骤扩展实验获得2.34x-7.72x加速；MeZO风格实验最高快2.55倍。

Conclusion: 将ZO更新表示为动态适配器状态为推理时训练提供了实用路径，轻量级适配可被调度为推理式工作负载而非独立训练任务。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) fine-tuning is attractive for large language models because it replaces backpropagation with forward objective evaluations. Existing implementations nevertheless execute ZO algorithms inside conventional training loops, even though their dominant work is repeated scoring under nearby parameter states. This creates a workload-runtime mismatch: the algorithm asks for structured inference-style scoring, while the system exposes a sequence of fragmented training-loop steps. We show that LLM ZO fine-tuning is an inference-dominated workload and execute its repeated scoring phase through a serving runtime. On OPT-13B SST-2, the resulting vLLM execution path completes the 20k-step LoZO run in 0.51 estimated training hours versus 4.15 hours for the official LoZO baseline under the matched LoRA-only setting, an 8.13x speedup, while reaching 0.922 final evaluation accuracy and 0.931 final full-validation accuracy. In core-step scaling experiments across OPT-1.3B to OPT-13B, the same runtime reorganization gives 2.34x--7.72x speedups. A MeZO-style high-rank factorized experiment shows that the same runtime paradigm can track a MeZO-like loss trajectory while running up to 2.55x faster. More broadly, representing ZO updates as dynamic adapter states suggests a practical path toward inference-time training, where lightweight adaptation can be scheduled as an inference-like workload rather than as a separate training job.

</details>


### [331] [Principled Algorithms for Optimizing Generalized Metrics in Multi-Label Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28767)
*Mehryar Mohri,Yutao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MMO算法，在EUM框架下优化多标签广义线性分式度量（如F-measure、Jaccard），通过设计新型替代损失函数提供非渐近的H-consistency保证，且在O(l)时间内精确分解，无需近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实多标签分类任务需优化F-measure、Jaccard等总体层面度量，现有EUM框架的理论结果局限于渐近Bayes一致性，缺乏针对有限样本和非渐近保证的原则性学习算法。

Method: 基于H-consistency这一更强概念，设计了新型替代损失函数，证明了其可分解性且能在严格O(l)时间内无近似运行；在此基础上提出MMO算法族用于优化广义线性分式度量。

Result: 提供了针对假设类和有限样本的非渐近H-consistency界限；在MS-COCO、Reuters-21578大规模数据集上，高稀疏深度学习设置下优于现有连续基线，展现了良好的可扩展性和性能。

Conclusion: 本文为一般多标签度量优化提供了理论严谨且实用的解决方案，弥补了EUM框架下非渐近保证的空白。

Abstract: Many real-world classification tasks require predicting multiple labels per instance, necessitating the optimization of complex evaluation metrics such as the $F$-measure and Jaccard index. While the Empirical Utility Maximization (EUM) framework is natural for these population-level metrics, existing theoretical results are largely limited to asymptotic Bayes-consistency. In this paper, we develop principled learning algorithms for optimizing a broad class of generalized metrics within the EUM framework, grounded in the stronger notion of $H$-consistency. Our key contribution is the design of novel surrogate loss functions for multi-label learning that admit provable $H$-consistency bounds, enabling optimization with non-asymptotic guarantees tailored to the hypothesis class and finite samples. Crucially, we prove these combinatorially formulated surrogates decompose exactly, operating in strictly $O(l)$ time without approximations. Building on this foundation, we introduce MMO (Multi-Label Metric Optimization), a new family of algorithms for optimizing generalized linear-fractional metrics. We validate our approach through extensive experiments, demonstrating robust scalability and superior performance over state-of-the-art continuous baselines on large-scale datasets (MS-COCO, Reuters-21578) in high-sparsity, deep learning regimes. Our results offer both theoretical rigor and practical effectiveness for general multi-label metric optimization.

</details>


### [332] [Multi-Mixer Models: Flexible Sequence Modeling with Shared Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28769)
*Kevin Y. Li,Asher Trockman,Ananda Theertha Suresh,Ziteng Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Oryx，一种沿序列轴灵活切换注意力机制与线性循环的新型混合模型，在保持参数共享和计算效率的同时，在语言建模和长文本检索任务上实现了优于单混合器基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax注意力机制在大语言模型中占据核心地位，但其内存随序列长度线性增长、计算量呈二次增长。线性循环模型（如线性注意力和状态空间模型）虽然实现了线性计算复杂度和常量内存，但在需要长上下文检索或上下文学习的任务上仍存在性能差距。现有的混合架构研究主要关注静态地交错或合并注意力与循环块，本文探索了一个新方向：沿token序列轴开发混合模型。

Method: 提出Oryx模型，该模型能够在序列处理过程中灵活切换不同的混合器：利用二次注意力进行丰富的上下文利用，使用线性循环实现高效生成。关键设计是将至少90%的参数在各混合器之间共享，使注意力和循环模式能够在共享的内部表示上操作。研究基于Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet变体进行了验证，模型规模达到14亿参数，采用固定token预算和混合训练策略。

Result: 在固定token预算和混合训练策略下，Oryx的性能与单混合器基线相当或更优。在14亿参数规模下，所有Oryx实例在平均语言建模任务上比各自基线提升至少0.7个百分点。在检索任务中，即使在注意力模式下仅处理极小部分（<10%）的token，Oryx仍能达到与Transformer基线相当的性能。实验验证了注意力和线性循环模型可以共享内部表示。

Conclusion: 研究表明注意力和线性循环模型能够共享内部表示，序列轴混合化是一个有前景的研究方向。Oryx证明了通过在序列处理过程中动态切换混合器，可以在保持计算效率的同时实现与纯注意力模型相当的检索性能，为混合架构设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Softmax attention is the cornerstone of modern large language models, but its memory scales linearly and compute quadratically with sequence length. Linear recurrent models, such as linear attention and state space models, have become widely studied as alternatives to attention due to their linear compute and constant memory. While these sub-quadratic token mixing methods, or mixers, achieve promising efficiency gains and competitive results on a wide range of benchmarks, current linear recurrent models still lag behind on tasks that require long-context retrieval or in-context learning. A growing body of work studies hybrid architectures that attempt to mitigate these trade-offs by statically interleaving or merging attention and recurrent blocks. In this work, we explore a new axis of developing hybrid models: across the token sequence. We propose Oryx, a hybrid model that can, throughout a sequence, flexibly switch between different mixers, for example quadratic attention for rich context utilization and linear recurrences for efficient generation. Oryx ties at least 90% of its parameters across mixers, enabling attention and recurrent modes to operate over shared internal representations. We validate our design with Mamba-2 and Gated DeltaNet variants, up to 1.4B models. Under fixed token budgets and a mixed-training strategy, Oryx achieves comparable or better performance than its single-mixer baselines. At the 1.4B scale, all instances of Oryx outperform their respective baselines by at least 0.7 percentage points on averaged language modeling tasks. On retrieval tasks, Oryx achieves performance comparable to the Transformer baseline even when processing only a tiny fraction (<10%) of the tokens in attention mode. These results suggest that attention and linear recurrent models can share internal representations, and motivate sequence-axis hybridization as a promising direction.

</details>


### [333] [Learn from Weaknesses: Automated Domain Specialization for Small Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28775)
*Suji Kim,Kangsan Kim,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了LearnWeak框架，通过利用更强的参考代理识别小型计算机使用代理在目标领域的弱点，自动合成针对性任务并构建监督，在OSWorld上相比EvoCUA-8B和OpenCUA-7B分别平均提升11.6和11.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型开放计算机使用代理虽然更实用，但能力较弱且表现出不均匀的领域特定失败；为每个软件领域部署独立的大型专家代理成本高昂；而直接合成大规模训练数据的朴素方法只能带来边际改进。

Method: 引入LearnWeak框架，使用更强的参考代理识别学生在目标领域的弱点、合成针对性任务并自动构建监督；提出错误感知特化目标，解耦规划和执行错误，实现比广泛均匀监督更精确的行为更新。

Result: 在OSWorld的八个领域中，LearnWeak相比EvoCUA-8B平均提升11.6个百分点，相比OpenCUA-7B平均提升11.1个百分点；学生感知的数据集生成和训练方法优于现有的自主轨迹生成和训练基线。

Conclusion: 强调了在数据合成和代理训练中学生感知的重要性，为在多样领域中特化小型计算机使用代理指明了一条更有原则且高效的路径。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) have recently made substantial progress, but deploying a separate large expert for each software domain remains expensive. Small open computer-use agents are more practical specialization targets, but they remain substantially weaker and exhibit uneven domain-specific failures. A straightforward remedy is to synthesize large-scale training data for the target domain, yet we find that this naive approach yields only marginal improvements. Building on this observation, we introduce LearnWeak, an annotation-free specialization framework for small computer-use agents that uses a stronger reference agent to identify the student's weaknesses in the target domain, synthesize targeted tasks, and construct supervision automatically. LearnWeak further introduces an error-aware specialization objective that disentangles planning and execution errors, enabling more behaviorally precise updates than broad uniform supervision. On OSWorld, LearnWeak achieves average gains of 11.6 and 11.1 percentage points over EvoCUA-8B and OpenCUA-7B, respectively, across eight domains. We also validate that our student-aware dataset generation and training approaches outperform existing autonomous trajectory generation and training baselines. Our work highlights the importance of student awareness in both data synthesis and agent training, pointing toward a more principled and efficient path for specializing small computer-use agents in diverse domains.

</details>


### [334] [Affective Music Recommendation: A Rollout-Based World Model for Offline Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28810)
*Audrey Chan,Aaron Labbé,Jacob Lavoie,Jordan Bannister,Arsène Fansi Tchango,Guillaume Lajoie,Laurent Charlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AMRS（情感音乐推荐系统），针对在线情感实验在伦理上受限的问题，采用基于rollout的世界模型进行离线策略训练和压力测试，在冷启动协议下实现了对情感信号的有效预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能性音乐应用面临独特的推荐问题：成功取决于听众的情感状态，但在线情感实验在伦理上受限，特别是对于无法可靠跳过歌曲或报告痛苦的临床人群（如患有神经认知疾病的老年人）。这导致了在线实验难以进行，需要开发能够在离线环境下有效训练和验证的情感推荐方法。

Method: AMRS系统基于rollout的世界模型架构：使用因果Transformer在日志监听数据上训练，联合预测参与度、二元评分以及自我报告的效价和唤醒度。该世界模型既作为离线策略训练的计算机模拟器，也作为部署前的压力测试工具。推荐策略通过行为克隆初始化，然后使用直接偏好优化（DPO）针对可配置的多目标效用函数进行离线微调。

Result: 在严格的冷启动协议下，世界模型以可用的保真度预测行为和情感信号；DPO在保持相似多样性配置的同时，提高了相比克隆基线的预测效价和唤醒度，并避免了贪婪优化产生的分布崩溃问题。

Conclusion: 这项工作为在在线实验伦理上不可行时的情感推荐方法论提供了早期部署验证，展示了通过世界模型和离线优化技术可以在不进行在线实验的情况下开发有效的情感推荐系统。

Abstract: Functional music applications, from consumer focus and sleep aids to clinical interventions, share a distinctive recommendation problem: success is defined by the listener's affective state, but online experimentation on emotion is ethically constrained, particularly for clinical populations who cannot reliably skip a song or report distress. We describe AMRS, the Affective Music Recommendation System deployed on LUCID's health-and-wellness platforms, which serve clinical users (primarily older adults with neurocognitive conditions) and consumer-wellness users across energize, focus, calm, and sleep modes. AMRS is built around a rollout-based world model: a causal transformer trained on logged listening data to jointly predict engagement, binary rating, and self-reported valence and arousal. The world model serves both as an in-silico simulator for offline policy training and as a stress-testing tool before deployment. A recommender policy initialized by behaviour cloning is fine-tuned offline with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) against a configurable multi-objective utility function. Under a strict cold-start protocol, the world model predicts both behavioural and affective signals with usable fidelity; DPO improves predicted valence and arousal over the cloned baseline while maintaining a similar diversity profile and avoiding the distributional collapse produced by greedy optimization. We position the work as an early deployed validation of a methodology for affective recommendation when online experimentation is ethically untenable.

</details>


### [335] [PEFT-Arena: Understanding Parameter-Efficient Finetuning from a Stability-Plasticity Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28819)
*Yangyi Huang,Ruotian Peng,Zeju Qiu,Jiale Kang,Yandong Wen,Bernhard Schölkopf,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PEFT-Arena基准，从稳定性-可塑性权衡角度评估参数高效微调方法，发现正交微调在可比参数预算下达到最佳帕累托前沿，并通过几何分析揭示遗忘与非等距表示扭曲的关联，提出路径回溯的后验改进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调（PEFT）方法的评估主要关注下游任务准确性，而忽视了对预训练能力的保留。作者认为PEFT应通过稳定性-可塑性困境来评估，即在目标任务适应性与抗遗忘能力之间的权衡。

Method: 1. 提出PEFT-Arena基准，联合测量下游性能和通用能力保留；2. 从权重空间进行谱分析，研究参数化如何与预训练奇异值结构相互作用；3. 在激活空间中设计保留度量指标，评估微调是否保留或扭曲通用能力表示；4. 提出路径回溯的后验改进方法。

Result: 1. 不同PEFT方法呈现独特的稳定性-可塑性特征；2. 在可比参数预算下，正交微调达到最有利的帕累托前沿；3. 遗忘与非等距表示扭曲相关；4. 最终SFT检查点常常错过更好的目标-保留操作点。

Conclusion: PEFT方法应从稳定性-可塑性权衡角度评估，正交微调在该框架下表现最优。通过几何分析揭示了性能差异的机制，并证明了路径回溯可以有效改善性能-保留的平衡。研究为PEFT方法的选择和改进提供了新的理论视角和实践指导。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient finetuning (PEFT) has become the standard approach for adapting large language models, yet evaluations largely emphasize downstream accuracy while overlooking the retention of pretrained capabilities. We argue that PEFT should be assessed through the stability-plasticity dilemma: the trade-off between target-task adaptation and resistance to forgetting. We introduce PEFT-Arena, a benchmark that jointly measures downstream performance and general capability retention. Across methods, we find distinct stability-plasticity profiles; under comparable parameter budgets, orthogonal finetuning achieves the most favorable Pareto frontier. To explain these differences, we analyze PEFT updates from two geometric perspectives. In weight space, spectral analysis reveals how parameterizations interact with the pretrained singular-value structure. In activation space, retention metrics show whether finetuning preserves or distorts general-capability representations, with forgetting linked to non-isometric representation distortion. Finally, an analysis shows that final SFT checkpoints often overshoot a better target-retention operating point. Inspired by this, we present case studies of a post-hoc improvement with path-wise rewinding.

</details>


### [336] [LNN-PINN: A Unified Physics-Only Training Framework with Liquid Residual Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08935)
*Ze Tao,Hanxuan Wang,Fujun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LNN-PINN框架，通过引入液体残差门控架构提升物理信息神经网络在复杂问题上的预测精度，在四个基准问题中一致降低了RMSE和MAE，同时保持原始物理建模流程不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在复杂问题中存在预测精度有限的局限性，需要提升其在复杂科学与工程问题中的准确性。

Method: 提出LNN-PINN框架，仅在网络隐藏层映射中引入轻量级液体残差门控机制，保持采样策略、损失函数构成和超参数设置不变，确保改进纯粹源于架构优化。

Result: 在四个基准问题中，相同训练条件下LNN-PINN一致降低了RMSE和MAE，绝对误差图进一步验证了精度提升；框架在不同维度、边界条件和算子特性下表现出强适应性和稳定性。

Conclusion: LNN-PINN为提升物理信息神经网络在复杂科学工程问题中的预测精度提供了一种简洁有效的架构增强方案。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have attracted considerable attention for their ability to integrate partial differential equation priors into deep learning frameworks; however, they often exhibit limited predictive accuracy when applied to complex problems. To address this issue, we propose LNN-PINN, a physics-informed neural network framework that incorporates a liquid residual gating architecture while preserving the original physics modeling and optimization pipeline to improve predictive accuracy. The method introduces a lightweight gating mechanism solely within the hidden-layer mapping, keeping the sampling strategy, loss composition, and hyperparameter settings unchanged to ensure that improvements arise purely from architectural refinement. Across four benchmark problems, LNN-PINN consistently reduced RMSE and MAE under identical training conditions, with absolute error plots further confirming its accuracy gains. Moreover, the framework demonstrates strong adaptability and stability across varying dimensions, boundary conditions, and operator characteristics. In summary, LNN-PINN offers a concise and effective architectural enhancement for improving the predictive accuracy of physics-informed neural networks in complex scientific and engineering problems.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [337] [Characterizing the Configuration of Starlink Queuing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27717)
*Johan Garcia,Simon Sundberg,Anna Brunstrom*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过高精度流量生成方法测量Starlink的单向延迟，发现其使用drop-front缓冲管理而非每流公平队列或drop-tail缓冲，这可能干扰基于丢包的拥塞控制机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Starlink接入网络具有动态容量特性，队列管理对保证用户通信性能和资源利用率至关重要，但其系统设计细节和内部队列配置为私有信息，缺乏公开研究。

Method: 开发高精度、突发模式控制的流量生成方法，精确测量Starlink单向延迟；结合延迟和丢包分析与队列模拟器进行对比研究。

Result: Starlink未采用每流公平队列或drop-tail缓冲，而是使用drop-front缓冲管理；drop-front虽能降低延迟，但可能干扰基于丢包的拥塞控制假设，导致吞吐量下降。

Conclusion: Starlink的队列管理策略（drop-front）可能影响传统拥塞控制算法性能，需针对其特性设计更合适的传输协议。

Abstract: In all networking systems, queuing is important to ensure appropriate resource utilization in the presence of bursty traffic and varying traffic demands. The Starlink access network is additionally also dynamic in terms of the capacity it can provide, and thus queuing plays an even greater role to ensure appropriate communication performance for the end-users while maintaining high resource utilization. However, for Starlink most system design details, along with the setup of the internal queuing, is private information and not publicly available. To address this we have developed a high-precision, burst-pattern controlled, traffic generation approach allowing us to precisely measure the one-way delay for Starlink. By analyzing the delay and loss in conjunction with a queue simulator we find that Starlink does not employ per-flow fair queuing or drop-tail buffers, but it does use drop-front buffer management. While drop-front reduces delay, it may also interfere with the assumptions made by loss-based congestion controls, potentially contributing to throughput degradation.

</details>


### [338] [A Vertical Look at UAV Connectivity in the Wild: Cellular vs. Starlink, 3D Characterization, and Performance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27755)
*Sravan Reddy Chintareddy,Sherwan Jalal Abdullah,Justin D. Clough,Victor S. Frost,Shawn Keshmiri,Morteza Hashemi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过真实飞行测试对比分析商用蜂窝网络与Starlink卫星网络在低空无人机场景下的性能表现，发现LEO卫星链路在延迟和下行容量方面显著优于蜂窝网络，同时揭示了高度对蜂窝网络性能的双刃剑影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无人机在偏远地区的应用需求增长，缺乏对商用蜂窝网络与新兴LEO卫星网络在低空UAV场景下的系统性性能对比研究，尤其是缺乏开放的真实飞行测试数据集和多层测量分析。

Method: 采用多层测量方法，包括物理层信号指标、多小区网络拓扑和端到端应用性能，通过10余次飞行测试、累计4.5+小时飞行时间，收集超过18K个样本，构建首个同时同地测量的蜂窝-星链双网络对比开放数据集。

Result: Starlink性能显著优于蜂窝网络：95% RTT低于50ms（蜂窝80%低于150ms），95%下行容量超过25Mbps（蜂窝仅5Mbps）；蜂窝网络中高度提升（330+m）虽通过LOS传播改善信号功率15-20dB，但导致切换率增加3-4倍；切换对RTT影响不对称，53.5%的切换改善RTT，但最坏退化（275ms）是最优改善（137ms）的2倍。

Conclusion: 研究提供了首个开放的低空UAV双网络对比数据集，证明LEO卫星网络在偏远地区UAV应用中具有明显优势，同时揭示了蜂窝网络高度相关的性能权衡，为未来UAV通信系统设计提供了重要参考依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we present an open-source measurement platform designed to characterize the performance of commercial cellular (Verizon, a major US provider) and LEO satellite (Starlink) networks through real-world flight tests in rural environments. We implement a comprehensive multi-layer measurement approach spanning physical layer signal metrics, multi-cell network topology, and end-to-end (E2E) application performance. Through an extensive flight campaign with more than $10$ flight tests, $4.5$+ hours of flight time resulting in more than $18$K samples, we present the first detailed, open-source dataset analyzing dual cellular and Starlink performance for low-altitude UAV operations. Our cellular-Starlink comparative results, which are collected \emph{simultaneously at the same time and location}, demonstrate significant performance differences between the two technologies: the LEO satellite link achieves superior latency performance with $95\%$ of Round-Trip Time (RTT) measurements below $50$ ms compared to $80\%$ under $150$ ms for cellular, and exceptional downlink capacity with $95\%$ exceeding $25$ Mbps versus only $5$ Mbps for cellular. Our analysis on cellular network performance demonstrates that while higher altitudes (e.g., $330+$ m above the sea level) improve signal power by $15-20$ dB via line-of-sight (LOS) propagation, it causes a $3-4$ $\times$ increase in handover rates, which is due to excessive multi-cell visibility rather than signal degradation. Furthermore, we observe asymmetric impacts on the RTT performance due to handovers such that $53.5$\% of handovers improve RTT, but worst-case degradation ($275$ ms) is $2$ $\times$ larger than best-case improvement ($137$ ms).

</details>


### [339] [A Preliminary Assessment of Midhaul Links at 140 GHz using Ray-Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27771)
*Sravan Reddy Chintareddy,Marco Mezzavilla,Sundeep Rangan,Morteza Hashemi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文评估了在城市环境中使用140 GHz太赫兹无线链路作为 disaggregated 5G 网络架构中CU与DU之间中传链路的可行性，结果表明采用3GPP option 2高层分割方案具有良好应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着移动数据需求的不断增长，传输网络架构必须能够承受5G及未来网络中多Gbps的流量要求。为了满足这种前所未有的需求，研究人员正在探索将太赫兹通信技术融入未来的移动网络中。

Method: 研究在城市环境中评估太赫兹无线中传链路在 disaggregated 5G 网络架构（具有功能分割）中连接中央单元（CU）和分布式单元（DU）的可行性。通过最小化服务所有DU所需的CU数量，定义了多种CU和DU节点选择策略，以确定CU与DU之间每条链路可支持的峰值数据速率。基于射线追踪技术进行数值分析。

Result: 基于射线追踪的数值结果表明，140 GHz无线链路采用3GPP option 2作为高层分割（HLS）方案，是中传传输网络中一项具有前景的技术。

Conclusion: 140 GHz太赫兹无线链路在采用适当的网络架构和功能分割方案时，能够有效支持5G及未来网络的中传传输需求。

Abstract: The ever-growing demand for mobile data necessitates a transport network architecture that can withstand the 5G-and-beyond multi-Gbps traffic requirements. To cater for such unprecedented demand, studies are being conducted to incorporate TeraHertz (THz) communications in future mobile networks. In this paper, we consider an urban environment and evaluate the feasibility of THz wireless midhaul links for the transport networks between the Central Units (CU) and Distributed Units (DU) in a disaggregated 5G network architecture with functional splits. Our goal is to study the feasibility of midhaul links at 140 GHz by minimizing the number of required CUs to serve all the DUs. To this end, we define several policies for selecting CU and DU nodes in order to determine the peak data rate that can be supported over each link between a CU and DU. Our numerical results based on ray-tracing suggest that wireless links at 140 GHz with 3GPP option 2 as High Layer Split (HLS) represents a promising technology for midhaul transport networks.

</details>


### [340] [Throughput-Optimized Networks at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27963)
*Conor James Green,Mithuna Thottethodi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TONS框架，通过线性优化方法自动合成数据中心网络拓扑，在谷歌TPU v4/5p超级计算机上实现了相比现有环面拓扑2.1倍和1.6倍的吞吐量加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心网络设计对AI训练至关重要，但现有的TPU网络在支持数千个加速器规模化扩展时仍存在巨大的吞吐量损失。如何设计接近最优的、满足物理和实现约束的吞吐量导向网络拓扑、路由和集合通信仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 提出TONS（Throughput Optimized Networks at Scale）自动化网络综合框架：1）将拓扑综合建模为线性优化问题，最大化以吞吐量为中心的代理指标；2）结合理论和启发式方法扩展到数千节点规模；3）引入支持有限虚拟通道和光交换机故障的无死锁路由方案。

Result: 在谷歌TPU v4/5p超级计算机上的评估表明：1）TONS网络在均匀随机流量下获得2.1倍的几何平均加速比；2）在全对全流量下获得1.6倍的几何平均加速比；3）相比最佳TPU v4/5p环面变体显著提升了吞吐量性能。

Conclusion: TONS成功填补了现有TPU网络中每秒太字节级别的吞吐量空白，证明了自动化网络综合方法在大型并行AI训练系统中的有效性，为设计高性能数据中心网络提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Datacenter network design plays a critical role in AI training by supporting scaling to thousands of accelerators. An open problem, designing a near-optimal throughput oriented network-topology, routing, and collectives-has not been achieved at scale and with broad applicability to physical/implementation constraints. We address this problem with a compelling use-case, Google's TPU v4/5p supercomputer where the topology may be reconfigured to achieve higher all-to-all throughput, supporting large, parallelized AI training. We show that the existing TPU networks leave terabytes per second of throughput on the table and we fill that gap. This paper presents Throughput Optimized Networks at Scale (TONS), an automated network synthesis framework that meets the high-throughput demands of modern computing. TONS formulates topology synthesis as a linear optimization problem that maximizes a throughput-centric proxy metric, using theory and heuristics to scale to thousands of nodes. We further introduce a deadlock-free routing scheme compatible with limited virtual channels and optical switch faults, enabling the synthesized topologies to realize their predicted throughput gains in simulation. Evaluating uniform random and all-to-all traffic, TONS networks have a geometric mean speedups of 2.1x and 1.6x, respectively, over the best TPU v4/5p torus variants.

</details>


### [341] [Kernel-Level Per-Slice UPF Latency Measurement in Containerised 5G Core Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28185)
*Akhil Dev Mishra,Mayank Pandey*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过容器化open5GS部署，首次对5G核心网用户平面功能（UPF）在多切片并发工作负载下的性能进行了实证测量研究，发现eMBB转发延迟对负载敏感，URLLC延迟与负载无关（验证了进程隔离），且N4 PFCP会话修改延迟始终低于200微秒，为AI驱动的UPF编排留有充足时间预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G核心网用户平面功能（UPF）负责每个用户数据会话的分组转发、GTP-U解封装和服务质量执行，但UPF在多切片并发工作负载下的行为在公开文献中尚未得到实证表征。具体而言，其转发延迟如何响应负载、切片间的隔离效果如何、以及为智能控制预留的时序预算是多少，这些问题均未解决。

Method: 在容器化open5GS部署环境中进行测量研究，设计并实现了一个命名空间感知的TC-BPF仪表化框架，解决了现有工具无法将延迟观测归因于单个容器化网络功能的问题。部署eMBB、URLLC和mMTC三种切片，在轻、中、重三种负载条件下运行真实应用流量，收集约2800万个匹配的N3到N6转发延迟对。

Result: eMBB转发延迟对负载敏感，第99百分位延迟从574微秒增长到1243微秒；URLLC延迟对负载不敏感，验证了UPF进程隔离效果；mMTC表现出宽尾TCP行为；N4 PFCP会话修改延迟在所有数据平面负载条件下始终低于200微秒。

Conclusion: 测量结果揭示了UPF在不同网络切片类型下的性能特征，验证了进程隔离的有效性，并为AI驱动的UPF编排设计提供了关键数据支撑——在假设的2毫秒预算内有充足的时间余量。研究将仪表化框架、实验脚本和数据集模式在GitHub上开源发布。

Abstract: The 5G Core User Plane Function is responsible for packet forwarding, GTP-U decapsulation, and quality of service enforcement for every user data session. How the UPF behaves under simultaneous multi-slice workloads remains empirically uncharacterised in the open literature. Specifically, how its forwarding latency responds to load, how well it isolates one slice from another, and what timing budgets remain available for intelligent control are all open questions. This paper presents a measurement study conducted on a containerised open5GS deployment with three concurrent network slices. We design and implement a namespace-aware TC-BPF instrumentation framework that resolves the fundamental obstacle preventing existing tools from attributing latency observations to individual containerised network functions. We deploy eMBB, URLLC, and mMTC slices with realistic application traffic under light, medium, and heavy load conditions and collect approximately 28 million matched N3 to N6 forwarding delay pairs. The gathered results reveal that eMBB forwarding delay is load-sensitive with the 99th percentile growing from 574 to 1,243 microseconds across load conditions. URLLC delay is load-insensitive, confirming per-UPF process isolation. mMTC exhibits wide-tail TCP behaviour. On this platform, N4 PFCP session modification latency remains consistently below 200 microseconds regardless of data-plane load, suggesting substantial timing headroom within the two-millisecond budget assumed by AI-driven UPF orchestration designs. The instrumentation framework, experiment scripts, and dataset schema are released at https://github.com/MP-Akhil-5G/open5gs-slice-measurement.

</details>


### [342] [Automated Heuristic Design for Network Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28197)
*Reza Namvar,José Gallego,Jose A. Ayala-Romero,Livia Elena Chatzieleftheriou,Andres Garcia-Saavedra,Albert Banchs,Marco Fiore*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文探索将自动启发式设计（AHD）框架应用于网络环境，并以5G低密度奇偶校验（LDPC）解码为案例进行实践验证，结果表明AHD生成的启发式算法可达到生产系统中现有最先进方案的性能水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络运营在协议栈各层依赖人工设计的启发式算法，这些方法需要大量专家知识和人力投入。近年来人工智能驱动的自动启发式设计在经典问题上展现出超越传统启发式算法的潜力，这启发了研究者探索将其应用于网络场景的可能性。

Method: 研究采用两个层面：一是讨论AHD与网络运营的一般性集成方案及相关挑战；二是针对特定网络任务——5G解码，提出AHD的实践实现方案，具体应用于低密度奇偶校验（LDPC）解码算法的设计。

Result: 实验结果表明，现代AHD工具能够设计出用于LDPC解码的启发式算法，其性能可媲美生产系统中已部署的最先进解决方案。

Conclusion: 自动启发式设计框架在网络环境中具有实际应用价值，能够为网络运营任务自动生成高质量启发式算法，为网络系统的自动化设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Network operation relies on heuristics to solve many tasks rapidly and efficiently across the protocol stack. These heuristics are the result of thorough human-driven design rooted in expert knowledge of the target system and problem. Recently, approaches powered by Artificial Intelligence have shown promising results in devising solutions that outperform long-established heuristics in classical problems. We explore the possibility of applying such Automated Heuristic Design (AHD) frameworks to network environments by (i) discussing the general integration of AHD with network operation and the associated challenges, as well as (ii) proposing a practical implementation of AHD for a specific networking task, i.e., 5G decoding. Initial results show how modern AHD tools can devise heuristics for Low-Density Parity Check decoding on par with state-of-the-art solutions implemented in production systems.

</details>


### [343] [A Goal-Oriented Networking Approach for Intelligent IoT Service Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28502)
*Federico Tonini,Davide Borsatti,Wint Yi Poe,Riccardo Trivisonno,Walter Cerroni*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对面向目标（Goal-Oriented）通信范式提出了一个端到端评估框架，通过多目标优化模型分析能耗、时延和目标准确性之间的权衡，证明了该范式在6G网络架构中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着6G标准化工作即将启动，网络面临沉浸式、大规模和超可靠低时延等新场景需求，需同时提升吞吐量、时延、覆盖和能效。面向目标通信作为一种新兴范式，通过只传输与任务相关的数据来节省资源，但其对网络本身的影响尚未被探索。

Method: 提出了一个实用的端到端评估框架，用于评估能耗、时延和目标准确性等关键绩效指标（KPI）；构建了多目标优化模型，分析面向目标网络中多个KPI之间的权衡关系。

Result: 通过仿真实验证明，网络可以从面向目标范式的应用中获益，该范式能够在保证任务目标准确性的前提下，有效降低能耗和时延，提升网络整体效率。

Conclusion: 面向目标通信范式在未来6G网络架构中具有重要应用潜力，研究结果表明该方法能够实现网络资源的优化利用，为6G网络设计提供了新的思路和方向。

Abstract: The first 6G standardization efforts are about to start, shaping the new generation of mobile networks. The IMT-2030 extends the IMT-2020 by expanding its usage scenarios to Immersive, Massive, and Hyper-Reliable and Low-Latency Communications. It also introduces novel scenarios by integrating Artificial Intelligence and Sensing with Communication and supporting Ubiquitous Connectivity. Compared to the previous generation, 6G is expected to improve not only throughput and latency, but also coverage and energy efficiency. A paradigm called Goal-Oriented (GO) communications has recently emerged as a promising solution to improve network efficiency. It relies on the fact that the goal of the communication network is to achieve a specific task with a defined accuracy, rather than creating perfect data delivery. Intelligent devices can pre-process data to send only what is relevant to achieve the task, thus saving precious network resources and energy. Recent works demonstrate that incorporating service- and application-level KPIs in the network allows to achieve higher communication efficiency for devices, but the consequence of using such techniques on the network itself has not yet been explored. This paper proposes a practical end-to-end framework to assess energy consumption, latency, and goal accuracy KPIs, which includes a Multi-Objective optimization model to evaluate the trade-offs between the multiple KPIs relevant to GO networking. We demonstrate, through simulation, that the network can benefit from the application of the GO paradigm, indicating its potential in future network architectures.

</details>
